- **The seven on Home and the one on Quotes.** The heading says "1 of 7 jobs" with a link to the Board's fold; enquiries that never had a quote are not listed under Quotes (ADR 0088).
- **Timesheet work.** The Work done merge goes with the link's restructure now, because with timesheets off it costs nothing; "Approve €290" waits for the switch.
- **The record picker.** The half step only (Offer to…); the client and venue sites after a month of use.
- **The pick list's Next.** Built, after the chip, with a line in ADR 0104; the research already names the pick list as the dock's second place, so this informs rather than asks.
- **Planner cells.** Keep "N/M" with a title; neither the chip nor "N of M" fits a month cell.

### Questions for Colly

Each has a recommendation; the programme is built on the recommendation where a move needs one, and each is one PR to reverse.

1. **"Booked" or "Confirmed" for a crew booking** that the office and the freelancer both read (freelancer-5)? *Booked*, on both sides in one PR; "Confirm" stays the office's act and "Confirmed" the client's yes.
2. **Should staff answer offers and send timesheets in the app**, from Home › Me and the "You on this job" card (ADR 0034 hid the buttons; ADR 0046 made the revisit due)? *Yes, as its own ADR after batch 3*, reusing the link's handlers with an own-offer check; until then the app points at the link, which stays the one place to answer.
3. **Two in-app notices** from history rows that exist, "Confirmed: <job>" to the heads of the departments on it and the warehouse's heads, and "Gone out: <job>" to the office, widening ADR 0058's "yours" to "your department's stage"? *A narrow yes*: in-app only, once per transition per job, after the owner by role; one switch to drop if the bell gets noisy.
4. **Stock's first screen** for anyone with the stock ability: Catalogue first with the strip as the jump (ADR 0033), or the day's cards above the catalogue? *The strip first*; revisit after two load-out weeks, and if yes it is one reorder for the stock ability only with a line in ADR 0033.
5. **Home's tiles ordered, and Housekeeping folded, by the viewer's role** inside the one Company view (the first by-role difference since ADR 0033 rejected a role-shaped Home when there were no roles)? *Yes*: the same tiles, the viewer's order, one fold, said in the Menu's line; no fifth view.
6. **Is the invoice part of the job's story** (a money-only quiet row after the tasks and a "To invoice" group on Quotes) or accounts' alone? *The row and the group*, never a ninth task or a sixth stage, with a one-off cut-off for jobs that ended before the row ships.
7. **The card gap inside a record page**, 16px (calm, as ADR 0082 reads) or the list's 24px? *16*, written into `app.css` beside the six space roles.
8. **For a role with nothing to press**, fold Where it is and omit the rest-state dock (ADR 0104 and 0109 for one role)? *No*; the "You on this job" card does the work and one job page for everyone is worth the 92px.
9. **A named "At risk" state** with its own Home tile and Filter value (director-2), or the chip's warn tone on a crew gap and the Board head's true count? *The tone and the count first*; the named state only if directors still ask "which ones".
10. **How long a change stays marked** on the freelancer's page? *Seven days or until the call, whichever is sooner*, as one constant.
11. **When do timesheets turn on** (ADR 0090)? The fortnight before is its own small phase for "Approve €290" and "sent N weeks ago"; the link is ready after batch 2.
12. **Who owns the Purchase list**: the office in the Menu or the warehouse on the link, and does "KIT TO BUY" go? *The kicker goes in batch 1; one owner is the office*, with the link's "Tell the warehouse ›" kept as the crew's way to ask.
13. **Should the verdict line draw when the month is fine**, or only name what is not? *Draw it quietly*: it is the director's one-line answer, in the hint style with no wash, and a warn notice only when a fortnight tile is bad.

### What stays open after the programme

The critic walked Colly's thirteen steps against the merged moves (section 5 has the table). After batch 5 the job's journey is one story on every surface the app already has, and five steps are as unrecorded as they are today, because none became a theme: an enquiry with dates this week never appears on Home's Today or the week ahead; logistics planning (vans, drivers, transport) has no task and no chip fact; preshow production (call sheet sent, show pack ready) is not recorded; nothing on the job page says which phase is on today for the office and heads; and deprep has no record beyond the tick, so "kit back with a fault open" still tells nobody. Marking a quote sent has no Undo, and a quote sent outside the app relies on the hand tick. These are the questions for the next job-flow phase, not this programme:

- Should vans, drivers and transport be a task or a chip fact in Going out and Back, or stay in Stock › Vehicles only?
- Should "call sheet sent" and "show pack ready" be recorded, and by whom?
- Should an enquiry with dates this week appear on Home's Today and The week ahead?
- Should the job page say which phase is on today for the office and heads, not only for staff on the job?
- Should deprep have a record (what came back, in what condition) beyond the tick, and should the office be told when kit is back with a fault open?
- Should marking a quote sent have an Undo, and should a quote sent outside the app be recorded as sent?

Two smaller known gaps are left as they are, by the sceptics' verdicts: the Stock area row's sideways scroll on a phone (nine views in 356px), and the desk's touch density (recorded in the system's adoption notes as sess-sys's decision).

## 10. Everything found

This is the full record behind sections 4 to 9: every theme and smaller finding that survived its sceptic, by lens, so that a move can be read against its evidence and a dropped item against its reason. Each theme carries its priority (P1 before the next busy week, P2 worth doing, P3 polish) and its severity on the NN/g 0 to 4 scale as the sceptic left them, the verdict word (kept, adjusted, added by the sceptic, or not re-walked), the roles and viewports it was seen on, the evidence with file and line, the heuristic or research behind it, the proposal with its size, trade-off and what was weighed against it, the screenshots, the learning for the shared system, and the sceptic's reason where one was given. The smaller findings are one line each. After each lens comes what that lens found already cohesive, which the moves leave alone, and what its sceptic dropped.

In numbers: forty-six themes (5 P1, 29 P2, 12 P3) and twenty-nine smaller findings kept; nine findings dropped. The five P1 themes are the kit-still-out count (sceptic-jobflow-1), the freelancer's forms (forms-5), the head's lens on Stock (head-1), the staff member's two homes (staff-1) and the changed call time on the link (freelancer-2), plus one P1 finding, Money's tables on a phone (director-f1); all six land in batch 1. The move that absorbs each theme is named in section 4; the sceptics' verdicts came from re-walking the bench, not from re-reading the reviewer.

Theme ids are the lens name and a number (`jobflow-1`); smaller findings carry an `f` (`jobflow-f1`); a sceptic's own addition carries the sceptic's name or an `x` (`sceptic-jobflow-1`, `head-x1`). Screenshot paths are under the project folder's `screens/ux-sweep/`.

### The job's journey

*How a job's position and movement read across every surface.*

On the job page itself the story is now strong: five stages worked out from the records, one Next button, a task list, and the ADR 0109 truth-telling held up on every bench job I opened (enquiry, quoted, lapsed, Planning, Back two days after, Back six days after with the quote invoiced, through after three weeks, lost, cancelled, a hire, a site visit). Away from the job page it frays in three ways. First, the same job's position is drawn in seven shapes with different words depending on which screen you are on, and the status pill keeps saying 'Confirmed' from the client's yes until the job is archived, so for four of the five stages the most prominent word on the head is the least informative one. Second, a job has no visible end: Back ends at a deprep tick while the thing the office is actually waiting on (invoice, payment) lives only on the quote page and Money, so a job reads 'Through every stage · all 5 tasks done' with the client unbilled, and an invoiced job can still read 'Scan the kit back'. The day after its dates a job also drops off Home and into Jobs › Past while its stage is Back and its Next is live. Third, the hand-offs between roles are pulls, not pushes: the office's Notifications page says 'Nothing here yet' on a company with 300 jobs, the heads and the warehouse learn a job is theirs by looking at tiles, and the one tile that was meant to show what is stuck ('74 jobs in Planning with the next task down to nobody', 74 of 132 coming up) is a constant, not a signal. The Board, meant as the stage view, is 45 Planning cards to one On the job, because Planning is where a confirmed job lives for months; the only sense of time anywhere is the Board's 'N days left / N days after'.

#### sceptic-jobflow-1 · Home counts 3 jobs with kit still out while the Board's Back holds 12, because Home ignores the kit that was never scanned out

**P1 · severity 3 · Added by the sceptic** · roles W, H, O · phone 390 dark, desk 1440 light · reproduced

ADR 0109 made 'N never scanned out: check they're back' the warehouse's open task on every job whose kit went out unscanned, and the Board's Back column and the Jobs list's Stage filter count those jobs (12). Home's Company tile 'jobs with kit still out after them' and the Warehouse view's 'Still out after the job' list count only kit scanned out and not back (3: Autumn Gala, Vartry, Liffey), so the nine jobs whose kit is to be checked back in, including Fergus Festivals Regatta with 23 and Inny Conferences Festival with 56 never scanned, reach no surface the warehouse opens on a phone. The one role whose task it is meets it only by opening the Board and scrolling past 31 Planning cards, and the office reads two different answers to 'what is still out after the job' on two screens.

- **Evidence:** # (Home, office, desk light and phone dark) and # as the warehouse, Warehouse view: Tile '3 jobs with kit still out after them'; Warehouse view 'Still out after the job (3)': Autumn Gala 2 still out, Vartry Theatre Company Open Day 1 still out, Liffey Conferences Hackathon 1 still out. (`web/src/home/standing.ts:140 (stillOut = view.moves.stillOut.length); shared/src/sync/pick-view.ts:413-417 (lists with p.stillOut > 0 only); web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:576-589`) [3 on Home against 12 on the Board and 12 in Jobs › Past › Filter › Stage › Back, read from the innerText dumps of each screen] · #jobs/board (office, desk light), Back column: 'Back 12': Liffey '1 still out, 22 days after', Fergus Festivals Regatta '23 never scanned out: check they're back, 10 days after', Inny Conferences Festival '56 never scanned out…, 11 days after', Slaney '14 never scanned out…, 6 days after', Owenmore '14 never scanned out…, 2 days after', all in the bad tone. (`shared/src/sync/steps-view.ts:238-240 (the never-scanned hint keeps Back in open); web/src/jobs/BoardScreen.tsx:34-38 (column = stage back)`) · #jobs › Past › Filter (office, desk light): Stage: Any stage (135) · Selling (7) · Back (12); the row for Owenmore reads 'Back · 14 never scanned out: check they're back · 2 tasks left open from before'.
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4 (consistency: one count for one thing) and 1 (visibility of status for the role whose turn it is); Colly's standing rule that every count opens the list that explains it; ADR 0109's own consequence that the fortnight is the window for checking unscanned kit, which only works if the warehouse is shown what to check. · NN/g ten heuristics (consistency and standards; visibility of system status); ADR 0109; design-system principles.md ('healthy is quiet', every count opens its list); ADR 0051 (the warehouse's Home).
- **Proposal (S):** One rule for 'something to sort after the job', read from the Back stage's open Back in task, shared by Home's tile, the Warehouse view's list and the Board's Back column, so the number is the same everywhere and the warehouse sees its check list on its own view. standing.ts: the tile counts jobs whose steps-view stage is back with the Back in task open (still out, or never scanned out), labelled 'jobs with kit to check back in', bad tone when any is more than two days after as the Board does; it opens Stock › Still out after the job as now. HomeScreen.tsx Warehouse view: 'Still out after the job (12)' lists the same jobs, each row with the task's own hint and age in the Board's words ('14 never scanned out: check they're back · 2 days after', warn tone until 2 days, bad after), opening the job's pick list where Scan the kit back lives. The Stock page's 'Still out after the job' part takes the same rule so the count's landing matches. Nothing changes on the Board or the job page.
- **Trade-off:** The tile goes from 3 to 12 on the bench and higher on live until the warehouse works through the unscanned jobs; that is the truth ADR 0109 chose, and it falls to zero as each is checked or passes the fortnight. A company that never scans will see the count sit at a dozen, which argues for scanning rather than for hiding it. **Depends on:** ADR 0109 (the never-scanned rule is already in steps-view; this reads it rather than pick-view's scanned-only list). Nothing stored changes. **Weighed:** A separate second tile 'jobs with kit never scanned out' keeps the old number honest but adds a tile for one idea in two halves; one count is calmer. A notice to the warehouse when a job ends with kit unscanned (the reviewer's theme 3) needs an event the history does not record; the Home row is the push the app already has.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-jobflow/home-warehouse-view-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-jobflow/board-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-jobflow/list-past-filter-office-desk-light.png`
- **Learning:** When a rule changes what a stage means (ADR 0109's 'never scanned out'), every count that stood for that stage must be re-read against the new rule, or Home and the Board drift apart within a week.
- **Sceptic:** added by the sceptic

#### jobflow-1 · One job's position is drawn seven ways, and the status pill goes on saying Confirmed for four of its five stages

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, D, H, W, S · desk 1440 light, phone 390 dark · reproduced

A job's position is drawn in at least six shapes with different words: the head's status pill (Enquiry/Quoted/Confirmed/Lost/Cancelled), the dock line, the Where it is strip with its summary line, the list row's stage line plus pill plus 'Crew b of n' plus 'Kit short', the Board card's one line with an age, and Home's compact row; the person page, call sheet and freelancer link show none. Once the client has said yes the head pill says 'Confirmed' for the rest of the job's life, on a job two days into Back and on one through three weeks, so the biggest word on the head is a Selling fact while the stage sits in a card below. Reading one job across Home, the list, the Board and the head the office meets four wordings of 'kit short' and must learn they are one thing. It costs reading, not errors: a 2.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/v1-job-00159 (Owenmore Productions Film Premiere, ended Wed 4 Nov, office, phone dark): Head pill 'Confirmed'; dock 'Next · Back · 14 never scanned out: check they're back'; card 'In Back · 4 of 8 tasks done'. Three drawings of position on one screen, the pill contradicting nothing but saying nothing. (`web/src/jobs/common.tsx:259-273 (JobStatusPill, STATUS_TONE: confirmed=green); web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:50-57 (the line); web/src/jobs/Steps.tsx:81`) [3 position renderings in the first 1,300 px of the phone page (screenshot)] · #jobs/v1-job-00005 (Shannonside Festivals Summit, through, desk light): Head pill 'Confirmed' on a job three weeks over; dock 'All done. Nothing left to press.'; card 'Through every stage · all 5 tasks done'; Board card 'Through: nothing still out'. (`web/src/jobs/StageLine.tsx:18; web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:86; web/src/jobs/BoardScreen.tsx:120`) · #jobs, #jobs/board, # (Lagan Rugby Club Summit, Planning, Thu 12 Nov): List: 'Thu 12 Nov · Show / Planning · 1 line of kit / Confirmed / Crew 2 of 2 / Kit short' (five parts). Board: 'Lagan Rugby Club · Market Hotel · Thu 12 Nov / 1 line of kit, 1 short · 6 days left'. Home Today/Week ahead: 'Thu 12 Nov / Planning / Crew 2 of 2 / Kit short'. Dock: 'Next · Planning · 1 line of kit, 1 short'. Four wordings of 'kit short' for one job on one day; only the Board says when. (`web/src/jobs/JobsScreen.tsx:432-464; web/src/jobs/BoardScreen.tsx:112-150; web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:198-217 (status word only when !== confirmed); web/src/jobs/PlanScreen.tsx:365 (status only when tentative)`) [4 surfaces × distinct wording, read from the innerText dumps in /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/43aab52f-273b-50fa-8d05-2a57cc5e7671/scratchpad/agents/lens-jobflow/text/] · /f/<token> (freelancer link), #crew/people/<id>, #jobs/<id>/sheet/<phase>: No job position at all: a booking on a lost job reads 'Withdrawn', a booking on a Back job reads 'Confirmed' (the offer's word, which is also the job's status word). (`server/src/crew/page.ts:118-127; web/src/crew/PersonScreen.tsx:397-430`)
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4 (consistency and standards: the same thing drawn the same way) and 1 (visibility of system status: the most prominent word should be the current one); design system principles 'one word for one thing' and 'healthy is quiet'; the 1 Nov research's finding 3 ('where a job was lived in five records') which ADR 0104 fixed on the job page but not in the row parts. · NN/g ten heuristics (consistency; visibility of status); Kanban Guide (where an item is must be visible, one representation of flow); GOV.UK task list and tag patterns (one status tag per row, fixed vocabulary); design-system principles.md and components.md (status chip).
- **Proposal (M):** One shared position part, a stage chip, drawn the same way by every row and head, with the status pill kept only for what a stage can't say: a sale in progress or a stopped job. A JobPosition component in web/src/jobs (sess-sys's own look; the system's status-chip keys, tone by state, text first) in one order everywhere: stage word · the current task's one-line hint · owner first name · time ('by Wed 4 Nov', 'was due Wed 4 Nov', 'in 6 days', '2 days after'). Job page head: pill for Enquiry, Quoted, Lost, Cancelled; once confirmed the head shows the chip ('Planning', 'Back', 'Through') and the dock's line is the same chip's text, so head and dock agree. Jobs list row: the chip replaces stage line + pill + 'Kit short'; 'Crew b of n' stays; rows in Selling or stopped keep the pill since there it is the position. Home Today and week-ahead rows: the chip, compact (stage · hint). Board card: the same component's hint and time as its one line. Person page and freelancer link: unchanged (calls, ADR 0104 part 2), except a booking on a lost or cancelled job says 'Job cancelled' or 'Job lost' beside 'Withdrawn'. Offer the part to the design system's components.md as the stage chip ADR 0104's learnings asked for.
- **Trade-off:** The green 'Confirmed' pill is a reassurance on a planning job; the chip keeps the ok tone while nothing is short, warn when short, bad when late, so the glance survives. Rows lose the 'Kit short' flag into the hint, so the list's Kit filter must stay. One component read by six surfaces is a wider blast radius for a wording change, which is why f1 goes first. **Depends on:** jobflow-f1 first (fields on the task view instead of regexes on hint text). Nothing stored changes. **Weighed:** Adding the stage word beside the pill on the head (S) leaves two words for one position everywhere and still lets 'Confirmed' outrank 'Back'. A stored 'done' status was rightly rejected: ADR 0104 made stages worked out, never ticked.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/job-backAccepted-office-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/job-through-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/list-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/freelancer-link-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-jobflow/job-backAccepted-office-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** A stage chip part: stage word, one-line hint, owner, time, in that order, with tone by state; the status pill is for decisions, the chip for position.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced on my own copy: Owenmore (ended Wed 4 Nov) carries pill 'Confirmed', dock 'Next · Back · 14 never scanned out: check they're back' and card 'In Back · 4 of 8 tasks done'; Shannonside (through) keeps 'Confirmed' with 'All done. Nothing left to press.'; the Jobs row for Lagan Rugby Club is 'Planning · 1 line of kit, 1 short / Confirmed / Crew 2 of 2 / Kit short'; the Board card is '1 line of kit, 1 short · 6 days left'; Home's row is 'Planning / Crew 2 of 2 / Kit short'. Not in the audit's 103 (the stage line, Board and Home rows are ADR 0104 parts 2 and 3; the head pill predates them and no ADR says it must outlive Selling). The planner instance I did not open. The rating is too high: nobody errs or is stopped by it, the words agree in substance, and the office reads one surface at a time; it is a 2, worth doing for cohesion because Colly asked for the flow to read as one thing. The proposal is sound and uses the system's status-chip keys, but it must land after jobflow-f1 (the Board and dock parse hint text) and should keep the status pill on list rows in Selling and on stopped jobs, where it is the position. (was: "One job's position is drawn seven ways, and the status pill goes on saying Confirmed for four of its five stages")

#### jobflow-2 · A job's page says nothing of its invoice: 'All done' with the client unbilled, 'Scan the kit back' with the client invoiced

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, D, W · desk 1440 light, phone 390 dark · reproduced

Colly's thirteen steps end 'invoiced, paid'; the app's five stages end at the deprep tick, and invoice and payment live only on the quote page's What happened card and on Money. So Owenmore reads 'Next · Back · 14 never scanned out' with an accepted quote nobody has invoiced, and Slaney reads the same while its quote is 'Invoiced' since Wed 4 Nov: neither page knows what the other says, and a job through every stage says 'All done. Nothing left to press.' to the office whose own last step is still open. The day after its dates a job in Back also leaves Home's Today and The week ahead and is filed under Jobs › Past, where only the Stage filter (Back, 12) finds it.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/v1-job-00005 (through) and #jobs/money: Job: 'Through every stage · all 5 tasks done', dock 'All done. Nothing left to press.'; Money › Owed to freelancers: 'Shannonside Festivals Summit · RF tech · €575.00 · Mark paid'. Nothing on the job page says a freelancer is owed or the client unbilled. (`shared/src/sync/steps-view.ts:186-189 (finished when Back's tasks closed; invoiced/paid feed nothing, grep returns none); web/src/jobs/Money.tsx:485 ('Not invoiced yet' hint 'Accepted, with no invoice yet: yours to invoice.')`) · #jobs/v1-job-00011 and #jobs/v1-job-00011/quote (Slaney Theatre Company Showcase, ended Sat 31 Oct): Quote page: 'Version 2 · Invoiced'. Job page: pill 'Confirmed', dock 'Next · Back · 14 never scanned out: check they're back', Board 'ended Sat 31 Oct / 14 never scanned out: check they're back, 6 days after' in the bad tone. (`web/src/jobs/QuoteStatus.tsx:11-19 (Paid > Invoiced > status on the quote pill only); web/src/jobs/BoardScreen.tsx:16 (no money on the Board)`) · # (Home, company) and #jobs vs #jobs/board for v1-job-00159 (ended Wed 4 Nov, quote accepted, not invoiced): Home Today (1) and The week ahead (6) do not list it; Jobs › Coming up (132) does not list it; it is under Past (135); the Board's Back column has it with '2 days after'; Money › Accepted, unpaid has it as 'Not invoiced yet'. Four screens to see that one job has three open things (kit back, deprep, invoice). (`web/src/home/standing.ts:72-89 (jobsOn/weekAhead future only); web/src/jobs/JobsScreen.tsx:91 (past = span.end < day)`) [Office taps from Home to that job's open work: Home › Jobs › Past › scroll (job is 3rd) = 3 taps + scroll; or Home › Jobs › Board › Back column (scroll past 45 Planning cards on a phone) = 3 taps + a long scroll]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 2 (match with the real world: the company's process ends at paid, the app's at deprepped) and 1 (visibility of status); the 1 Nov research's five-stage model names Back as the close and lists 'invoiced, paid' among Colly's steps with no home; behavioural.md's Zeigarnik effect (an open loop out of sight is a loop forgotten); Cooper's stage-gate (put a decision where one is really made: the invoice is the office's last decision on a job). · 1 Nov job-flow research (five-stage model; Colly's thirteen steps); Cooper 1990 stage-gate; GOV.UK task list pattern (a task per real decision, done in any order); behavioural.md Zeigarnik; NN/g visibility of status.
- **Proposal (M):** Give the office its close as a task the app reads from the quote, without letting it hold the job in Back: the job is through when the kit is, and 'Invoiced' stays open on a through job until the quote says so. shared/src/steps.ts: a ninth task 'invoiced' under Back, label 'Invoiced', line 'The client has been invoiced for the job.' steps-view reads it from view.money.currentOf: done with 'Invoiced Wed 4 Nov' when the quote is invoiced or paid; 'Not needed' when the job has no quote, or is an errand or meeting; open ('Accepted, not invoiced yet') otherwise. It is the one task the fortnight rule leaves open, and it does not keep a job out of 'through': finished means the kit is back and checked, as now. The dock for a through job with it open says 'Through. Invoice to send' with the button 'Mark it invoiced' for money roles, which opens the quote's What happened card; other roles see 'Through. The invoice is the office's.' The task row shows only the word and the owner, never an amount, so ADR 0046 holds and the Board stays money-free (its Through card does not change). Home: the Money card's existing 'Not invoiced yet' count is the place for it; no new group. Jobs list: the Past tab's head puts 'Still in Back (n)' before the months so a job in Back is not filed as finished; the quote page's What happened card gains the stage chip (theme 1) so money and position read together.
- **Trade-off:** Nine tasks, so 'N of 9 tasks done'; a through job can carry one open task, which is honest but new. Jobs ended before the task existed with an accepted quote never marked invoiced in the app will show 'Invoice to send' until someone marks them, so the ship needs a one-off 'Not needed' for jobs ended before the task's date, as ADR 0109 handled imported jobs. **Depends on:** ADR 0104 (a task under the stored keys) and ADR 0109 (the fortnight rule excludes it and it never holds a job in Back). Colly's word that the invoice is part of the job's story rather than accounts' alone. **Weighed:** A sixth stage 'Closing' (invoiced, paid) was rightly rejected: paid is weeks later and would hold every job in a stage the warehouse can do nothing in. The reviewer's version, the task gating 'through' with no auto-tick, was weighed and rejected because it would refill Back with every job invoiced only in the accounts package. Leaving money out and fixing only Home and Past (S) removes the filing problem but leaves 'All done' untrue for the office.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/money-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/quote-backInvoiced-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/home-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-jobflow/quote-backInvoiced-office-desk-light.png`
- **Sceptic:** Mostly reproduced, with one instance wrong and one softened. Slaney Theatre Company Showcase: quote 'Version 2 · Invoiced' (Wed 4 Nov, SH-1041) while the job page says 'Next · Back · 14 never scanned out: check they're back' and the Board has it in the bad tone 6 days after: money and position do not know about each other. Owenmore (ended Wed 4 Nov, quote accepted, not invoiced): absent from Home's Today (1) and The week ahead (6), under Jobs › Past (135), in the Board's Back column at '2 days after', and on Money as not invoiced: four screens for one job's three open things. But Shannonside Festivals Summit has no quote at all ('Make a quote' on its quote page), so the app cannot know it is unbilled: that instance proves only that a freelancer owed (€575 on Money) is not on the job page. And since 1.85 Jobs › Past has a Filter with Stage · Back (12), so a Back job is findable there, though the tab is still called Past. Not among the 103 fixed; ADR 0042 puts invoiced and paid on the quote and Money, ADR 0104 part 3 keeps money off the Board, neither decides that the job page says nothing of them. The proposal needs changing: a ninth task that the fortnight rule never ticks and that the office records only when it remembers to mark the quote invoiced would hold every such job in Back for good, refilling the swamp ADR 0109 just drained (audit finding 3, P1). The end of a job should stay the kit; invoicing is an office task that outlives it. Re-rated 2/P2: the office does find its invoicing on Money today, and the 'vanishes from Home' half is really the Home-versus-Back disagreement I give as an extra (P1). (was: "A job has no end: 'Through every stage' with the client unbilled, and the day after its dates it leaves Home for Jobs › Past")

#### jobflow-3 · Hand-offs between roles are pulls, not pushes, and the one 'stuck' count is a constant

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, H, W, S · phone 390 dark, desk 1440 light · reproduced

When the office confirms a job the heads and the warehouse are told nothing: they meet it on tiles. Notifications for the office is empty on a company of 300 jobs because notices go only to people named on a job, who sent an offer or were given a task, and tasks are owned by role in practice, not by name. The tile built to show what is stuck, 'jobs in Planning with the next task down to nobody', reads 74 of 132 coming up (56%), which describes how the company works rather than signalling anything, and teaches the office to ignore it. The warehouse never lands on its own view by default.

- **Evidence:** # (Home, company, office, phone dark and desk light): '74 jobs in Planning with the next task down to nobody' under Housekeeping; Coming up is 132 jobs. (`web/src/home/standing.ts:185-191 (tasks-nobody tile: filters stage planning, tasks nobody)`) [74 / 132 coming up = 56% of live jobs flagged, counted from the Jobs tab head] · #notifications (office, desk): 'Nothing here yet. When someone changes a job you're on, your leave or an offer you sent, it shows here.' below it 'To sort out' lists 'Harbour Lights Festival 1 short · Rigger · Load in · in 3 days · Find crew' etc. The flow hand-offs (confirmed, gone out, back, over) are not in either. (`server/src/notices.ts:15-37 (who is told: you / sent / on / made); web/src/Notices.tsx:83-183`) · # as warehouse and as head (phone dark): Both land on the Company view with the office's tiles: '1 person running late', '7 calls still need crew', '3 kit lines short', '73 answers to check', '50 timesheets to approve'. The warehouse's own 'Going out (n)' rows are a segment away; nothing says which of the 7 calls or 3 lines is in this head's department until Department is picked. (`web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:433-538 (department view), 540-599 (warehouse view); the view is remembered per phone, so first use lands on Company`) · #jobs/v1-job-00012 as staff crew (phone dark): 'Next is kit and vans settled, for the heads of department. Nothing here for you to press.' The dock names a role as owner ('the heads of department', 'the warehouse', 'whoever books the crew') while the task card says 'Not given to anyone yet' and the tile counts it as 'down to nobody': three answers to whose it is. (`web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:27-36 (WHOSE); web/src/jobs/Steps.tsx:252`)
- **Basis:** Design-system principle 'healthy is quiet' (a count that is always large is noise); NN/g heuristic 1 (visibility of status: a hand-off must reach the person whose turn it is) and 8 (aesthetic and minimalist: every tile should earn its place); the 1 Nov research's 'owners default to nobody' finding, which 8a answered with a count rather than a default; behavioural.md Default Effect (an owner by role is a default people don't have to set). · 1 Nov research ('owners default to nobody', 'nothing outside the job page counts late tasks'); Kanban Guide (make the hand-off explicit; limit what's in progress rather than count it); GOV.UK notifications and task list; NN/g visibility of status; design-system principles.md ('healthy is quiet', the 19 rules) and behavioural.md (Default Effect).
- **Proposal (M):** Give every task a default owner by role (the dock's WHOSE map made real), push the two hand-offs the history already records as notices to that role's people, and count on Home only what is late or due. shared/src/steps.ts: each task has a 'for' (office / heads / warehouse) that steps-view uses when ownerId is null; the task card says 'For the warehouse (nobody named)' with 'Give it to someone' beside it. Home: the 'down to nobody' tile goes; 'jobs with a task late' stays and gains 'tasks due this week (n)', each opening the Jobs list filtered as now. server/src/notices.ts: two new kinds read from history rows that already exist, project.update to confirmed (to the heads of the departments on its calls or kit lines and the warehouse's people: 'Confirmed: Harbour Lights Festival, Mon 9 Nov') and the first scan out on a job (to the office: 'Gone out: Harbour Lights Festival'). In the app only, the notification list part, nobody emailed (ADR 0048). HomeScreen.tsx:72: a head whose department is the warehouse lands on the Warehouse view the first time; the Company view's tiles name the department when narrowed ('2 LX calls still need crew').
- **Trade-off:** Two more notice kinds, once per transition per job. A default owner by role keeps 'Your tasks' empty for people never named; their work shows on the Department and Warehouse views, where ADR 0051 put it. Dropping the tile loses the one count of unowned tasks, which the Jobs list's Tasks filter keeps. **Depends on:** ADR 0073/0104 (owners are staff by name; this adds a role beneath the name). ADR 0058: extends 'yours' to 'your department's stage', which is Colly's to decide. Nothing contacts anybody outside the app. **Weighed:** Thresholding the tile to jobs within 14 days (S) is honest and could ship first. Notices for 'over with kit still out' and 'all back, deprep open' were weighed and dropped: they are not history events, and the warehouse view's 'Still out after the job' rows are the right home once they count what Back holds (extra sceptic-jobflow-1).
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/home-office-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/notifications-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/home-warehouse-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/job-planning-staff-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-jobflow/home-office-phone-dark.png`
- **Sceptic:** Two of the three instances reproduce: '74 jobs in Planning with the next task down to nobody' on Home (office, phone and desk) against Coming up (132), and Notifications 'Nothing here yet' for the office while To sort out lists calls short. The third is partly the harness: HomeScreen.tsx:72 lands a head who has a department on the Department view the first time (saved?.for ?? department ? 'department' : 'company'); my head persona landed on Company only because the preview's 'me' has no department. For the warehouse the point stands in code: no role ever defaults to the Warehouse view. The tile was added in 1.85 as the fix for the audit's 'nothing on Home counts late or unowned tasks', so this re-litigates a fresh fix, but 56% of coming-up jobs flagged is real evidence that it is a description not a signal, and 'healthy is quiet' is Colly's rule. The proposal needs trimming: ADR 0058's notices are the history narrowed and keep nothing new, so 'Confirmed' (project.update) and 'Gone out' (a move) can be notices, but 'the last day passing with kit still out' and 'all back with deprep open' are not events anyone records; they need a new mechanism the ADR excluded, and they are better served by the Home rows the warehouse already has (see extra). Extending 'what's yours' to a department's stage is a change to ADR 0058 to put to Colly, cited. Re-rated 2/P2: nothing is stuck for want of it this week; the office works from the calls and kit tiles and those do reach them. (was: "Hand-offs between roles are pulls, not pushes, and the one 'stuck' count is a constant")

#### jobflow-4 · Nothing but the Board says how long, and the Board is forty-five Planning cards to one On the job

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles D, O, H · desk 1440 light, phone 390 dark · reproduced

ADR 0104 asked that where an item is be visible and its age known. The job page, the list, Home and the dock show a stage and a count but never how far off the dates are or how long the job has sat there; only the Board's card line carries time. The Board at eight weeks on the office desk is 66 cards over 7,884 px, 31 of them Planning with no break, because Planning is where a confirmed job lives for months; its Back column is nine 'never scanned out' lines in the bad tone because the company rarely scans. The flow is there but drowned, and the run-up is absent from the surfaces the office actually reads.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/board (office, desk light, Next 8 weeks): Column counts in the heads: Selling 14, Planning 45, Going out 0, On the job 1, Back 10, Through 11. Back cards: 'Fergus Festivals Regatta · ended Thu 15 Oct · 23 never scanned out: check they're back, 22 days after' (bad), nine more alike. (`web/src/jobs/BoardScreen.tsx:34-44 (columns), 118-136 (tone: bad when after > 2)`) [Full-page screenshot 2880×15768 at scale 2 = 7,884 CSS px tall; Planning column is ~85% of it. Counted from the column heads in the text dump.] · #jobs/v1-job-00012 and the list/Home rows for it: Head: 'WHEN Thu 12 Nov'. Dock: 'Next · Planning · 1 line of kit, 1 short'. List: 'Thu 12 Nov · Show'. Home: 'Thu 12 Nov'. None says 'in 6 days' or how long it has been in Planning; the Board says '6 days left'. (`web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:50-57 (line has due only when dueOn set); web/src/jobs/StageLine.tsx:13-33; web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:198-217`) · #history/job/v1-job-00159: 'Today ▸ Device eseed1, 9 changes, 13:10 to 13:11 · That's the start of the history.' The history is a device log, not a timeline of the job's journey (confirmed when, sent when, went out when). (`web/src/HistoryScreen.tsx:111`)
- **Basis:** Kanban Guide and Ahmad/Markkula/Oivo 2013 (age of an item in a stage is the signal; a column that holds everything shows nothing); ADR 0104's own rule of thumb (visible position and known age); NN/g heuristic 8 (minimalist: the Board's Back column cries bad for the normal case) and 'healthy is quiet'. · Kanban Guide; Ahmad, Markkula and Oivo 2013; ADR 0104; GOV.UK details/disclosure pattern; design-system states.md (tones) and components.md (disclosure).
- **Proposal (S):** Put the run-up on the stage chip everywhere ('in 6 days', '2 days after') and break the Board's Planning column into the run-up bands the office actually works in. The stage chip (theme 1) ends with time, worked out from the span: before the first day 'in N days' (bad when within 3 days and something is short), on the job 'until Fri', after 'N days after'; a task with a due day shows 'by Wed' instead. The Board's Planning column groups its cards under 'This week', 'Next week', 'Later' with a count each and 'Later' folded by default (the system's disclosure part), so the column reads as the office's horizon; Selling's 'Dates passed, no answer' fold already sets the precedent. Back's 'never scanned out' line takes the warn tone until returnDays + 2 and bad after, and the column head says 'Back 10 · 3 late' as Planning's does. The Board's sub-line 'A column a stage, soonest first' becomes 'A column a stage. This week first; later folded.' Optionally the job page's Where it is card ends with one quiet line, 'In Planning since Tue 3 Nov · first day in 6 days', which gives the page the time it lacks without a timeline.
- **Trade-off:** Bands add three heads to a column and a fold that hides most of the company's work by default; a director who wants everything presses 'Later'. Time on every chip adds a few characters to phone rows already carrying crew and kit. **Depends on:** Theme 1's chip for the time to appear everywhere; alone, the Board changes are S and independent. **Weighed:** Weighed a timeline view of the job built from History (what happened when): L, and the history is grouped by device until sign-in is on, so it would be thin on live today; the one 'since' line on the card gives most of the value. Weighed a WIP limit or a seventh column: rejected, the research says the Board is a view, not the work.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/board-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/job-planning-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/history-job-office-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-jobflow/board-office-desk-light.png`
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced with different numbers on my copy of the same template (the reviewer's counts do not match mine, so one of us read a different day or span): Next 8 weeks on the office desk is Selling 14, Planning 31 · 1 short, Going out 0, On the job 1, Back 12, Through 8 this month; the full page is 7,884 CSS px tall at a desk and 5,158 px on a phone, and Planning is 31 of 66 cards. Every Back card but three is 'N never scanned out: check they're back, N days after' in the bad tone. The job page, list and Home rows show a date but no run-up; only the Board's line has 'N days left' / 'N days after'. Not among the 103 (the audit's finding on the Board was the swamp of unclosed jobs, fixed by ADR 0109; its size is still 7,884 px). ADR 0104 part 3 decided a column a stage, soonest first, read only; grouping Planning by horizon with Later folded does not change that. Proposal is sound and S; the time on the chip depends on theme 1. The Back tone rule (bad after 2 days) is the Board's, not an ADR's, and warn until the return day plus two is kinder to a company that rarely scans. Rated 2/P2 as the reviewer did. (was: "Nothing but the Board says how long, and the Board is forty-five Planning cards to one On the job")

**Smaller findings**

- **jobflow-f1 · Hint wording is load-bearing: the Board and the dock regex the task hint to choose labels and tones** (P2 · severity 1 · Kept by the sceptic). NextDock and BoardScreen match '(\d+) of (\d+) booked', '(\d+) short', 'On until', 'Sent …' in the hint strings steps-view builds, so a wording change in shared/src/sync/steps-view.ts silently changes a button's label or a card's tone. Any proposal above that touches the words (and theme 1 does) must first move these to fields on the view. *Direction:* steps-view exposes {booked, needed, short, outOf, until, sentOn} on the task alongside the hint; the dock and the Board read the fields. (S). *Sceptic:* Verified in code: NextDock.tsx:121-136 matches /^(\d+) of (\d+) booked/, /(\d+) short/ and 'On until' on task.hint; BoardScreen.tsx:104 and 132-136 test hint.includes('short') and /^Sent (.+)$/ on the quote task's auto text; steps-view.ts:190-249 builds those strings. Not a usability problem in itself, which is why 1; P2 only because themes 1 and 4 cannot safely change a word until it is done. Direction and size S are right.
- **jobflow-f2 · 'Confirmed' means three things, and a job row can show two of them side by side** (P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic). 'Confirmed' is the job's status pill, the Selling task's name and a booking's state; on the job page the first two are one meaning, so the only real clash is the job's 'Confirmed' beside bookings' 'Confirmed' on the person page's rows and the list. Theme 1 retires the pill after Selling; renaming the task 'Client said yes' to match its own buttons is a one-line tidy. *Direction:* Rename the task to 'Client said yes' (label) with line 'The client has said yes, and the job is going ahead.' unchanged; keep 'Confirmed' for bookings, where crew already know it. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced: the freelancer page has 'Confirmed' fourteen times as the booking's word, the Jobs row has 'Confirmed' as the job's, and the job page has it as the pill and as the task. But on the job page the pill and the task mean the same thing, the client's yes, so that is one word for one thing; the real overlap is the job's 'Confirmed' against a booking's 'Confirmed' on the person page's rows, and crew know the booking sense well. ADR 0103 names the step 'Confirmed' as the client's answer, so renaming the label to 'Client said yes' is a one-line departure to note, cheap and harmless. Theme 1 removes the pill after Selling anyway. P3, 1. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/list-office-desk-light.png`
- **jobflow-f3 · Errands and meetings say 'Errands and meetings have no stages.' in the card slot but keep the Confirmed pill and a Quote tab** (P3 · severity 1 · Kept by the sceptic). A site visit's page reads pill 'Confirmed', 'MEETING Mon 8 Mar', then a bare line 'Errands and meetings have no stages.' where Where it is would be, then tabs Phases · Crew · Kit and vans · Show pack · Quote. It is honest (ADR 0104: no stages) but the line is a dead slot and the tabs offer a quote and a show pack to a meeting; theme 1's chip would show 'Meeting · Mon 8 Mar' and the slot could go. *Direction:* Drop the slot line; keep the tabs the kind uses (Phases, Crew) and fold Kit, Show pack and Quote behind 'More' for errands and meetings. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced on Brosna University site visit (office, desk light): pill 'Confirmed', 'MEETING Mon 8 Mar', 'Change details', then the bare line 'Errands and meetings have no stages.' where Where it is would be, then tabs Phases · Crew · Kit and vans · Show pack · Quote. ADR 0104 decided errands and meetings have no stages; it did not decide the line stays as a dead slot or that a meeting offers a quote and a show pack. Honest, minor, P3. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-jobflow/job-meeting-office-desk-light.png`

**What held up:** The job page's Where it is card and the one Next dock: on eleven bench jobs at every stage and edge (enquiry, quoted, lapsed, Planning, Back at 2 and 6 days, through, lost, cancelled, hire, meeting) the dock said the right thing in the right place, the role check held (staff crew got 'Nothing here for you to press'), and the ADR 0109 wording ('14 never scanned out: check they're back', 'Taken as done: it ended … with nothing still out', 'Record what happened') read truthfully on each. Lost and cancelled jobs read the same everywhere: pill, dock ('Lost. To take it up again, change its status under Change details.'), no stage line, Not going ahead tab. The Board's Selling fold 'Dates passed, no answer (7)' and Quotes › To follow up's groups tell one story with Home's housekeeping tile. The Board's card line and the dock's line are already nearly the same sentence; theme 1 is making that literal.

### Consistency across areas

*The same kind of thing on Jobs, Stock, Crew, Quotes and Admin.*

sess-sys reads as one app at the frame level and as three or four apps inside it. What holds it together is real: one 24px h1 on every screen, one width, the '‹ All jobs / All stock / All people' Up, one dark surface and one pill class set across jobs, quotes and offers, one date shape ('Fri 6 Nov', with the year added only when it is far) on 23 of 26 screens scanned, a shared Empty part and shared list tools. What pulls it apart is the layer below: a record's page comes in two shapes (job, product and person in the page head with a sub line and tabs; client, venue, item, place, vehicle, quote and pick list as a title inside their first card, with On this screen, or nothing, in place of tabs); the one main action of a list sits in a different place with a different weight in each area (plain 'New job' at the top of Jobs, plain 'Add product' after 'Show all 209 products' at the foot of Stock, filled 'New quote' at the top of Quotes, filled 'Ask for something to be bought' on the Purchase list), and the same red fill is 'Book 1 more crew', 'Check' ×3, 'Save office details' ×2, 'Print or save as PDF' beside 'Client accepted', and 'That's me'; the same split of time is 'Past' on Jobs, 'Before' on a client and a venue, 'Worked' on a person and 'Earlier' on a vehicle, under group heads drawn at four sizes from 12px capitals to the browser's 18.72px; and a fraction is '2 of 3' and '2/3' on the same job page and 29 times 'N of M' beside 56 times 'N/M' on the month planner. None of these stops a person, but each is a small re-learning on crossing from Jobs to Stock to Crew, and the heuristic they break is the fourth (consistency and standards): the same thing should look the same and sit in the same place.

#### consistency-1 · A record's page comes in two shapes, and which one you get depends on the area it was built in

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, D, H, W · phone 390×844 dark, desk 1440×900 light · reproduced

A job, a product and a person put their name in the shared page head (24px h1 outside any card, a sub line such as 'Audio · Speakers · 23 items' or 'Staff · LX · Level 2', the status pill beside the name, then tabs). A client, a venue, an item, a place, a vehicle, a quote and a pick list put their name inside their first card with no sub line, and a job's own sub-pages disagree with each other (the quote's h1 is the job's name under a 'Quote' kicker; the pick list's h1 is 'Pick list'). ADR 0082 wrote the rule for every record and reached the three built first. The office moves between these all day: the eye finds the name in a different place, and 'where are its documents' is a tab on a person and a scroll on a venue.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/v1-job-00025, #stock/product/v1-product-00001, #crew/person/v1-person-00002 (phone 390 dark): h1 in .page-head, sub line under it, pill beside the name on the job, part tabs ('Phases 5 | Crew 2/3 | Kit and vans | Show pack | Quote'; 'Details | On jobs 1 | Items 23 | Cable labels'; 'Coming up | Details | Documents | Worked 2 | Their link') (`web/src/jobs/JobScreen.tsx:156; web/src/jobs/common.tsx:45 (Top takes heading/sub/badge)`) [DOM walk of 32 screens: h1 inside .page-head on 3 record pages, inside .card on 7] · #jobs/clients/v1-client-00001, #jobs/venues/v1-venue-00001, #stock/item/v1-item-00001, #stock/place/v1-place-00001, #stock/vehicles/v1-vehicle-00001, #jobs/v1-job-00144/quote, #jobs/v1-job-00025/pick: h1 ('Shannonside Festivals', 'Riverside Conference Centre', 'SH-000047', 'Bay C1', 'Big Van', 'Barrow Chamber of Commerce Regatta' under a 'QUOTE' kicker, 'Pick list') inside the first card; no sub line; parts as On this screen on the item and venue, nothing on client, place, vehicle (`web/src/jobs/ClientScreen.tsx:29; web/src/jobs/VenueScreen.tsx:34; web/src/stock/ItemScreen.tsx:75; web/src/stock/PlaceScreen.tsx:34; web/src/jobs/Quote.tsx:162`) · desk 1440 light: #stock/product/<id>, #stock/item/<id>, #stock/place/<id> vs #jobs/<id>, #crew/person/<id>, #jobs/clients/<id>, #stock/vehicles/<id>: the Stock area row ('Catalogue Places Labels Counting Testing 16 …') stays above the three stock records; the other five records show no area row (`web/src/jobs/common.tsx:214 (Beside passes views whatever is open); JobScreen uses Page`) [facts-desk.json: views present on product/item/place, absent on job/person/client/venue/vehicle] · #jobs/clients/<id>, #jobs/venues/<id>: the record's jobs are grouped under 'Coming up' and 'Before' drawn as the browser's default h3 (18.72px, heavier than the card's own 20px h2 looks), while the same grouping on Jobs is 12px tracked capitals (`web/src/jobs/Attachments.tsx:272 (['Before', before]); no h3 rule for these cards in jobs.css`) [getComputedStyle: 18.72px/none on client and venue; 12px/uppercase on #jobs 'This week']
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4 (consistency and standards); navigation.md 'the title as the one h1 that says where you are' and 'Tabs are for peers: two to six views of one thing'; ADR 0082 decision 1 (one page head on every screen, outside any card) and 8 (tabs on pages that are really several pages) · NN/g 'Consistency and standards' (Nielsen, 10 heuristics); Material 'canonical layouts' via design-system navigation.md; ADR 0082
- **Proposal (M):** Every record page uses the one Top head (name, a sub line of its kind and facts, pill) and one of two part patterns by length: tabs when it has three or more parts, a plain page otherwise. Client, venue, item, place, vehicle, quote and pick list move their h1 out of the first card into Top (jobs/common.tsx:45) with a sub line in the same grammar as a product and a person: client 'Client · 12 jobs · Shane Lynch'; venue 'Venue · Dublin 1 · 8 jobs here'; item h1 'SH-000047' with sub 'Mic case 24 · Bay C2'; place 'Place · 576 items · counted 27 Sept'; vehicle 'Van · 241-XX-100 · 3 seats'; quote keeps the job's name as h1 with sub 'Quote · version 2 · Sent' and its pill; pick list h1 the job's name with sub 'Pick list · Wed 13 Jan to Tue 19 Jan' and its pill, so the two sub-pages read the same way. The item's and venue's parts become the shared Tabs (Tabs.tsx: item 'Faults | Certificates | In it | Counts | Log'; venue 'Details | On the map | Documents | Jobs here'); client gets 'Details | Documents | Jobs'; place and vehicle stay one page. The record's own facts stay as the first card without repeating the name as its h2. The desk split view (Beside) is left as it is: the Stock row stays because the list is still on screen.
- **Trade-off:** Seven screens change at once and 'Change details' in the first card moves up a few lines; the item page trades its one-scroll feel for a tab switch (ADR 0082 chose tabs for exactly this). **Depends on:** Nothing; Top, Tabs and crumbs exist. Best after consistency-3 so the group heads land once. **Weighed:** Only lift the h1 and leave the parts: S, removes the 'name in a different place' half but leaves documents a tab here and a scroll there. Moving job, product and person to the card-title shape is rejected by ADR 0082.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-consistency/record-heads-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-consistency/record-heads-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-consistency/record-heads-job-person-client-item-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** A record page is one part with one head: name, kind-and-facts sub line, state; the system should say so under Containers so that the fourth record built looks like the first.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced on my own copy (office, phone 390 dark and desk 1440 light): a DOM walk put the h1 inside .page-head on the job, product and person and inside the first .card on the client, venue, item, place, vehicle, quote and pick list, with no sub line on any of the seven (facts.json). Not fixed by 1.77–1.88 and not decided against: ADR 0082 decision 6 states the rule in general ('a record's name is the page's title, outside its cards') and names only the job, person and product, so the proposal carries the ADR to the records it did not reach rather than re-litigating it. Two adjustments. (1) Drop the desk area-row evidence and the 'Beside stops passing the area row' piece: on a desk a product, item and place open beside the Catalogue list (audit finding 25, Beside in common.tsx:214, the search box is still on screen), so the Stock row belongs to the list that is still there; a job, person, client or vehicle has no list beside it. That is a question about which records get a desk split view, not about the head. (2) The client's and venue's 18.72px h3 belongs to consistency-3 (it is a 1.86.0 leftover). I could not confirm an On this screen strip on the item or venue with my selector, so the tabs half of the sketch stands on the reviewer's word; the h1 half is certain. Also worth adding to the statement: a job's two sub-pages name themselves opposite ways (the quote's h1 is the job's name under a 'Quote' kicker; the pick list's h1 is 'Pick list' with the job only in Up). Size M is honest for seven screens. (was: "A record's page comes in two shapes, and which one you get depends on the area it was built in")

#### consistency-2 · The one main action of a screen sits in a different place with a different weight in each area, and the red fill means five things

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, D, H, W · phone 390×844 dark, desk 1440×900 light · reproduced

Each list's 'add' is drawn where its area happened to put it: Jobs' 'New job' is a plain fold at the top; Stock's 'Add product', 'Add place', 'Add vehicle' and People's 'Add person' are plain folds after the list (after 'Show all 209 products' on the Catalogue, 3042px down); Quotes' 'New quote', the Purchase list's 'Ask for something to be bought' and Labels' 'Set numbers aside' are filled red at the top. The same red fill is also 'Book 1 more crew' (the job's Next), 'Check' on each of 50 timesheet rows, 'Save office details' and 'Save printed quotes' both on Admin, 'Print or save as PDF' and 'Client accepted' both on a quote, 'Put it on the map' on a venue and 'That's me' on Leave. So the office cannot tell from the look whether a red button is the next step of the job, a save, a print, or a yes to a client, and cannot predict where the add is on the next list. The design system gives one primary per region in the accent and a quiet variant for row actions; sess-sys has no quiet variant, so row actions borrow primary.

- **Evidence:** #jobs, #stock, #stock/places, #stock/vehicles, #crew/people, #quotes, #stock/buy, #stock/labels (phone 390 dark): 'New job' plain at the top; 'Add product' plain after 'Show all 209 products'; 'Add place' plain after 20 rows; 'Add vehicle' plain after 'Show all 5 checks'; 'Add person' plain after 'Bring back' ×3; 'New quote', 'Ask for something to be bought', 'Set numbers aside' filled [PRIMARY] at the top (`web/src/jobs/JobsScreen.tsx:379 (Fold 'New job'); web/src/stock/StockScreen.tsx:379, 641; web/src/stock/Vehicles.tsx:81; web/src/crew/CrewScreen.tsx:744; web/src/stock/Purchases.tsx:67 (Fold className='primary'); web/src/stock/Labels.tsx; web/src/jobs/QuotesScreen.tsx`) [button list per screen from the DOM walk: 3 of 8 list adds are .primary, 5 plain; 2 at the head, 6 at the foot] · #crew/timesheets, #admin, #jobs/v1-job-00144/quote, #jobs/venues/v1-venue-00001, #crew/leave, #jobs/v1-job-00025: 'Check [PRIMARY]' ×3 visible (50 rows); 'Save office details [PRIMARY]' and 'Save printed quotes [PRIMARY]' on one tab; 'Print or save as PDF [PRIMARY]' and 'Client accepted [PRIMARY]' on one quote; 'Put it on the map [PRIMARY]'; "That's me [PRIMARY]"; 'Book 1 more crew [PRIMARY]' in the dock (`web/src/crew/Timesheets.tsx; web/src/CompanyCard*.tsx; web/src/jobs/Quote.tsx; web/src/jobs/VenueMaps.tsx; web/src/crew/Leave.tsx; web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx; 31 files use className="primary" (grep)`) [primary buttons counted per screen: Timesheets 3 on screen, Admin › Company 2, quote 2, job 1]
- **Basis:** components.md Button: 'Primary: the one main action in a region. One per region, in the accent'; 'Quiet: toolbars, a row's actions'; principles.md rule 1 (the accent is the one thing to do next); NN/g heuristic 4 and Default Effect (behavioural.md) · components.md Button (one primary per region, quiet variant); NN/g 'Visual hierarchy'; GOV.UK Design System button guidance (one primary call to action per page)
- **Proposal (M):** One rule: a list's add is a plain fold first in the list on every list; red fill is kept for the job's Next dock and for one explicit yes per screen; row and save actions take a quiet variant. Every list opens with its add as a plain Fold under the search, as Jobs does on a phone: Catalogue 'Add product', Places 'Add place', Vehicles 'Add vehicle', People 'Add person' move up from the foot; Quotes' 'New quote', the Purchase list's 'Ask for something to be bought' and Labels' 'Set numbers aside' lose className='primary' (Purchases.tsx:67, QuotesScreen, Labels.tsx:456); on a desk Jobs' 'Add job' goes plain to match its phone fold. Timesheets' 'Check' and Calls' 'Checked' become button.quiet (new in app.css after the system's ds-btn--quiet: no fill until hovered). The quote keeps 'Client accepted' as its one primary and 'Print or save as PDF' goes plain; 'Put it on the map' and "That's me" go plain. Admin's three Saves stay for now, each being its card's one action. Then the red fill reads as one thing on every screen: the next step or the one yes. The Next dock is untouched.
- **Trade-off:** The Purchase list and Labels lose a loud button the warehouse may have learned; on the long Stock lists the fold now opens at the top, under the search. **Depends on:** A quiet button variant in app.css (half a day). **Weighed:** The head's actions slot for every add: rejected because that slot is the Next dock (ADR 0104) and would put a dock on every list. Every add primary at the foot: rejected, invisible at the foot of a 3000px list.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-consistency/list-heads-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-consistency/list-adds-and-red-fill-jobs-buy-quotes-timesheets-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** An app's list pages need the head's action slot as much as its record pages do; the system could say under App bar / Section where a list's add lives.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced: the filled-red count per screen on my copy matched or exceeded the reviewer's (Timesheets 'Check' ×3 in view, Admin › Company three Saves not two: office details, printed quotes, travel and subsistence; the quote's 'Print or save as PDF' and 'Client accepted'; the venue's 'Put it on the map'; Leave's "That's me"; Calls' 'Checked'; the job's 'Book 1 more crew'; Quotes' 'New quote', the Purchase list's 'Ask for something to be bought' and Labels' 'Set numbers aside' at the top, with 'New job' a plain fold at the top of Jobs and 'Add product', 'Add place', 'Add vehicle', 'Add person' plain folds at the foot; code at JobsScreen.tsx:379, StockScreen.tsx:379 and 641, Vehicles.tsx:81, CrewScreen.tsx:744, Purchases.tsx:67). One more instance the reviewer missed: on a desk the Jobs list's add is a filled 'Add job' (the form sits beside the list) while on a phone it is the plain fold, so the same list's add changes weight by viewport. The rule already exists and is broken: components.md Button says one primary per region and quiet for a row's actions, and ADR 0104 says the dock 'is the only filled red on the page'; the audit's finding on the job page's second primary was fixed in 1.78, so this is the same rule applied to the other 30-odd files. Not an audit finding and no ADR decides the lists' adds. The proposal needs one change: Top's actions slot is the Next dock (ADR 0104: 'the page's one next thing', docked above the bar on a phone), so putting every list's add there would give every list a dock and dilute Next. The add should instead be a plain Fold first in the list card everywhere, which is Jobs' pattern and already where the eye is. Admin's Saves each sit in their own card, so 'one primary per region' is arguably met there; I'd leave Admin out of the first pass. Size M is honest (52 files carry className="primary"). (was: "The one main action of a screen sits in a different place with a different weight in each area, and the red fill means five things")

#### consistency-3 · The same split of time has four names, under group heads drawn at four sizes

**P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, D, H, W, S · phone 390×844 dark, desk 1440×900 light · reproduced

Every list of jobs splits the same way (ahead, happened, not going ahead) and names the second part differently: 'Past' on Jobs, 'Before' on a client and a venue, 'Worked' on a person, 'Earlier' on a vehicle. The heads over such groups are 12px tracked capitals on Jobs, Stock, the pick list and Crew's dates, 13px capitals on Crew's sections, Timesheets and Money's 'Paid lately', 16px sentence case on Quotes, Repairs, Testing and Home, and the browser's default 18.72px on a client's and venue's job groups (new in 1.86.0, with no rule) and Money's 'Invoiced'. Calls has two sizes on one screen. A head's size says nothing about how important the group is.

- **Evidence:** #jobs, #jobs/clients/v1-client-00001, #jobs/venues/v1-venue-00001, #crew/person/v1-person-00002, #stock/vehicles/v1-vehicle-00001, #quotes: 'Past (135)' chip; 'Before' h3; 'Before' and 'Not going ahead' h3; 'Worked 2' tab; 'Earlier (10)' summary; 'Dates passed with no answer' h3 (`web/src/jobs/JobsScreen.tsx:84-86; web/src/jobs/Attachments.tsx:272; web/src/crew/PersonScreen.tsx:56; web/src/stock/Vehicles.tsx:446; web/src/jobs/QuotesScreen.tsx`) · #jobs 'This week', #crew 'Fri 6 Nov', #quotes 'Waiting for an answer', #jobs/clients/<id> 'Coming up', #admin/changes '1.88.0 …': group heads at 12px uppercase, 13px uppercase, 16px none, 18.72px none, 20px none (`web/src/jobs/jobs.css:56 (text-pill uppercase); web/src/crew/crew.css:123,152 (text-hint uppercase); web/src/jobs/jobs.css:632 (text-small); no rule for client/venue h3; web/src/jobs/jobs.css:485 (money h3 unsized)`) [getComputedStyle(h3).fontSize/textTransform across 32 screens (facts-phone.json): five distinct pairs]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4 and 2 (match between system and the real world: one word for one thing); components.md Section: 'a heading in text.label and ink.tertiary'; principles.md Type: 'the label is the workhorse' · NN/g 'Consistency and standards'; GOV.UK content design 'use the same word for the same thing'; design-system components.md Section
- **Proposal (S):** One word for a job list's past, and one shared group-head style for a head over a run of rows. Words, in shared/src/plain.ts: 'Coming up' / 'Past' / 'Not going ahead' on the client's and venue's JobsCard (Attachments.tsx:272), the person's 'Worked' tab becomes 'Past 2' with a first line 'Jobs they worked', the vehicle's 'Earlier' becomes 'Past'; Quotes keeps its own state names. Heads: one .group-head rule in app.css (the Jobs list's: text-pill, 700, uppercase, .05em, --muted; 13px if the phone reading proves 12 too small) on every h3 that heads a run of rows: Crew's sections and dates, Timesheets, Quotes' follow-up groups, Repairs, Testing, Home's groups, the client's and venue's job groups, Money's owed groups. Counts stay in the head.
- **Trade-off:** 'Worked' carried a meaning that 'Past' loses; the first line keeps it. Quotes' 16px sentence heads were readable and get smaller. **Depends on:** Nothing. **Weighed:** 16px sentence case as the one head: rejected, the system's Section says label size in tertiary ink and the capitals already head the lists crew use most.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-consistency/group-heads-jobs-timesheets-quotes-client-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** A Section head rule should name its size on a phone; sess-sys found 12px capitals the de facto group head and the system could record it.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced. Words: Jobs' chip 'Past', the client's and venue's 'Before' (Attachments.tsx:272), the person's 'Worked' tab (PersonScreen.tsx:56), the vehicle's 'Earlier' summary (Vehicles.tsx:446). Heads, by getComputedStyle on 32 screens: 12px uppercase on Jobs, Stock, the pick list and Crew's date heads; 13px uppercase on Crew's 'On today' and 'Short of crew', Timesheets and Money's 'Paid lately'; 16px sentence on Quotes, Repairs, Testing and Home; the browser's 18.72px default on the client's and venue's job groups, Quotes' 'Before this app, from Current RMS' and Money's 'Invoiced' heads. Calls has 12px and 13px heads on the one screen. Three adjustments. (1) The item's and place's 'Put an item in' and What's changed's 20px are form sub-heads and release titles, not heads over a run of rows: drop them from the evidence. (2) Quotes' three groups are quote states, not a split of time, and 'Dates passed with no answer' was chosen in 1.78.0 (changelog, ADR 0109): leave Quotes' wording alone. (3) The client/venue 18.72px head is a 1.86.0 leftover (the grouping is new, with no rule in jobs.css), which is the one instance a person would notice as unfinished. Re-rated nn 1, P3: a head reading 'Worked' then 'Before' then 'Past' re-learns a word, nobody is stopped. Size S holds; components.md Section already says a heading in text.label and tertiary ink, which the 12–13px capitals in --muted are. (was: "The same split of time has four names, under group heads drawn at four sizes")

#### consistency-4 · A fraction is 'N of M' on one line and 'N/M' on the next, and money shows cents on one line and not the next

**P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, D, H · phone 390×844 dark · reproduced

The crew count is '2 of 3 booked' in the job's facts and 'Crew 2/3' on its tab a few lines below; the month planner draws 'N/M' 56 times in its cells and 'N of M' 29 times in its list on the one screen. Home's tile says '€19,189' and its sentence '€19,189.00' for the same number, because the tile rounds with its own formatter while everything else uses euroText (cents only when not whole). A head scanning the month for '1/2' and then '1 of 2' is matching two shapes.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/v1-job-00025: '2 of 3 booked, 1 to confirm' under CREW and 'Crew 2/3' as the tab label (`web/src/jobs/JobScreen.tsx:74 (tab labels); StageLine`) [text scan: 'N of M' ×4, 'N/M' ×1 on the one page] · #jobs/plan/month, #jobs/plan/week: cells '1/2', '2/2', '1/3'; list rows '2 of 2', '2 of 4', '0 of 2' [regex over innerText: month 56 'N/M' and 29 'N of M'; week 1 and 2] · #home, #quotes, #crew/people, #crew/timesheets: '€19,189' tile and '€19,189.00' sentence on Home; '€3,540.00' on Quotes; '€270' rates on People; '€683.50' beside '€290' on Timesheets (`web/src/format.ts:30 rateText (cents only when not whole) vs shared/src/money.ts formatting with two decimals`) [Home: 6 amounts with cents, 8 whole]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4; nngroup.md on numbers (one format for one kind of number); Baymard on price display consistency · NN/g 'Consistency and standards'; Baymard Institute price formatting; design-system nngroup.md
- **Proposal (S):** One shape for a fraction ('N of M' in prose, rows and tabs; 'N/M' only inside a planner cell) and Home's tile using the shared euroText. JobScreen.tsx:152 makes the tab 'Crew 2 of 3'; the planner's list rows keep 'N of M' and its cells keep 'N/M' with a title 'N of M booked'; HomeScreen.tsx:394 wholeEuro goes, the tile reads euroText like the sentence under it.
- **Trade-off:** The tab gets three characters wider. **Depends on:** Nothing. **Weighed:** Leave the tab as 'N/M' as the compact form: acceptable only if the system records it as the form for a cell under 60px; the tab has room.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced: the job page has '2 of 3 booked' in its sub line and 'Crew 2/3' as its tab (JobScreen.tsx:152 builds `${booked}/${needed}`); the month planner has 56 'N/M' and 29 'N of M' by regex on innerText, the week 1 and 2. Money: Home's tile says '€19,189 confirmed' and its sentence '€19,189.00 confirmed of €22,729.00 quoted' for the same number (HomeScreen.tsx:394 wholeEuro rounds, the sentence uses another formatter). But the rest of the money half is one rule, not two: shared/src/money.ts:84 euroText and format.ts:29 rateText both drop the cents only when the sum is whole, so '€270' and '€683.50' are the same rule applied, and the quote sheet showing '€3,540.00' is a priced document reading as an invoice does. So the money finding narrows to Home's two formatters. Not fixed in 1.77–1.88, no ADR. nn 1, P3, S. (was: "A fraction is 'N of M' on one line and 'N/M' on the next, and money shows cents on one line and not the next")

#### consistency-5 · Search placeholders come in two grammars and an example

**P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, H, W · phone 390×844 dark · reproduced

Jobs, Stock and Quotes say 'Find a …', Clients, Venues and People say what to type ('Name or contact', 'Name or address', 'Name, phone, email, department or skill'), and Testing and the pick list give an example ('SH-000123'). None stops anyone; it is a small sign the lists were written one at a time.

- **Evidence:** #jobs, #stock, #quotes, #jobs/clients, #jobs/venues, #crew/people, #stock/testing, #jobs/<id>/pick: the seven placeholders above as the DOM showed them (`grep placeholder= across web/src: 'Name, phone, email, department or skill' ×2, 'SH-000123' ×4`) · #stock, #stock/repairs, #crew/people, #jobs/v1-job-00025/pick: 'All (209) | Audio (105) | Video (40) | 2 more' chips; 'All (15) | Audio (9) | Video (1)'; People: 'Filter' only; pick: 'All | Audio 1 left | Video 1 left' + 'By department | By where it's kept' (`web/src/stock/StockScreen.tsx; web/src/crew/CrewScreen.tsx (ListTools department filter); web/src/jobs/PickScreen.tsx`)
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4; ADR 0099 (each list keeps its one main choice as chips, filters narrow inside) · ADR 0099; NN/g search placeholder guidance; design-system components.md List tools
- **Proposal (S):** One placeholder grammar on every list's search. 'Find a client by name or contact', 'Find a venue by name or address', 'Find a person by name, phone, email or skill', 'Find an item by its number' on Testing and the pick list; the three 'Find a …' stay.
- **Trade-off:** People's placeholder gets longer on a phone and may clip. **Depends on:** Nothing. **Weighed:** Department chips on People: rejected, ADR 0099 put People's department in Filter and 1.79.1 starts a head on their own.
- **Sceptic:** The seven placeholders reproduced word for word on my copy ('Find a job, client or venue', 'Find a product, number or serial', 'Find a job, client, venue or number', 'Name or contact', 'Name or address', 'Name, phone, email, department or skill', 'SH-000123'). The department half is decided: ADR 0099 decision 1 names Jobs', Quotes' and the catalogue's main choice as chips and put People's department inside Filter (CrewScreen.tsx:205-223), and 1.79.1 makes a head start on their own department there, so the reviewer's 'per ADR 0099' reading is the opposite of what it decided; the pick list's toggle is a different question, as the reviewer says. What is left is a placeholder grammar, which nobody trips on: demoted to a finding, nn 1, P3, S. (was: "Search boxes and department narrowing are worded and drawn differently per area")

**Smaller findings**

- **consistency-f1 · The Up word names the area where the page it opens is named Catalogue or Calls** (P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic). A product's Up reads '‹ All stock' and opens 'Catalogue'; a person's '‹ All people' opens 'People' but a page under Crew's root reads '‹ Crew' and opens 'Calls'. ADR 0082 titled those two pages by themselves on purpose; the Up words were not changed with them. *Direction:* Up names the parent page as its title reads: '‹ Catalogue' (STOCK.up) and '‹ Calls' (CREW.up) in Up.tsx:23-24, leaving the Menu's 'Stock' and 'Crew' as the areas' names. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced: a product's Up reads '‹ All stock' (Up.tsx:23) and opens the h1 'Catalogue'; the Menu's 'Stock' does the same. But the page's title is decided: ADR 0082 decision 1 says 'Calls and Catalogue are titled by themselves now, not Crew and Stock; the bar's small line still names the area', and the audit noted 'Stock opens Catalogue' under what held up without a finding. So the direction 'or the page is titled Stock' is out. What is open is the Up word, and it is a pattern of two: '‹ All stock' opens 'Catalogue' and '‹ Crew' (CREW has no up word) opens 'Calls', while '‹ All jobs' opens 'Jobs'. The item's '‹ Mic case 24' is right (the parent is its product, navigation.md Up) and its crumbs rightly skip Places, so drop that half. nn 1, P3, S.
- **consistency-f3 · Stock's logs draw a day with a second formatter: no weekday and 'Sept' where everything else says 'Sep'** (P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic). Twenty-three of twenty-six screens draw a day with shared dayLabel ('Fri 6 Nov', the year when far: 'Wed 22 Sep 2027'). The item's log and Testing use an Intl call in stock/common.tsx:575 that drops the weekday and spells 'Sept', so the same item reads 'Mon 28 Sep 2026' on its place's count and '27 Sept 2026' on its own log. *Direction:* stock/common.tsx's day formatter calls dayLabel(d, today) so the log, Testing and counts share the one shape; What's changed may keep its long date as a document's. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced the item's '6 Nov 2026' and '27 Sept 2026' (stock/common.tsx:575, Intl en-IE short month, no weekday), Testing's '20 Oct 2025' (same formatter) and What's changed's '6 November 2026' (ChangesScreen.tsx:20, a release date written long on purpose). But the vehicle's 'Wed 22 Sep 2027' is the shared dayLabel with its year because the date is far (crew-days.ts:95-98), the standard shape, not a third one; the place's 'Mon 28 Sep 2026' is the same. So the finding is two formatters, not three shapes: shared dayLabel ('Sep', with the weekday) and the Intl call in stock/common.tsx ('Sept', no weekday) on the item log and Testing. Not among 1.77–1.88 (1.82's year fix was Timesheets). nn 1, P3, S.

**What held up:** One 24px h1 on every one of 32 screens, and one width: the frame is one app (ADR 0082, 0091). One Up grammar on records: '‹ All jobs', '‹ All stock', '‹ All people', '‹ All places', '‹ All vehicles', '‹ All clients', '‹ All venues'; '‹ Menu' for the Menu's pages. One date shape, 'Fri 6 Nov' with the year only when far, on 23 of 26 screens including 99 dates on the Board and 74 on Quotes. One pill class set shared by jobs, quotes and offers (pending/confirmed/cancelled/bad/waiting), so 'Quoted', 'Sent' and 'Offered' look alike as waiting states. The shared Empty part ('Nothing here yet.' then the screen's own line) and the shared list tools (Sort, Filter (n), chips, '12 of 140 jobs.') on every long list. The area rows on Jobs, Stock and Crew with counts where there is something to count, and the three records built first (job, product, person) as the model for the rest.

**Dropped by the sceptic:** consistency-f2 (The edit fold has three names: Reproduced the labels, but it is not a pattern: 'Change details' is on all seven records and 'Change details and checks' is one vehicle-only label (Vehicles.tsx:347), while 'Change' on a document row or a phase is a row's own action on one line, a different thing from a record's edit fold. One screen's slip, which the harness says not to report.); consistency-f4 (Quotes lives in two places by viewport while Money lives in two places on one: Reproduced (phone: '‹ Menu', no area row; desk: a sidebar root with no Up).
