- **Sceptic:** Reproduced on my server as the office, desk light and phone dark: Home's views row is Company · Department · Warehouse · Me (HomeScreen.tsx:56-60), the office lands on Company, and its 15 tiles put '73 answers to check' 7th and '50 timesheets to approve' 10th under 'Housekeeping', below four warehouse tiles; Crew's heading reads 'Answers to check (74)' and the Crew badge 74. personas.md line 74-76 promises 'Home, shown for the office, puts answers, crew needed, timesheets, documents and running late first', which the code does not do, so this is a documented intent not met rather than taste; the 20 October finding 33 grouping (SOON set) was built for the company, not the office. Not fixed in 1.77-1.88 and no ADR decides the office's Home order (ADR 0033 has four views and sends the office to Crew). Two parts of the proposal I cut: (1) a fifth 'Office' view is bigger than needed, since the viewer's role is known (shared/src/roles.ts: 'office') and the Company view can order its tiles for whoever looks; (2) folding the planner's To sort out into a link is wrong, because 1.84.0 deliberately put the three-day crew-short list on the planner where the office books, and five rows drawn twice costs less than a hop. The crew-short vocabulary point is real but mild: heading, count, button and filter are different grammatical slots; only 'Crew still to find' (planner legend) and 'Short of crew' (Crew sub-heading) are a second adjective for one state. (was: "What needs the office is spread over six surfaces, in different words, with counts that don't agree")

#### office-2 · Arriving at a job's part from another screen lands at the top of the job page, a screen and a quarter above the part, on a phone

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, H, W · phone 390 dark, desk 1440 light · reproduced

Every list that sends the office or a head into a job for a reason links to the part: Crew › Calls 'Open job' and 'Offer…' and To sort out's 'Find crew' go to #jobs/<id>/crew. On a phone the tap lands at the top of the job page with the tab row 800-1,050 px down, so the person reads the head they already read on Calls and then scrolls to the thing they came for. ADR 0109 made the Next button bring its tab into view; the same page ignores the part in its own address. Home's tiles already land on the part they name (arrive()), so the job page is the one screen where a link to a part does not.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/v1-job-00086/crew (opened by address, phone): Landed at scrollY 0; the tab row's top at 1,052 px; the crew panel at 1,120 px. The screen shows the head, the dock and the top of Where it is. (`web/src/jobs/JobScreen.tsx:96-128 (`go` sets `went`; `asked` from the address only sets `wanted`)`) [Playwright, 390×844, getBoundingClientRect of [role=tablist] after goto: top 1052; scrollY 0] · #jobs/v1-job-00086 › Next 'Book 3 more crew' (phone): After the Next press the tab row is at 155 px (scrollY 897): the fix of ADR 0109 works for the button. (`web/src/jobs/JobScreen.tsx:118-128`) [scrollY 897, tablist top 155 after click] · #crew (Calls), #notifications, #jobs/plan: 'Open job' (twice on Calls), 'Offer…' and 'Find crew' all link to #jobs/<id>/crew. (`web/src/crew/CrewScreen.tsx:351-352,453-454; web/src/jobs/PlanScreen.tsx:577`) [4 entry points on 3 screens, same address shape] · #jobs/v1-job-00086 (desk): '0 of 3 booked, 1 to confirm' appears in the head's CREW fact, in the Next line, in the Crew booked task row, and as 'Crew 0/3' on the tab, before the crew list. (`web/src/jobs/JobScreen.tsx head facts; web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:59-122; web/src/jobs/Steps.tsx task rows`) [the same count 4 times on one screen above the tab panel]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 1 (visibility of system status: the press seems to do nothing) and 8 (minimalist: the same fact four times); the 5 November audit finding 1 and ADR 0109's own rule that Next 'brings what it opens into view' — the links in from other screens break the same rule; the design system's navigation page (a link lands on the thing it names). · NN/g 'Visibility of System Status'; NN/g response-time and 'the user's place' research; Bergstrom-Lehtovirta and Oulasvirta (2014) thumb reach cited by ADR 0104; the 5 November audit finding 1 and ADR 0109
- **Proposal (S):** A link that names a part of the job page lands on that part, the same way Next does. JobScreen.tsx: on first render with `asked` set from the address (and no askedTask), run the same scrollIntoView on the tab row that `go` runs, with focus on the tab and no history step. Opened with a task from Home's Your tasks, keep today's behaviour. On a desk the same code runs and is nearly a no-op (row at 832 px in 900).
- **Trade-off:** A direct link to a job's crew no longer shows the head first; someone wanting the dates scrolls up instead of down. A reload after picking a tab lands on that tab too, which is arguably right. **Depends on:** Nothing; ADR 0109's scroll code is in place. **Weighed:** A sticky tab row under the bar on a phone: rejected, the head and Where it is would still sit between the person and the part, and it edges toward nested scroll.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/job-crew-link-lands-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/job-crew-next-lands-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/crew-calls-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/job-confirmed-crew-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-office/job-crew-from-calls-tap-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** A rule for the page's one primary action should also say what a link into a part of the page does: it lands on the part.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced on phone 390x844 dark as the office: opening #jobs/v1-job-00086/crew by address lands at scrollY 0 with the tab row at 1,052 px (page 2,116 px); tapping 'Open job' on Crew › Calls lands on #jobs/<id>/crew at scrollY 0 with the tab row at 809 px (a real tap, not only the address); pressing Next 'Book 3 more crew' brings the row to 155 px (scrollY 897), so ADR 0109's fix covers the button and not the link in. JobScreen.tsx:118-128 scrolls only when `went` increments. Not among the 103 (finding 1 was the Next button) and not decided by an ADR; the app's own convention elsewhere (Home tiles call arrive() with a part and the list scrolls to it) says a link lands on the part it names, so the job page is the odd one out. Two adjustments: the rating, because the tap visibly opens a new page (the job's head) rather than seeming dead, so it is a 2 and not finding 1's 3, and on a desk the row is already at 832 px in a 900 px viewport; and the second half of the proposal (dropping the count from the Next dock's line on a phone) is cut, since the dock's line is the stage hint ADR 0104 defines and the head fact, task row and tab each have their own job. The design system's navigation.md does not say 'a link lands on the thing it names' in those words; the basis is NN/g heuristic 1 and the app's own arrive() convention. (was: "Arriving at a job's part from another screen lands at the top of the job page, a screen and a quarter above the part, on a phone")

#### office-4 · The job's story ends at deprep on the job page and the Board, while the office's ends at timesheets approved, invoiced and paid

**P3 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, D · desk 1440 light, phone 390 dark · reproduced

Where it is ends at 'Checked and deprepped' and the Next dock says 'All done', while the office's last steps of a job (timesheets approved, invoiced, paid) are recorded on the quote's What happened card and listed on Money by client and on Timesheets by person. A through job's page never says it is uninvoiced, and no list says which jobs are through and not yet invoiced. personas.md's table records this split as the design; the cost is that the office closing a job visits three screens to know it is closed.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/v1-job-00086 (Where it is) and #jobs/board (Through): Tasks end at 'Checked and deprepped'; the Through column cards say only 'Through: nothing still out'. (`shared/src/steps.ts (eight tasks, five stages); web/src/jobs/BoardScreen.tsx:16 ('nothing about money is on it')`) [0 of the 8 tasks or 5 stages names a timesheet, an invoice or payment] · #jobs/money: 'ACCEPTED, UNPAID €166,715.70 with VAT, of which €98,066.70 not invoiced yet' and 'Not invoiced yet €98,066.70 · Accepted, with no invoice yet: yours to invoice' listed by client (Fergus Festivals 2 quotes €28,028.01 …), not by job. (`web/src/jobs/Money.tsx (Accepted, unpaid)`) [the not-invoiced list is by client; a job's own page never says 'not invoiced'] · #crew/timesheets and #jobs/<id>/crew: Timesheets › To approve (50) rows name the job ('Dara Quinn · Autumn Gala · Show · Sound No.1 · €683.50 · Check'); the job's Crew tab lists the same calls with no timesheet state. (`web/src/crew/Timesheets.tsx:55-80; web/src/crew/Calls.tsx (job's crew panel)`) [a job's timesheet state is only reachable by scanning a 50-row list by person] · #jobs/v1-job-00065/quote: What happened: SENT Fri 6 Nov · CLIENT'S ANSWER Not yet · Client accepted / Client turned it down; invoiced and paid are recorded here, two pages from the job's Where it is. (`web/src/jobs/QuoteRecords.tsx; web/src/jobs/Steps.tsx:279 (the job page already reads `current.invoicedOn`)`) [job page → Quote tab → Open the quote → What happened: 3 taps to the invoice record]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 2 (match between system and the real world: the company's job ends when it is paid) and 1 (status); the job-flow research's stage-gate rule that the flow shows every station the company has (Colly's list ends at deprep, but personas.md's table ends at 'Invoiced, paid' for O and D); ADR 0104 (five stages, money off the Board) and ADR 0042 (only the office and directors see money), cited as the constraint, not re-litigated. · Cooper's stage-gate (every station the company has), GOV.UK task list (rows worked out, any order), NN/g 'match between system and the real world'; sess-sys personas.md 'How a job moves through them' (rows 'Timesheet approved' and 'Invoiced, paid')
- **Proposal (S):** Give Quotes a 'To invoice' group and the job page, for money roles only, one quiet line after the tasks that reads the quote's record, leaving the Board and the five stages as ADR 0104 has them. Quotes › To follow up (Money.tsx): a group 'To invoice (N)' after 'Answered: put the job in step', each row 'Job · client · €amount · through since Mon 19 Oct', opening the quote's What happened. Job page (Steps.tsx, under the task list, only with the money ability): one row in the task-list shape, worked out never ticked: 'Invoice · Not invoiced yet' (opens the Quote tab) quiet while the job is before Back, warn once it is through; 'Invoiced Mon 2 Nov · paid' when done. Home (money roles) counts 'N jobs through, not invoiced' with href #quotes part 'To invoice'.
- **Trade-off:** One more row on the job page for money roles; the words must stay in step with QuoteRecords.tsx. **Depends on:** currentOf(job).invoicedOn/paidOn already read in Steps.tsx:279. **Weighed:** A sixth 'Settled' stage: rejected with the reviewer, a stage everyone sees but only money roles can move breaks ADR 0034. Timesheet state on the job's Crew tab: deferred until timesheets are in use (ADR 0090).
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/jobs-board-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/money-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/crew-timesheets-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/job-quoted-quote-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-office/job-80-accepted-quote-tab-desk-light.png`
- **Learning:** When a flow's stages are everyone's but its last steps are one role's, the record page can carry a role-gated tail in the task-list shape without adding a stage.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced: job v1-job-00080 (quote accepted, not invoiced) has no word about money on its page or Where it is; its Crew tab has no timesheet word; its Quote tab says 'Accepted, Fri 6 Nov · Invoice number · Mark invoiced', one tab away, and a paid job's Quote tab says 'Paid'. Money lists 'Not invoiced yet €98,066.70' by client; Timesheets › To approve (50) is by person. personas.md's own table ends the job at 'Timesheet approved | Crew › Timesheets; Home' and 'Invoiced, paid | Recorded on the job's quote; owed and paid on Jobs › Money', so what the reviewer describes is the documented design, and ADR 0104 keeps money off the Board and the stages. Not fixed in 1.77-1.88. What is true in use: a through job's page says 'All done' to the office while its quote is uninvoiced and its timesheets unapproved, and nothing lists jobs to invoice by job. The job page already carries a money-only Quote tab, so a role-gated tail breaks no ADR. Re-rated to P3: the records exist and are one tab or one screen away, the seed's 50 timesheets and €98k are volume-seed scale, and Session Hire won't use timesheets on day one (ADR 0090). The proposal is trimmed: a Quotes 'To invoice' group and a quiet money-only line on the job page; the per-job timesheet narrowing and a timesheet word on every Crew-tab row are dropped as a second screen for a feature that is off. (was: "The job's story ends at deprep on the job page and the Board, while the office's ends at timesheets approved, invoiced and paid")

#### office-3 · A quote waiting on a client is said four ways, with two clocks, and 'worth a call' shows on one screen only

**P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles O, D · desk 1440 light, phone 390 dark · reproduced

A sent quote with no answer reads 'Quote with the client since Fri 6 Nov' on the Board, 'Still quoted' on the jobs list, the job page's Next line and the Confirmed task, and 'Sent 3 days ago' on Quotes › To follow up; a job quoted with no quote record reads 'Waiting on the client'. The Board's Selling column shows two clocks side by side (days left to the job on enquiries, the day sent on quotes), and the one nudge the office acts on, 'worth a call' after seven days, is worked out in Money and shown only on Quotes and Notifications, which ADR 0081 chose. The overview surfaces (Board, list, job page) can't tell a quote sent yesterday from one sent a fortnight ago; the follow-up surface can.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/board (Selling): 'Barrow Chamber of Commerce Hackathon … Waiting on the client' beside 'Corrib Arts Week … Quote with the client since Tue 3 Nov' beside 'Shannonside Festivals Fashion Show … No quote yet · Still enquiry · 10 days left'. (`web/src/jobs/BoardScreen.tsx:133 (`sent ? 'Quote with the client since …' : 'Waiting on the client'`)`) [3 phrasings and 2 clocks ('since', 'days left') in one column of 14 cards] · #jobs (list) and #jobs/v1-job-00065: List row: 'Selling · Still quoted · Quoted'. Job page Next line: 'Next · Selling · Still quoted'; task row 'Confirmed · Still quoted · Next'; Quote sent row 'Sent Fri 6 Nov · Done'. No 'days since sent' and no 'worth a call'. (`web/src/jobs/StageLine.tsx; web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:59-115; shared/src/steps.ts STAGE_LINES`) [the sent date appears only on the Quote sent task row; the age is never computed on the job page] · #quotes (To follow up): 'Waiting for an answer: Corrib Arts Week · Sent 3 days ago … Lagan Rugby Club AGM · Sent today'; 'worth a call' only after CHASE_AFTER_DAYS (7). (`web/src/jobs/Money.tsx:148,186,212,261`) [9 waiting quotes; 0 marked worth a call on the seed (all sent within 3 days)]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4 (consistency: one state, one phrase) and 1 (status: how long it has waited); ADR 0104's own aim that the stage line, Board, Home and the job page read one record; the job-flow research's Kanban rule that an item's age is visible; design-system principles.md on wording (one word for one thing). · Kanban Guide (age of an item visible), as adopted in the 1 November job-flow research; NN/g consistency; Baymard on status wording in order flows (one status vocabulary)
- **Proposal (S):** One shared line-maker for the Selling stage that every surface reads, carrying the quote's age and 'worth a call' in the warn tone where the Board and the job page already draw the stage's hint. shared/src/steps.ts, beside STAGE_LINES: sellingLine(job, quote, today) returning 'No quote yet · N days left', 'Draft started · N days left' (with '· 1 line needs a price' when the draft has one, office-7), 'Quote with the client N days' (+ ' · worth a call' at CHASE_AFTER_DAYS, warn), 'Dates passed, no answer'; 'Waiting on the client' stays for a quoted job with no quote record. BoardScreen.tsx:131-134, StageLine.tsx's Selling words, NextDock's line and Quotes' FollowRow read it. Quotes' list and its rule are unchanged.
- **Trade-off:** The Board's quote cards grow a number; CHASE_AFTER_DAYS moves from money-view.ts to steps.ts or is imported there. **Depends on:** quote.sentOn on the record; CHASE_AFTER_DAYS exported from shared/src/sync/money-view.ts:495. **Weighed:** Leave the overview surfaces as they are and rely on Quotes and Notifications (ADR 0081): acceptable, which is why this is P3.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/jobs-board-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/job-quoted-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/quotes-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-office/jobs-board-desk-light.png`
- **Learning:** A worked-out state should have one line-maker in shared code that every surface reads, so the Board, the list, the record page and the follow-up list can't drift.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced: the Board's Selling column shows 'Waiting on the client' (3 cards), 'Quote with the client since Tue 3 Nov / Fri 6 Nov' and 'No quote yet · Still enquiry · 10 days left' side by side (BoardScreen.tsx:131-134); the job page reads 'Next · Selling · Still quoted' and the Quote sent row 'Sent Fri 6 Nov'; Quotes › To follow up says 'Sent 3 days ago' and shows 0 'worth a call' on the seed. Not fixed in 1.77-1.88. But ADR 0081 decides where follow-up lives: 'Quotes on Money … sent and waiting for an answer, with worth a call after a week', and on Notifications; the Board is read-only overview (ADR 0104) and the office follows up from Quotes, which already has the age and the nudge. 'Waiting on the client' is a distinct state (status quoted with no quote record, three seed jobs; on live it means quoted by hand or outside the app) and renaming it 'Quoted outside the app, N days' has no N to count from. The cost in use is therefore small: the office's follow-up surface is right, and only the overview surfaces lack the age. Re-rated to NN 1, P3, with the proposal trimmed to the shared line-maker and the age on the Board and the Next line. (was: "A quote waiting on a client is said four ways, with two clocks, and 'worth a call' shows on one screen only")

**Smaller findings**

- **office-5 · Fifty timesheets are approved one card at a time, with no 'approve as sent' from the row** (P3 · severity 2 · Kept by the sceptic). Crew › Timesheets › To approve (50) shows three rows then Show all; each row's only action is Check, which opens a 'Check and approve' card with the figures, a note and one button 'Approve €683.50'. A timesheet with nothing to change still costs two presses and a card. The office approving a show week's timesheets does 100+ presses with no keyboard path through the list. Not a theme: one screen, one pattern; but it is the office's biggest count on Home. *Direction:* On a row whose figures match the booking (no change from what was offered: days, rate, no expenses), offer 'Approve €290' beside Check, as the pick list offers 'Count out the N'; keep Check for anything that differs. Size S. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced: Timesheets › To approve (50) shows Check on every row for the office (Timesheets.tsx:64 `checks = mayDecide && !amountsHidden`, so Open is only for roles without rates), and the Check card ends in one 'Approve €683.50' with no approve-from-the-row. No ADR decides that every timesheet is opened before approval (ADR 0090 says the office 'check and approve', ADR 0046 only swaps Check for Open by role). Not in 1.77-1.88. P3 stands: timesheets are off until used (ADR 0090) and the 50 is seed scale; the proposal (Approve on the row when days, rate and extras match the booking) is the Default Effect used as the pick list's 'Count out the N' already is, size S honest. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/crew-timesheets-desk-light.png`
- **office-6 · Home's count and the heading it opens disagree by the running-late line** (P3 · severity 1 · Kept by the sceptic). Home says '73 answers to check' and '1 person running late, not yet noted' as two tiles; both open #crew, where the one heading reads 'Answers to check (74)' and the Crew tab's badge says 74. Every count should open the list that explains it with the same number. Rolled into office-1's proposal; listed here so it isn't lost if office-1 isn't picked. *Direction:* Either count running late inside Home's answers tile (dropping the separate tile, since Crew already lists it first) or head Crew's list 'Answers to check (73) · 1 running late'. Size S. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced: Home '73 answers to check' and '1 person running late, not yet noted' as two tiles (standing.ts:162-163, both href #crew part 'Answers to check'); Crew's heading 'Answers to check (74)' and the badge '74, 74 to check' (CrewScreen.tsx:410 sums late + docs + toSortOut + toCheck). Breaks Colly's rule that every count opens the list that explains it. Not fixed, not decided. Kept as its own S finding so it survives if office-1 isn't picked; either of the two directions is fine, the second (head Crew 'Answers to check (73) · 1 running late') keeps Home's running-late tile, which the office does act on first. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/home-office-phone-dark.png`
- **office-7 · The enquiry-to-sent-quote path is short, but the enquiry's quote starts with the kit's price missing and says so only on the quote page** (P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic). New job (name, client with Did you mean, venue, status, kind, one phase) → Add job → job page → Next 'Add the kit to quote' → Kit tab → Add kit picker: three screens and three presses plus typing, which is good. But the seed's enquiry's one kit line reaches the quote as '12 × no price a day × 1 day: needs a price' and the job page's Next line says only 'A draft is started'; the office learns the quote can't go until they open it. A quote with an unpriced line already asks before sending (1.79.0), so this is a visibility note, not a gap: the Next line could say 'Draft started · 1 line needs a price'. *Direction:* Carry 'N lines need a price' into the Selling line-maker of office-3, so the job's Next line says it; no separate change. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced by code and on the seed: a job with a draft reads 'Next · Selling · A draft is started' with 'Finish the quote' (NextDock.tsx:105-106, the stage hint from steps.ts), and only the quote page says '1 line with no price'. True and small; the sending guard (1.79.0) already stops a bad send. Folded into office-3's shared line-maker rather than its own change, as the reviewer suggested; not worth a separate pick. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/new-job-did-you-mean-desk-light.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-office/job-enquiry-quote-desk-light.png`

**What held up:** One filled red Next in one place on the job page, with 'Hand it to someone' beside it and a plain-words box when there is nothing to press (ADR 0104/0109): the same on every job the office opened. Did you mean on New job's client and venue, in one press, with 'will be added' for a new one; the New job form is one card with sensible defaults (Enquiry, Gig, one Show phase today). Answers to check bundles yes/counter/decline/pull-out/running late/documents into one list with Ring, Noted, Offer… and 'Noted: all 12 declines on calls already full' in one press; a pull-out says 'the call is 2 short' with Offer… right there. Quotes › To follow up grouped by what to do (Waiting for an answer, Dates passed with no answer with Mark the job lost, Not sent yet), and the Board's 'Dates passed, no answer (7)' fold reading the same rule. The stage line under every jobs-list row and Home row, and the Jobs Filter with Stage and Tasks, so the list, Board and Home read one record. Change handling warns ahead: To sort out lists calls short in the next three days, vehicle clashes and crew offered while booked elsewhere; the planner flags 'Crew on a day their phase moved off'; Kit short shows on the row, the Board card, Home and the Next button. Every switch that would contact someone says so where it is off ('the app sends nothing', 'Marked sent, it becomes quote 75 … send it yourself'), and the quote's What happened card says 'These are the office's records'.

### The head of department

*A department's crew and kit, on a phone, in show weeks.*

Home for a department is the one place the app is a head's: 'Needs attention in LX' counts their calls, answers, certificates, repairs and tests in their words, and every count lands on a list that says 'Showing LX only. Show all departments'. Walk off Home and the lens falls away. The Crew tab holds it (Calls and People start on LX, the badge counts LX), but the Stock tab opens Repairs, Testing, the catalogue and Kit short for the whole company with company-wide counts in its row, the Jobs list and Board have no department anywhere, the planner's and Notifications' 'To sort out' chase Riggers and Stagehands at a head of LX, and the job page says 'CREW 5 of 10 booked' and 'Book 5 more crew' when LX is 0 of 1, with the LX call fifth of seven rows. 'Waiting on me' is in four places that count different things: Home's department view hides the two leave requests waiting on them because the requesters aren't LX, the Crew row says 'Timesheets 50' for a list the page then calls 'Waiting on the office', and a counter-offer sits in their 'answers to check' with only 'Say no'. The job's journey is drawn for the office: Selling vocabulary ('No quote yet', 'Still quoted', 'Waiting on the client') on a role that is told 'Quotes are for the office and directors'. It reads as the office's app with a filter that holds on two tabs out of five.

#### head-1 · The department lens holds on Home and Crew and falls away on Stock, the Jobs list and the job page

**P1 · severity 3 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles head, warehouse · phone 390x844 dark, desk 1440x900 light (same figures) · reproduced

ADR 0046 made a head's Home and Crew › Calls start on their department, and the 5 November audit (findings 6 and 42) carried that to the badge, Answers to check and People. Nothing else narrows unless the head arrives from a Home tile: Stock's Repairs, Testing, catalogue and Kit short open company-wide from the Stock tab with company-wide counts in the Stock row, the Jobs list and Board have no department filter or figure, the planner's and Notifications' 'To sort out' list other departments' calls with 'Find crew', and a job page gives the company's crew and kit figures with the LX call buried among seven. The head's three questions ('are my people sorted, is my kit ready, is anyone waiting on me') are answered on Home and then contradicted on the next tab; the persona's rule, Limited Choice (a list of forty becomes a list of four), holds on two of five tabs. On a phone in a show week that is reading 15 faults to find 4, and seven call rows to find one.

- **Evidence:** #stock/repairs opened from the Stock tab, as head of LX, phone dark: 'All (15) · Audio (9) · Lighting (5) · Video (1)', 'Damaged, can't go out (6)', first row 'SH-000183 d&b B22-SUB' (audio). Opened from Home's '5 repairs open' tile the same page says 'Showing LX only. Show all departments', 'Damaged, can't go out (2)', first row 'SH-001344 Chroma-Q Color Force II 72'. Side by side in the screenshot. (`web/src/stock/Faults.tsx:342 useNarrowed('#stock/repairs') with no start; web/src/stock/Inspections.tsx:268; web/src/stock/StockScreen.tsx:421; against web/src/crew/CrewScreen.tsx:267 useNarrowed('#crew', myDepartment) and :212 for People; web/src/Views.tsx:64-65 Stock row counts unnarrowed vs web/src/crew/CrewViews.tsx:23 useMyDepartment`) [15 rows vs 4 rows (innerText counts); the Stock row reads 'Testing 16 · Repairs 15' on both, while Home for LX says 5 repairs and 8 can't go out] · #stock/testing from the Stock tab: 'Can't go out (16)' listing d&b M2, d&b D40, L-Acoustics Kara II among LX items; Home for LX had said '8 items can't go out until tested' and '3 inspections due in 30 days', and the tile landing says 'Showing LX only'. (`web/src/stock/Inspections.tsx:268-269`) [16 vs 8 items] · #stock (catalogue) Kit short card: 'Kit short — d&b Y10P: short 6 · Brightwater Tech Summit', 'd&b Y10P: short 6 · Harbour Lights Festival', 'Shure KSM137: short 8' (all audio) for a head of LX whose Home shows no kit-short tile because LX has none. (`web/src/stock/StockScreen.tsx:421, 441`) · #jobs list and its Filter, phone: Filter offers Status, Kind, Crew, Kit, Stage, Tasks and no department; rows read 'Planning · 5 of 10 booked · 7 lines of kit, 1 short, 2 vehicles', 'Crew 5 of 10', 'Kit short' for the company. 'Kit in the notes — 56 jobs' notes list kit that isn't on them yet. Work through them' is offered to the head. (`web/src/jobs/JobsScreen.tsx (filter groups); shared/src/steps.ts hints`) [6 filter groups, 0 for department] · #jobs/0mw4zt64edqmjaur597bq (Harbour Lights Festival) and its Crew tab, phone: Head: 'CREW 5 of 10 booked', 'KIT 7 products, 1 short'; dock 'Next · Planning · 5 of 10 booked — Book 5 more crew'. Crew tab lists Load in '2 × Rigger', '2 × Stagehand', Show '1 × Sound No.1', '1 × Monitors', '1 × LX op 0 of 1, both days · Fionn Gallagher (asked for more)', '1 × Crew chief', Load out '2 × Stagehand'. Kit tab opens on 'AUDIO' with the short line (d&b Y10P) and 'Show all 7 lines' before any lighting. (`web/src/jobs/JobScreen.tsx (facts), web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:122 label from the whole job's count; web/src/jobs/Kit.tsx grouping by stock department`) [the LX call is the 5th of 7 call rows on a 2,674 px page; the head's own figure (0 of 1 LX) appears nowhere on the page] · #jobs/plan and #notifications 'To sort out', phone: Both list 'Harbour Lights Festival 1 short · Rigger · Load in · in 3 days · Find crew' and 'Stagehand · Load in · in 3 days · Find crew', then 'Big Van Clash' and 'The Luton Check', for a head of LX. The planner's week line says 'crew found for every day'. (`web/src/jobs/PlanScreen.tsx 'To sort out' (not narrowed); web/src/Notifications (same list)`) [0 of the 5 To sort out rows concern LX]
- **Basis:** Personas H: Limited Choice ('narrowing to one department turns a list of forty into a list of four'); NN/g heuristic 4 Consistency and standards (the same role, two tabs, two rules) and 6 Recognition rather than recall; Colly's rule 'every count opens the list that explains it' (the Stock row's 16 and 15 open lists that aren't the department's 8 and 5 Home just showed); ADR 0046 §5-6 extended by audit findings 6 and 42, which stopped at the Crew tab. · NN/g, 'Filters vs. Facets' and 'Recognition vs recall'; Baymard, applied filters must be visible and one tap to clear; design-system behavioural.md Limited Choice and principles.md behaviour rule on narrowing with the way back; docs/personas.md H; ADR 0046; audit-ux-2026-11-05 findings 6 and 42.
- **Proposal (M):** Make the head's own department the starting lens of every Stock list and count, and of the job page's crew figure, exactly as Crew does, with the one existing line to widen; narrow Jobs and the planner's To sort out as a second step that keeps no-department calls in view. Step one (S): Faults.tsx:342, Inspections.tsx:268 and StockScreen.tsx:421 (Kit short) take useMyDepartment() as useNarrowed's start, mapped through narrower().kit (LX → Lighting), under the existing <Narrowed> line; Views.tsx:51-52 count Testing and Repairs through the same filter as CrewViews.tsx:23 does for Calls, so the row's number is the list's number; the job head's CREW fact reads 'LX 0 of 1 · all 5 of 10' for a head with a department, and the Crew tab lists their department's calls first under a subhead (call.department exists, ADR 0046 §1). Step two (M): a Department group in the Jobs Filter (jobs with a call or a kit line of that department) starting on the head's; the planner's To sort out narrowed with the same line but always keeping calls with no department, with a hint 'and N calls with no department', since those are the ones Home can't count.
- **Trade-off:** A head covering another department pays one 'Show all departments' tap per Stock page per visit, the cost ADR 0034 weighed; a head with no department on their card (the warehouse persona) is unchanged because useMyDepartment returns ''. The job head gains one two-figure fact for heads only. **Depends on:** Nothing in the model for step one (call.department and model.department exist). Step two needs a jobs-by-department index on the device (steps-view already walks calls and kit lines). **Weighed:** A company-wide department switch in the app bar: rejected, a new part the system doesn't have and a second place for the choice Home's Department select already makes. Narrowing Notifications' To sort out: rejected, that page is 'what others changed on what's yours' and the calls short there include ones with no department that a department filter would hide.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/repairs-from-stock-tab-vs-from-home-tile-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/direct--stock-testing.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/direct--stock.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/jobs-filter-open-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/job-crew-tab-vs-crew-calls-narrowed-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/plan-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-head/repairs-stock-tab-vs-home-tile-side-by-side-head-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** A narrowing that a role's Home chooses should be the start of every list that can take it, not only the lists whose tiles were audited; the design system could say that a 'lens' chosen on Home is remembered per tab with one line to widen.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced every instance on my own server: Stock row 'Testing 16 · Repairs 15' beside Home › LX's '8 can't go out until tested' and '5 repairs open'; #stock/repairs from the tab 'All (15) · Audio (9) · Lighting (5) · Video (1)', first row d&b B22-SUB, against 'Showing LX only' and 'Damaged, can't go out (2)' from the tile (side by side in my screenshot); #stock/testing 'Can't go out (16)' listing d&b M2, D40, Kara II; Kit short on #stock all audio; Jobs Filter has no department; the job page 'CREW 5 of 10 booked' with the LX op call the 5th of 7 rows and 'Book 5 more crew' in the dock; planner and Notifications 'To sort out' leading with Rigger and Stagehand. Code checks out: Faults.tsx:342, Inspections.tsx:268 and StockScreen.tsx:421 call useNarrowed with no start, CrewScreen.tsx:267 passes useMyDepartment(); Views.tsx:51-52 count unnarrowed. Not among the 103 (findings 6, 42 and 47 stopped at Crew and the Home tiles), and ADR 0034's rejection is of limiting what a head can change, not where their lists start: it says 'Revisit once calls carry a department', which ADR 0046 did, and the audit's batch 4 fixed Crew on exactly the 'start on theirs, one line to widen' pattern. Two adjustments: (1) the 'To sort out' instance is weaker than it looks, because the Harbour Lights load-out Stagehand calls have no department at all (ADR 0046 Consequences), so narrowing that list to LX would hide calls Home honestly says it can't count; To sort out should keep no-department calls in view (as Crew needed's 'No department' choice does) or stay as it is, and Notifications is 'what others changed on what's yours', not a department list. (2) The Jobs Filter 'Department' group and the job-page subhead are the M part and need a jobs-by-department index; the Stock lists, the Stock row counts and the CREW fact are a morning's work and carry most of the cost. Keep the theme at P1 for Stock (Home's numbers contradicted on the next tab, 15 rows to find 4 on a show-week phone) with the Jobs and planner parts as the second step. (was: "The department lens holds on Home and Crew and falls away on Stock, Jobs, the job page, the planner and Notifications")

#### head-2 · Two counts of what waits on a head don't match the list they stand for: leave hidden on Home › Department, timesheets counted on the Crew row

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles head · phone 390x844 dark, desk 1440x900 light · reproduced

A head's 'is anyone waiting on me' is answered by Home › Department and the Crew row, and the two disagree in both directions. Home's department view narrows leave by the requester's department, so Cian Murphy's two requests to Dylan (the approver) are on the Crew row and the Leave page but not on the one view that is his; the Crew row counts 'Timesheets 50' for a role whose Timesheets page says they are waiting on the office. Home's rule since 1.29.0 is that a count is only offered to someone who can act on it; the row doesn't follow it, and the leave tile narrows by the wrong owner.

- **Evidence:** #home Department view vs #crew/leave, phone dark: Home › 'Needs attention in LX' has no leave tile. Crew row: 'Leave 2'. Leave page: 'To approve (2) — Cian Murphy, Annual leave, Tue 1 Dec to Sat 5 Dec · Approve | Decline' and 'A day in lieu for Wed 28 Oct'. Cian Murphy is not LX, so the requests waiting on Dylan as an approver are left off the one view that is his. (`web/src/home/standing.ts:133 `view.leave.queue.filter((q) => by.person(q.person) && …)` narrows by the requester's department; approvers are per person (ADR 0024, canApproveLeave)`) [0 on Home, 2 on the row and page] · #crew row and #crew/timesheets as head: Row: 'Timesheets 50'. Page: 'Your role doesn't see crew rates, so the amounts are hidden, and the office checks and approves timesheets.' then 'WAITING ON THE OFFICE (50)' with 'Open' on each. Home withholds the same count for the same reason. (`web/src/crew/CrewViews.tsx:34 (toApprove counted for mayCrew) vs web/src/home/standing.ts:132 (!r.amountsHidden)`) [50 counted on the row, 0 on Home, 0 the head can act on] · bottom bar, #crew row, #home, #notifications: Bottom-bar Crew badge '10'; Crew row 'Calls 8'; Home 'answers to check 8'; Notifications badge '19', its 'To sort out' leading with 'Harbour Lights Festival 1 short · Rigger … Find crew', 'Stagehand … Find crew', 'Big Van Clash'. (`web/src/crew/CrewBadge.tsx:29; web/src/crew/CrewViews.tsx:23-35; web/src/home/standing.ts:119-197, 209-211 (tasks tiles only on 'company')`) [10 / 8 / 8 / 19 for 'what needs me' on four surfaces of one screen set] · #crew Answers to check (LX), phone: 'Fionn Gallagher asks for a different rate · Harbour Lights Festival · LX op · “Two long days; can you do 320?” · The office agrees rates: your role doesn't see them. · Say no'. The counter-offer is in the head's 8 answers to check and in their Home count, but the only button is Say no; nothing hands it to the office or says the office has it. (`web/src/crew/CrewScreen.tsx:525`) [1 of the 8 'answers to check' can't be checked by the person counted at]
- **Basis:** Colly's rules 'every count opens the list that explains it' and 'a button that can't work for your role isn't offered' (a count is a promise of a list to act on); NN/g heuristic 1 Visibility of system status and 4 Consistency; design-system behavioural.md Time Scarcity and the notification-list part (one list of what needs you, counted once); personas.md H ('1 leave request waiting on them' on Home for Audio is the stated design). · NN/g 'Indicators, Validations, and Notifications' (one count per thing, the count opens the thing); GOV.UK 'task list' pattern (one list of what is yours, with its state); design-system components.md notification list and behavioural.md Time Scarcity; ADR 0024 (leave approvers are people), ADR 0046 §10 (a role without rates can't agree one).
- **Proposal (S):** Count what waits on a head by who owns the decision, the same on Home › Department and the Crew row. standing.ts:133: in the department view, count leave by approver, not requester (drop by.person for the leave tile; the tile's label 'leave requests waiting on you' already says so, and the department heading can carry it as 'waiting on you' in the row's words). CrewViews.tsx:38: count Timesheets as standing.ts:131 does, leaving out rows with amountsHidden, so a head's row shows no count where the page says 'Waiting on the office' (keep 'Not in yet' with Ask, which they can do, uncounted as today).
- **Trade-off:** The leave tile on Home › Department stops being 'in LX' for that one row, which is right because approval is per person; nothing else moves. **Depends on:** Nothing. **Weighed:** A 'Waiting on you' card on Home › Department holding leave, answers, tasks, votes and counters (the reviewer's): rejected as a new part duplicating Me's card and the tiles for what two count fixes remove. Dropping the Leave count from the row: rejected, it is right.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/direct--crew-leave.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/direct--crew-timesheets.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/direct--notifications.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/direct--crew.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-head/home-department-vs-leave-page-side-by-side-head-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** A count narrowed by a department must narrow by the thing's own owner where the thing is owned by a person (leave by approver, tasks by owner), not by the requester's department.
- **Sceptic:** Two of the four disagreements are real and reproduced; two are not disagreements. Real: (a) Home › Department shows no leave tile while the Crew row says 'Leave 2' and #crew/leave lists Cian Murphy (Transport, madeup.ts:175) twice under 'To approve (2)' with Approve | Decline: standing.ts:133 narrows the leave queue by the requester's department, though approvers are a per-person flag (ADR 0024) and the audit's own sceptic on finding 28 ruled that leave is not narrowed by department, so the one tile whose label says 'waiting on you' is the one that hides what waits on them. (b) The Crew row says 'Timesheets 50' for a head whose page then says 'Your role doesn't see crew rates … the office checks and approves timesheets' and 'WAITING ON THE OFFICE (50)': 1.29.0 withheld that count on Home ('a head no longer sees timesheets … that only the office decides') but CrewViews.tsx:38 still counts toApprove.length for anyone with the crew ability. Not disagreements: the bottom-bar badge 10 is exactly the row's Calls 8 + Leave 2 (CrewBadge.tsx:32 sums the same two functions), and Notifications' 19 is the bell's count of changes, which the page says in its first line is a different question. The counter-offer is head-f1. The reviewer's 'one Waiting on you card' is a new part on Home › Department that duplicates Me's card and the tiles, about 300 px above the charts, for two count fixes; the reviewer's own alternative (fix the counts, S) is the smallest change that removes the incoherence, so I take that. Rated 2: each is a wrong number a head meets daily, not a blocked task. (was: "'Is anyone waiting on me?' has no one answer: four places count four different things")

#### head-3 · A quote the role isn't sent is said as missing on the Jobs list, the Board and the job's steps

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles head, staff crew · phone 390x844 dark, desk 1440x900 light · reproduced

For a head, every enquiry's stage line reads 'No quote yet · Still enquiry', including the five whose quote the office has drafted; the director's Board says 'A draft is started' on those. The hint is derived from the quotes on the device, and a head's device holds none (ADR 0042/0046), so the gap between hidden and missing, which the fields close with rateHidden and linkHidden, leaks through the derived words on three screens. A head then chases the office for a quote that exists, or reads the Selling column as the office doing nothing.

- **Evidence:** #jobs and #jobs/board as head, phone: List rows: 'Selling · Still quoted · Quoted', 'Selling · No quote yet · Still enquiry · Enquiry'. Board Selling column (14): 'Waiting on the client' ×5, 'No quote yet · Still enquiry · N days left' ×9. #quotes for the same role says 'Quotes are for the office and directors.' (`shared/src/steps.ts stage hints read quotes the role isn't sent (ADR 0042) and say 'No quote yet'; web/src/jobs/BoardScreen.tsx; web/src/jobs/StageLine.tsx`) [9 'No quote yet' rows for the head against 3 for the director on the same data (map-roles, director-board vs head-board)] · #jobs/0mw4zt64edqmjaur597bq head, phone: 'Where it is — In Planning · 2 of 8 tasks done', dots 'Selling ✓ · 2 Planning · 3 Going out · 4 On the job · 5 Back'; 'Crew booked 5 of 10 booked · Next'; 'Kit and vans settled 7 lines of kit, 1 short, 2 vehicles'; dock 'Next · Planning · 5 of 10 booked — Book 5 more crew · Hand it to someone'. LX on this job: 1 × LX op, 0 of 1. (`web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:122 `Book ${short} more crew` from steps.current.hint; web/src/jobs/Steps.tsx task rows`) [0 mentions of LX in the head, the Where it is card or the dock; the head's figure needs the Crew tab, 5th row] · #jobs list foot, head: 'Kit in the notes — 56 jobs' notes list kit that isn't on them yet. Work through them': an office tidy-up offered on the head's list. (`web/src/jobs/JobsScreen.tsx (Kit in the notes card gated on kit ability, not job)`)
- **Basis:** ADR 0046 §8 ('a screen says it's hidden rather than missing'); ADR 0073 (heads plan crew and kit: their step should be legible as theirs); job-flow research 1 Nov (the stage model is one for everyone, but each role reads their own task first); NN/g heuristic 2 Match between system and the real world (a head's words are calls and kit, not quotes) and 8 Aesthetic and minimalist design. · ADR 0042/0046 on what a role reads and how a hidden thing is said; job-flow research 2026-11-01 (one model, role-specific task reading); NN/g 'Match between system and the real world'; design-system principles.md plain words in the crew's own vocabulary.
- **Proposal (S):** Let the stage hint say the quote is with the office when the role isn't sent quotes, rather than that there is none. steps-view.ts:195: when the device's reading leaves money out (view.reading, as Narrowed.tsx reads it), the quote task's hint is 'Quote: with the office' in the quiet tone on the Jobs list row, the Board card and the job's Where it is, in place of 'No quote yet' / 'A draft is started'; 'Still enquiry · N days left' and 'Waiting on the client' stay, since they come from the job's own status and dates.
- **Trade-off:** One more branch in a shared hint; a head with no quotes drawn can't tell a drafted quote from none, which is the decision ADR 0062 made. **Depends on:** Nothing: view.reading is on the device. **Weighed:** Hiding the quote task from the steps for roles without money: rejected, ADR 0104's eight tasks are one map for everyone and the head still needs to see the stage.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/job-head-next-vs-jobs-list-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/job-head-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/jobs-list-phone-dark-full.png`
- **Learning:** When a role isn't sent a record, every derived hint must say 'with the office' rather than 'none yet': the gap between hidden and missing leaks through derived words, not only fields.
- **Sceptic:** The hidden-read-as-missing part is reproduced and solid: on the Board as head, 'No quote yet' appears 9 times and 'A draft is started' 0; as director on the same data 'No quote yet' 3 and 'A draft is started' 5, so five jobs whose quote the office has started tell a head there is none, which ADR 0046 §8 rules out in words ('a screen says it's hidden rather than missing'). steps-view.ts:195 builds the hint from the quotes the device holds without knowing the role isn't sent any. Not among the 103 (finding 18 is about dates passed). The 'own figure' part is reproduced (0 LX on the job head, 'Book 5 more crew') but is the same instance as head-1's job-page evidence, so I leave it there to avoid two themes for one change. The 'Kit in the notes' instance is dropped: it is gated on the kit ability (NotesKit.tsx:80) and heads put kit on jobs (ADR 0034), so it is theirs to do, if company-wide. The Selling stage and its vocabulary on a head's screens is the shared five-stage map the research and ADR 0104 chose, and the reviewer keeps it; only the derived words need to change. Size S, not M: one branch in steps-view keyed on view.reading, which Narrowed.tsx already reads. (was: "The job's journey is drawn in the office's words and figures for a head, whose own share has no line of its own")

#### head-x1 · A head's department has two names on two tabs: LX on Crew and Home, Lighting on Stock

**P3 · severity 1 · Added by the sceptic** · roles head · phone 390x844 dark, desk 1440x900 light · reproduced

Home says 'Needs attention in LX' and Crew narrows to 'LX'; the same head's kit on Stock is 'Lighting (5)' in the Repairs chips and the catalogue's 'Lighting (62)', and the narrowed Repairs page says 'Showing LX only' over a list the chips would call Lighting. The mapping (LX → Lighting, LED → Video, Rigger → Rigging) lives in narrower().kit and nowhere a head can read. A head knows both words, so it is a moment's doubt rather than a wrong turn, but it is the same department named two ways on the two tabs that are theirs, and the purchase list uses a third list (audit, 5 November, 'the department list is the crew's, not the stock's').

- **Evidence:** #stock/repairs opened from Home's '5 repairs open' tile, head of LX, phone dark: 'Showing LX only. Show all departments' over Chroma-Q, GLP, Claypaky, Molefay, Chauvet rows; opened from the tab the same kit sits under a 'Lighting (5)' chip. (`web/src/home/standing.ts narrower().kit; web/src/Narrowed.tsx:40-46 (inKitOf) and the Narrowed line's wording; web/src/stock/Faults.tsx:347-350 (chips by stock department)`) · #home Department view vs #stock catalogue, head of LX: Home's select lists Audio, Backline, LED Tech, LX, Production, Rigger…; the catalogue's chips list Audio, Lighting, Video and '2 more'. [9 crew departments on Home's select against 5 stock departments on the catalogue chips (innerText counts)]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4 Consistency and standards (one thing, one name) and 2 Match between system and the real world; design-system principles.md on plain words in the crew's own vocabulary. · NN/g 'Consistency and standards'; design-system principles.md vocabulary rule.
- **Proposal (S):** Say both names where the stock lists are narrowed to a crew department, so the mapping is visible once. The Narrowed line on Stock pages reads 'Showing LX (Lighting) only. Show all departments' when the crew department's stock name differs (Narrowed.tsx, a kit flag from the caller), and nothing changes on Crew, where the two agree.
- **Trade-off:** Three more words on one line; the two department lists stay separate, since ADR 0025's crew departments and the stock list's are different things. **Depends on:** Nothing. **Weighed:** One shared department list for crew and stock: rejected, a crew department (Rigger, Production) is not a stock one and ADR 0025/0046 keep them apart.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-head/repairs-stock-tab-vs-home-tile-side-by-side-head-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** Where one person's lens maps onto two vocabularies (their crew department and its stock department), the narrowing line should say both names once.
- **Sceptic:** added by the sceptic

**Smaller findings**

- **head-f2 · The Stock row's counts and the Crew row's counts follow different rules for the same role** (P2 · severity 2 · Kept by the sceptic). On one screen set a head's Crew row reads 'Calls 8' (LX) and the Stock row 'Testing 16 · Repairs 15' (company). Both are 'things to do' counts on tab rows; one is the department's and one isn't. It is an instance of head-1, listed here because it is a one-line fix worth doing on its own. *Direction:* Count the Stock row's Testing and Repairs through narrower(useMyDepartment()).kit as the Crew row counts through useMyDepartment, and open each on the narrowed list. Size S. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced on phone and desk: the Stock row reads 'Testing 16 · Repairs 15' beside the Crew row's 'Calls 8 · Leave 2', while Home › LX says 8 and 5 and the Repairs page opened from the tile lists 5. Views.tsx:51-52 count view.inspections.attention and view.faults.open unnarrowed; CrewViews.tsx:23-24 count through useMyDepartment(). It is head-1's first step, and worth its own line because it is a morning's work on two lines and breaks 'every count opens the list that explains it' on every Stock page a head opens. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/direct--stock-testing.png`
- **head-f1 · A counter-offer in a head's list says who agrees rates but not that the office already has it** (P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic). Fionn Gallagher's 'can you do 320?' on the head's one short LX call shows in the head's Answers to check with 'The office agrees rates: your role doesn't see them. Say no'. The head can refuse but not accept or ask the office, and nothing shows the office is on it; the call stays '0 of 1' on their Home. ADR 0046 §10 decided the head can't agree a rate; it didn't decide there is no hand-off. *Direction:* Change the hint under the counter-offer to say it is in the office's answers to check, in the quiet tone, keeping Say no (ADR 0046 §10). No share panel and no new count. Size S. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced word for word in Answers to check (LX): 'The office agrees rates: your role doesn't see them. Say no'. But ADR 0046 §10 decided this screen exactly ('a counter-offer shows only Say no'), and the hand-off the reviewer wants already exists on the office's side: answersToCheck puts the same counter in the office's and director's Answers to check with 'Agree €320', so the office is not told nothing. What is missing is one sentence telling the head that. The proposed share panel is a second channel for a thing already in the office's list, and 'rates to agree' on the office's Home would count it twice. The smallest change is wording: 'The office agrees rates and has this in its answers to check; your role doesn't see them.' A 1: a moment's doubt, with the office already holding it. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-head/direct--crew.png`

**What held up:** Home › Department: 'Needs attention in LX' in the head's words, split 'The next fortnight' / 'Housekeeping', with the honest hint about the uncounted call with no department; every tile lands on a list that says 'Showing LX only. Show all departments' (all six tiles checked, phone and desk). Crew › Calls and People start on LX with the badge counting LX (audit findings 6 and 42 hold), and 'Showing LX only. Show all departments' is the one wording everywhere it appears. The one Next dock in one place with a verb and its object, and 'Hand it to someone' beside it; what a head can't do is said in words ('The office agrees rates: your role doesn't see them') rather than greyed. The Leave page puts 'To approve (2)' first with the requester's note and the clash ('Maeve Doyle is off Mon 30 Nov to Tue 8 Dec') beside Approve | Decline.

**Dropped by the sceptic:** head-f3 (A person outside the head's department loses 'Their link' with no line saying why: Reproduced (Ben Moran, Transport: tabs 'Coming up | Details | Documents | Worked', no 'Their link'; Eoin Collins, LX: 'Their link' drawn), but it is a decision the audit already made: finding 94(f) said a tab holding only 'your role doesn't see it' should be left off, and PersonScreen.tsx:75 does that, citing it. ADR 0046 §8 is about fields on a record a role is sent; the link is left out of their reading altogether (ADR 0062), so the tab's absence is the 'hidden, not missing' rule applied to a whole part, not half-kept. Re-reporting it would reverse a fix from this week.)

### The warehouse

*Picking, scanning, faults and counts on the floor.*

Walked as the Warehouse persona (a head of department, ADR 0051) on a 390x844 dark phone with touch, offline mid-pick, and once at a 1440 desk, against the template company (300 jobs). The floor's tools themselves hold up well: Home on Warehouse puts Going out first with '0 of 27 out' straight into the pick list; the pick list head carries the real deadline ('In 3 days: Load in, Mon 9 Nov at Riverside Park') and the van and driver; a scan answers in one line with a 65x64 Undo; Report damage sits inline on the scan answer; a fault reported there is on Repairs and in the job's 'Checked and deprepped' hint at once; offline the bar says 'No signal · 2 waiting' and the scans held through a trip to Home and back. What makes the warehouse's app feel like several apps is everything around that core. The Stock tab is the data model, not the day: Catalogue first (the day's Pick lists card starts 1,126px down), a nine-page row that scrolls sideways (871px of links in 356px), and the 'On this screen' strip empty. The job page's one Next is the office's: a job going out tomorrow with 0 of 27 out says 'Settle the kit and vans', and the pick list is only reachable through the Kit and vans tab (3 taps from Jobs), while the pick list itself has no Next or stage line. The week's housekeeping is nine tiles below Home's fold, with counts on only three of Stock's nine row pages and one list named four ways. Finding an item by number tells you where it is kept but not that it is out or broken.

#### warehouse-1 · Stock opens on the catalogue with the day's work a screen and a half down, and the strip that was meant to jump there is gone

**P2 · severity 3 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles W, H · phone 390x844 dark, desk 1440x900 dark · reproduced

Home › Warehouse puts Going out first, but the Stock tab, offered on every screen, opens on the Catalogue with the Pick lists card at y=1,179 and Kit short at y=2,324 on a phone. ADR 0033 and personas.md say Stock keeps this order because 'its strip jumps straight to Pick lists or Faults'; that strip is hidden today, because OnThisScreen draws nothing under three parts and the catalogue card has no heading while Faults became the Repairs page (ADR 0041). The decision stands on a mitigation the code no longer has, so on a load-out morning a person who taps Stock scrolls past 209 products to reach today's list, against Time Scarcity.

- **Evidence:** #home (Show for: Warehouse): Cards in order: 'Going out (5)' (rows 'Moy Film Conference · Tomorrow · Sat 7 Nov · 0 of 27 out' → #jobs/<id>/pick), 'Needs attention' (9 tiles), 'Kit booked out, the next fortnight', 'Still out after the job (3)'. The first row sits at y=407, under the fold. (`web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:540-599`) [Going out heading y=365, first row y=407 (Playwright getBoundingClientRect, 390x844, dpr 2); page 2,424px tall.] · #stock: h1 'Catalogue'. First card: Scan, 'Find a product, number or serial', chips 'All (209) | Audio (105) | Lighting (62) | Video (40) | 2 more', sort and Filter, the windowed list. 'Pick lists' heading at y=1,126; 'GOING OUT IN THE NEXT TWO WEEKS' y=1,168; 'STILL OUT AFTER THE JOB' y=1,947; 'Kit short' y=2,271. The nav labelled 'On this screen' had no entries 1.2s after load. (`web/src/stock/StockScreen.tsx:145 (heading='Catalogue'), :483-500 (Pick lists card after the catalogue); web/src/Views.tsx:48-70 (StockViews, nine pages)`) [Headings' y by getBoundingClientRect at scroll 0; page 3,042px tall. Stock row: scrollWidth 871px in clientWidth 356px, overflow-x auto (nine links 69-122px wide, 56px high).] · #stock (scrolled to Pick lists): The same list Home shows first, under Stock's own name 'Pick lists', with the sub-heading 'Going out in the next two weeks' where Home says 'Going out' and limits to seven days (HomeScreen.tsx:543). (`web/src/stock/StockScreen.tsx:483-500; web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:543`) · #menu: Stock group lines: 'Stock — The catalogue, and kit going out and coming back', 'Places — Where kit is kept', 'Labels', 'Counting', 'Testing', 'Repairs — Faults and missing kit to sort out', 'Racks', 'Vehicles', 'Purchase list'. The line promising 'kit going out and coming back' opens the Catalogue. (`web/src/MenuScreen.tsx:97-103`) · docs/personas.md:118-124: The personas page records the gap ('the Stock tab put the catalogue first and the day's work below it, with no way to jump, 3 on a load-out morning') and says Home fixes it while 'Stock keeps its order, and its strip jumps straight to Pick lists or Faults'. The strip was empty in this walk, so the jump is not there.
- **Basis:** Design system behavioural.md Time Scarcity ('puts the next thing to do first'); principles rule that healthy is quiet and the next thing goes first; NN/g Match between system and the real world (the tab named for going out and coming back opens a catalogue) and Flexibility and efficiency (a long sideways row for nine pages); personas.md W 'What fell short' rated 3. · NN/g ten heuristics (match, flexibility); Coglode Time Scarcity as adapted in design-system behavioural.md; sess-sys personas.md W; GOV.UK 'start with user needs' ordering of a page by the task.
- **Proposal (S):** Put back the jump the ADR relies on, then let the day's cards rise above the catalogue list without changing Stock's row, names or addresses. Step 1 (S, an hour): give the catalogue card a heading ('Catalogue (209)') so OnThisScreen finds three parts and the strip returns as 'Catalogue 209 · Pick lists · Kit short', the same part every other long screen has; update personas.md W 'Now' to name Pick lists and Kit short rather than Faults. Step 2 (S, a day, for Colly to weigh against ADR 0033's 'Stock keeps its order'): on #stock keep Scan and 'Find a product, number or serial' first, then draw PickLists and ShortKit, then the chips, Sort and Filter and the windowed list (StockScreen.tsx Catalogue(): StockRest before the list part of StockCard). Finding by number stays one tap for everyone; the h1 stays 'Catalogue'; the row, the Menu and every address stay as they are.
- **Trade-off:** Step 2 moves the office's product browsing one swipe down on a phone (on a desk the Beside layout already puts Pick lists at x=734, y=249, so nothing moves there); the catalogue's heading adds one line to the page. **Depends on:** Nothing for step 1. Step 2 needs a line in ADR 0033 (or the W entry in personas.md) recording the reversed order and why. **Weighed:** The reviewer's 'Today' row page with the catalogue moved to #stock/catalogue and a Housekeeping page: closest to ADR 0033's rejected 'tab for each part', needs redirects, a Menu rename and the warehouse-3 regrouping, M-L for the same first-screen result. Leaving Stock as it is because Home › Warehouse is the morning's screen: true for someone who starts on Home, but the Stock tab says 'kit going out and coming back' on the Menu and is where a person who is already in the app goes.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/home-warehouse-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/stock-catalogue-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/stock-picklists-card-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-warehouse/stock-catalogue-phone-dark.png`
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced on my own server (head, 390x844 dark): #stock opens on h1 'Catalogue', the Pick lists heading sits at y=1,179 and Kit short at y=2,324 on a 3,095px page, the row is 871px in 356px, and the 'On this screen' strip is hidden (hidden=true). The strip is not a timing fluke: OnThisScreen.tsx counts cards with an h2 under '.app > section.card > h2' and draws nothing under FEWEST=3; the catalogue card has no h2 and Faults moved to its own Repairs page (ADR 0041), so Stock has two parts and the strip the W persona entry and ADR 0033 rely on ('its strip jumps straight to Pick lists or Faults') no longer exists. So the W gap personas.md rates 3 is open again on the Stock tab, though Home › Warehouse (the ADR's main answer) holds up, which keeps this P2 not P1. The reviewer's proposal is too large and re-opens what ADR 0033 rejected ('a tab for each part of an area: Catalogue, Pick lists, Faults…'); it also moves #stock to a redirect and renames the Menu. The smallest change that removes the incoherence restores the mitigation the ADR chose and, as a second step Colly can weigh, puts the day's cards above the catalogue list. Not among the 103 (finding 79 covered the name 'Catalogue' and was settled by the rename; finding 69 added 'can't go out' to product rows). (was: "Stock is laid out as the data model, so the warehouse's day lives only on Home")

#### warehouse-2 · On a job the one Next is the office's next, and the pick list is two taps below the fold with no Next of its own

**P2 · severity 3 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles W, H · phone 390x844 dark, desk 1440x900 dark · reproduced

ADR 0104 gives the job one Next and ADR 0109 makes it true, but for a confirmed job going out tomorrow with 0 of 27 out the warehouse reads the planner's verb ('Settle the kit and vans') and finds no way to the pick list on the job page: it is on the Kit and vans tab at y=1,070, three taps from the Jobs list where Home is one. The pick list itself, which the Next rules name as the dock's second place, has no stage line and no dock, so when the last item is scanned the screen says 'ready to load' and stops. The job's position is therefore one thing on Home and the job page and nothing on the screen the warehouse works in.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/v1-job-00074 (Moy Film Conference, confirmed, goes out tomorrow, 0 of 27 out): Foot dock: 'Next · Planning · 1 line of kit' / filled 'Settle the kit and vans' / quiet 'Hand it to someone'. Where it is: 'In Planning · 3 of 8 tasks done … Crew booked · All 2 crew booked · Kit and vans settled · 1 line of kit · Next'. No link to the pick list on the page; tab row 'Phases 1 | Crew 2/2 | Kit and vans | Show pack' at y=757. (`shared/src/sync/steps-view.ts:325-327 (stage's first open task before the early pick); web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:131-133 ('Pick the kit' only on case prep)`) [Dock buttons at y=713 in an 844 viewport; tabs y=757; no element with href ending /pick on the page (querySelectorAll('a[href*="/pick"]') → 0). Taps from Jobs list to the pick list: 3 (row, Kit and vans tab, 'Pick list' link). From Home › Warehouse: 1.] · #jobs/v1-job-00074/kit: Kit tab: 'Kit · Pick list 27 to go out · Kit held Fri 6 Nov to Sun 8 Nov…' and 'Vehicles · Nothing here yet. Book a van for…'. The pick list link lives here only. (`web/src/jobs/Kit.tsx`) · #jobs/<id>/pick (Harbour Lights Festival): Head: 'Pick list · Confirmed · Harbour Lights Festival · Mon 9 Nov to Fri 13 Nov · Going out | Coming back · 0 of 122 out · In 3 days: Load in, Mon 9 Nov at Riverside Park · Going in: Big Van (241-XX-101), Cian Murphy driving'. No stage strip, no Next dock; foot is the five tabs only (75px). (`web/src/jobs/PickScreen.tsx:237-256 (head card), :446 ('All N out: ready to load.')`) [footer innerText = 'Home Jobs Stock Crew 74 Menu', height 75px; on the job page the same footer is 164px with the dock.] · #jobs (list, as the warehouse): Row: 'Harbour Lights Festival · Shannonside Festivals · Riverside Park · Mon 9 Nov to Fri 13 Nov · Load in, Show, Load out · Going out · 6 of 122 out · 2 tasks left open from before · Confirmed · Crew 5 of 10 · Kit short'. Six facts, two of them the warehouse's. (`web/src/jobs/JobsScreen.tsx:432-464; web/src/jobs/StageLine.tsx:13-33`) · research job-flow-research-2026-11-01.md, 'Rules for the Next button' bullet 1; map-flow.json inconsistencies[0]: The agreed basis names the pick list as the second place for the Next dock; the flow mapper recorded that the pick list and Home's warehouse rows carry no stage.
- **Basis:** ADR 0104 (one Next) and ADR 0109 (Next tells the truth) as they cost the warehouse in use: the truth for the warehouse on a job going out tomorrow is the pick. Research 'Rules for the Next button' (the pick list is the second place for the dock). Design system Time Scarcity (the next thing first; keep the safe path the short one) and Goal Gradient (the reward for scanning the last item is seeing what comes next). NN/g Visibility of system status and Recognition over recall (the pick list behind a tab the person must remember). · sess-sys job-flow research 1 Nov 2026 (Next button rules; field-service apps put the technician's next step on the work order itself); NN/g Visibility of system status, Recognition rather than recall; Coglode Goal Gradient and Time Scarcity via design-system behavioural.md.
- **Proposal (M):** Keep one Next per job, but put the pick list one tap from the job head and give the pick list the same dock and stage words, with picking offered as the dock's quiet second link while the planner's task is still open. (1) Job head: the KIT fact becomes a link, 'Kit · 1 product · 0 of 27 out' → #jobs/<id>/pick, on every job with kit, phone and desk (the head's dl, JobScreen). (2) NextDock on the job page: while the current task is kit (Planning) and prep is open with kit to pick and the job's span starts within Home's seven-day window, the quiet second slot (the research's 'quiet second choice as a link') reads 'Pick the kit · 0 of 27 out' for anyone with the stock ability, beside or in place of 'Hand it to someone' for that case; the filled button and the task line stay the office's, so ADR 0104 and 0109 hold. (3) Pick list: draw NextDock (fixed above the bar on a phone, in the head on a desk, as the job page does) with the StageLine words: Going out 'Pick the kit · 6 of 122 out'; all out 'All 122 out: ready to load. On the job from Mon 9 Nov' with nothing to press and why; Coming back 'Scan the kit back · 2 still out'; all back 'Tick off the deprep' (the existing task) with the open faults count. The sticky scan box stays; the pick list's foot grows from 75 to 166px as the job page's does.
- **Trade-off:** The pick list loses 91px of a phone screen to the dock; on a phone the dock row carries two quiet links in one case, so the wording must stay short. The warehouse still reads 'Settle the kit and vans' as the filled verb on a job going out tomorrow until the office ticks it, which is ADR 0109's decision; this proposal makes the pick the visible second choice rather than changing the task. **Depends on:** Nothing in the data. A line in ADR 0104's record that the dock is drawn on the pick list, which the research already asks for. **Weighed:** The reviewer's per-role Next (prep before the stage's open tasks for stock roles within seven days): removes the planner's verb for the warehouse but makes the Where it is card show two Nexts and amends ADR 0104/0109; weighed and preferred the quiet link. Ticking Kit and vans itself within seven days: against ADR 0109 ('ticked by hand only while a job is being planned'). Only the head link (1) without the dock (3): S, but leaves the pick list a dead end after the last scan.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/job-page-warehouse-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/job-kit-and-vans-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/picklist-harbour-top-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/jobs-list-warehouse-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-warehouse/job-page-head-phone-dark.png`
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced: Moy Film Conference (confirmed, goes out tomorrow, 0 of 27 out) as the head shows the dock 'Next · Planning · 1 line of kit · Settle the kit and vans · Hand it to someone' (footer 166px), zero a[href*=/pick] on the job page, tabs at y=770, and on the Kit and vans tab the 'Pick list' link is at y=1,070, under the fold even there; the pick list's footer is the five tabs only (75px) with no stage line; its Going out head stops at 'ready to load' with nothing after. steps-view.ts current() takes the stage's open tasks before prep, as cited. The research's Next rules say 'One part, NextDock, used only on the job page and the pick list', so the missing dock on the pick list is a gap against the agreed basis, not taste. But the reviewer's part (1), a different Next task per role, goes further than ADR 0104's one Next and ADR 0109 (which decided Kit and vans is ticked by hand while in Planning) and makes two people read two Nexts on one job; the research already gives the dock 'a quiet second choice as a link', which carries the pick without changing the task. Part (4) hides 'Crew 5 of 10' from heads, who book their department's crew, so it is dropped. From Home the pick list is one tap and the audit held that up, so P2 rather than P1; nn 3 on the morning (2 at a desk). (was: "On a job the one Next is the office's next, and the pick list is two taps below the fold with no Next of its own")

#### warehouse-4 · Finding an item by number says where it is kept, not that it is out or broken

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles W, H, S · phone 390x844 dark · reproduced

The warehouse finds an item by number to answer 'where is it and can it go?'. The catalogue's search row for a number says only the product and the shelf ('SH-000553 · d&b D40 · Bay C1' for an item damaged since July, 'SH-000905 · Martin MAC Aura XB · Bay C3' for one out with a job since 14 Oct), while the product's Items tab draws the same row with 'Damaged, can't go out' or 'PAT failed on …' in the bad flag, and a place's page lists bare numbers. Out with a job is said on no row, only on the item's page. So a quick look on the floor gives an answer that looks right and is wrong, and the same item is drawn two ways on two Stock screens.

- **Evidence:** #stock, search 'SH-000008' (out with Autumn Gala since 28 Oct): One row 'SH-000008 d&b Y10P Bay A1'; nothing says it is out. (`web/src/stock/StockScreen.tsx (item rows in the catalogue search); web/src/stock/common.tsx whereLabel`) · #stock, search 'SH-000007' (damaged in this walk, 'can't go out'): Row 'SH-000007 d&b Y10P Bay A1'; the item page the row opens says 'FAULT Damaged, can't go out: Grille dented on the front' and under Faults 'Back from Autumn Gala, reported today at 13:49'. (`web/src/stock/ItemScreen.tsx:75`) · #jobs/<id>/pick (Coming back) and #stock/testing: The same items with their state: 'SH-000007 Back Missing' per row; Testing rows 'SH-001048 Chauvet Maverick MK2 Spot · PAT failed on 20 Oct 2025'. (`web/src/jobs/PickScreen.tsx:749-760; web/src/stock/Inspections.tsx`)
- **Basis:** Design system states.md 'Values that are not plainly true' (a value the tool knows is not plainly true is said in the row, never left blank); NN/g Visibility of system status; personas.md W 'finding an item by number'. · Design-system states.md (unknown is never blank; a worked-out value says its reason); NN/g Visibility of system status; Baymard on search results carrying availability in the row.
- **Proposal (S):** Share the Items tab's row everywhere an item is listed, and add 'Out with <job>' to it. Lift Items' row (ProductScreen.tsx:538-551: number, whereLabel, serial, faults.stopping and inspections.blocks as bad flags, Pending) into stock/common.tsx as ItemRow and use it in the catalogue's 'Items found' list (StockScreen.tsx:347) and the scanner's last-read line (:309); add one plain line before the place when the item is out: 'Out with Liffey Conferences Hackathon since 14 Oct · Bay C3' (the item page's own words, no flag: out is healthy) and 'Missing' with the bad flag when a missing fault is open. A place's folded product lines keep their numbers but each number's chip takes the bad flag when it can't go out.
- **Trade-off:** Rows with a state grow by a line; the windowed product list is untouched. **Depends on:** Nothing; view.warehouse already carries each item's out job and open fault. **Weighed:** Only the fault flag on search rows: half the question. Only opening the item on an exact match (f3): fixes the number search, leaves the place page and the scanner's last-read line blind.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/stock-find-still-out-item-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/stock-item-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/picklist-coming-back-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-warehouse/stock-search-fault-item-phone-dark.png`
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced with the template's own data, not a fault made in the walk: searching 'SH-000553' (damaged since 31 Jul, 'Pan motor noisy') gives the row 'SH-000553 d&b D40 Bay C1 · Serial DXB843074' and searching 'SH-000905' (out with Liffey Conferences Hackathon since 14 Oct) gives 'SH-000905 Martin MAC Aura XB Bay C3 · Serial…'; each item's page says 'FAULT Damaged, can't go out' or 'OUT With … since 14 Oct'. The pattern is real and sharper than the reviewer put it: the product's Items tab (ProductScreen.tsx:544-547) already draws the same item row with 'Damaged, can't go out' and 'PAT failed on …' in the bad flag, while the catalogue search row (StockScreen.tsx:347-356) draws it from whereLabel only, and a place's page lists bare numbers; 'out with a job' is said on no row at all. Finding 69 (fixed in 1.85) covered product rows, not item rows, so this is new. The reviewer's shared ItemRow is the right part but the Items tab's row is the one to share, which makes it smaller than drawn; opening the item on an exact match is a separate behaviour, moved to finding f3. (was: "Finding an item by number says where it is kept, not that it is out or broken")

#### warehouse-3 · Stock's row counts three of the warehouse's lists and Home counts five, and the repairs list has three names

**P3 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles W, H, D · phone 390x844 dark, desk 1440x900 dark · reproduced

Home › Warehouse's tiles are the one list of the week's housekeeping, each opening the list that explains it. Stock's row, which the warehouse sees on every Stock page, carries counts on Testing (16), Repairs (15) and Vehicles but not Counting, though Home says '12 places to count this week', so the row and the tiles disagree about what needs someone. The same repairs list is 'Repairs' on the row and h1, 'Faults and repairs' on its card and 'Repairs — Faults and missing kit to sort out' on the Menu.

- **Evidence:** #home (Warehouse): Tiles: '1 job to pick for today or tomorrow', '3 jobs with kit still out after them', '3 kit lines short for confirmed jobs', '16 items can't go out until tested', '15 repairs open', '6 damaged items can't go out', '12 places to count this week', '4 inspections due in 30 days', '5 vehicle checks running out'. 'Needs attention' heading at y=827; the Going out list above it shows 'Moy Film Conference · Tomorrow'. (`web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:574 (Tiles keys TILES_FOR.warehouse); web/src/home/standing.ts:161-194`) [Heading y=827 of 844 (getBoundingClientRect); nine tiles; two of them ('1 job to pick…', '3 jobs with kit still out…') restate the two lists on the same screen.] · #stock row (every Stock page): 'Catalogue | Places | Labels | Counting | Testing 16 | Repairs 15 | Racks | Vehicles | Purchase list' (Vehicles shows a count only when a check has run out; here none). Counting has 12 places due this week and no count. (`web/src/Views.tsx:50-54 (tests, repairs, vehicles counted; counting not)`) · #stock/repairs: h1 'Repairs'; card 'Faults and repairs · Kit that can't go out is taken off what's free for jobs until it's fixed or found.'; sections 'Damaged, can't go out (6) | Damaged, fit to go out (3) | Missing (6)'; per row a quiet 'Sort it out' (73x44) that opens 'Mark fixed | Mark not faulty | Write off | Change details' in place.
