(`web/src/stock/StockScreen.tsx:179; web/src/stock/Faults.tsx:202,269`) · #stock/vehicles and #crew/people: 'Vehicle checks running out (5)' card drawn on both (map-screens inconsistency; seen on Vehicles). (`web/src/stock/Vehicles.tsx:70`) · #stock/testing: 'Test a batch' form first (PAT | Thorough examination, Who's testing, When, Scan), then 'Inspections due · Can't go out (16) | Due in the next 30 days (4)'. The intro says 'Each pass waits five seconds with Undo, then it's recorded, with no signal too.' (`web/src/stock/Inspections.tsx:539`)
- **Basis:** Colly's rule that every count opens the list that explains it, applied the other way: the row's counts and the tiles' counts should be one set. NN/g Consistency and standards (one list, four names) and Aggregation Effect (design-system behavioural.md: bundle what needs someone into one notice with the list behind it). Design system navigation.md: an area row names pages, not a to-do list. · NN/g Consistency and standards; Coglode Aggregation Effect as adapted in design-system behavioural.md; GOV.UK 'one thing per page' weighed against the warehouse's need for one list (rejected for lists of lists, adopted for the forms, which stay on their pages).
- **Proposal (S):** Draw the row's counts from the same standing.ts figures Home's tiles use, and give the repairs list one name. Views.tsx StockViews: Counting takes the 'places to count this week' count from standing.ts (the warehouse tile's own function) and Testing and Repairs read the same functions as the tiles so the three places never drift; a count of zero stays quiet. Faults.tsx: the card heading becomes 'Repairs' (the h1's word), with the three sub-headings as they are; MenuScreen: 'Repairs — Damaged and missing kit to sort out'. Nothing moves.
- **Trade-off:** One more count on the nine-link row, which already scrolls; the word 'faults' drops from the card heading, though the item page and Report damage keep it where it is the thing being reported. **Depends on:** Nothing; standing.ts already computes the tile counts. **Weighed:** The reviewer's Housekeeping page gathering six lists: one page for the week, but two routes to each list meanwhile, a long phone page and a summed row count against navigation.md; weighed and preferred the small fix, with Home's tiles staying the one list. Dropping the restating tiles on Home: finding 79 already judged this and chose the rename.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/home-warehouse-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/stock-counting-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/stock-repairs-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/stock-vehicles-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/stock-testing-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-warehouse/home-warehouse-phone-dark.png`
- **Sceptic:** Partly reproduced. True: Stock's row counts Testing 16 and Repairs 15 but not Counting, while Home's tile says '12 places to count this week' (Views.tsx:50-54 counts tests, repairs and vehicles only); the repairs list is 'Repairs' (row, h1), 'Faults and repairs' (card h2), 'Repairs — Faults and missing kit to sort out' (Menu); Needs attention starts at y=847 under Going out. Not new or not a slip: the two tiles that restate the lists on Home ('1 job to pick…', '3 jobs with kit still out…') were finding 79 of the 5 November audit, whose fix settled on one name ('Still out after the job') and kept both tile and list, so that instance is decided; 'Vehicle checks running out (5)' on Crew › People is 1.87's deliberate 'People shows certificate and vehicle reminders only to those who look after them', so a second place for the people who look after them, not a stray copy; and the tiles under the Going out list is Time Scarcity working as the W entry asks (the morning's list first), which the reviewer's own alternative concedes. What remains is a consistency slip across two screens (row counts against Home's tiles; one list under three names). The Housekeeping page proposal is M-L, keeps two routes to each list 'for a while' and is close to the ADR 0033 alternative; navigation.md's own rule that an area row names pages, not a to-do list, argues against a summed 'Housekeeping N' entry. The small fix removes the disagreement. (was: "The warehouse's week is nine tiles under Home's fold, counts on three of nine Stock pages, and one list with four names")

**Smaller findings**

- **warehouse-f1 · Offline is honest in the bar but silent on the thing that waits** (P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic). With the signal gone the bar says 'No signal' and after a scan 'No signal · 1 waiting', and the totals ('3 of 122 out', Home '6 of 122 out') count the scans at once; nothing on the scanned row, the answer line or Home's row carries the design system's pending state (a dashed ring, 'Saved on this device'), and the pick list's intro says nothing about signal while Testing's says 'with no signal too'. Honest and it held, but the person has to look at the bar to know what is still on the phone. *Direction:* Draw the app's own pending pill ('Waiting to sync') on the scan answer line and the row while the scan is unsent, as item rows and the job pill already do, so the person need not read the bar to know what is still on the phone. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced: with the signal cut, Back on SH-000007 answered 'SH-000007 back. Undo · Report damage to SH-000007', the head went to '7 back, 1 still out' at once and the bar said 'No signal · 1 waiting'; nothing on the row or answer carried a pending mark (0 elements), and 'Up to date' came back on reconnection. Honest and not lost, as the audit's offline walk found (finding 34 fixed the first minute). The app already has the part the system asks for: StatusPill's pending tone, 'Waiting to sync', drawn with <Pending> on catalogue item rows and the pick list's own job pill (PickScreen.tsx:240), so the fix is to use it on the scan answer rather than the system's 'Saved on this device' wording, which would be a third phrase for one state. Testing's 'with no signal too' line is a nicety, not a rule. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/picklist-offline-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/home-offline-phone-dark.png`
- **warehouse-f2 · Per-item 'Back' is 45px wide beside a 64px 'Missing' on the Coming back rows** (P3 · severity 1 · Adjusted by the sceptic). Each still-out item on the pick list has two inline buttons, 'Back' (45x44) and 'Missing' (64x44). Both are 44 high, so they pass the system's target, but the one pressed most is the narrower and sits 8px from the one that reports kit missing; Missing acts at once with Undo (right by the system) so a glove slip costs an Undo and a flash of amber. *Direction:* Give Back the same minimum width as Missing (64px) and a 16px gap between them, keeping Back first and filled-quiet and Missing plain, so the one pressed most is not the narrower. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced: Back 45x44 at x=116 and Missing 64x44 at x=173 on each Coming back row (12px apart). Both meet the system's 44px, 1.55 already made them 'harder to mis-tap' and Missing undoable, and finding 86 listed the targets under 44px and this was not one, so it is polish. The reviewer's direction, making the number itself a tap that scans the item back, turns a reading row into an action with no press state and would record returns from a scroll with gloves; the smaller change is a minimum width for Back and a wider gap. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/picklist-coming-back-phone-dark.png`
- **warehouse-f3 · Searching the catalogue by number leaves the day's cards drawn under the one result** (P3 · severity 1 · Kept by the sceptic). Typing 'SH-000007' narrows the catalogue card to one row, but the Pick lists and Kit short cards stay underneath (first pick-list row at y=806), so the result reads as one line in a long page rather than an answer; an exact number match could open the item. *Direction:* While a search is typed, draw only the catalogue card (as Jobs does), and open the item when the text is a whole number that matches one. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced: after typing a number the one result is followed by Pick lists at y=787 and Kit short at y=1,932, so the answer reads as a line in a long page. One screen, no pattern, so a finding; the fix (draw only the catalogue card while a search is typed, and open the item on a whole-number match) is small and in the app's own grammar (Jobs' search narrows its list alone). Not among the 103. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-warehouse/stock-find-by-number-phone-dark.png`

**What held up:** Home › Warehouse: 'Going out (5)' first with 'Tomorrow · Sat 7 Nov · 0 of 27 out' rows straight into the pick list (one tap), 'Still out after the job (3)' with the bad flag, and the view remembered on the phone (sh.home). The pick list head is the floor's: 'In 3 days: Load in, Mon 9 Nov at Riverside Park' and 'Going in: Big Van (241-XX-101), Cian Murphy driving', so what goes in which van is one place; department chips 'Audio 5 left | Lighting 1 left | Power 1 left' and 'By where it's kept'; every button 44px high; the scan box docked under the top bar. Scan → one-line answer with a 65x64 Undo ('Undone: SH-000013 Amp rack isn't out: it's back in stock.'); Coming back → 'd&b Y10P back: 7 of 8 back' with 'Report damage to SH-000007' inline, a two-field form (What's wrong, It can still go out), then 'reported damaged: can't go out until it's fixed.' The fault loop closes on the job at once: Repairs went 15 → 16, the job's Where it is reads 'Back in · 1 still out · Next' and 'Checked and deprepped · 2 faults open on its kit', the dock 'Scan the kit back'; the office sees it through its Home tiles ('16 repairs open', '7 damaged items can't go out'), quiet and computed, consistent with how kit short is told. Offline held: 'No signal' then 'No signal · 2 waiting' in the bar, scans counted at once, a trip to Home and back kept them, 'Up to date' on return; the You page says what the browser may clear and how to keep it. Testing's 'Test a batch' intro is the best-worded form on the floor: who, what, five seconds with Undo, works with no signal, shared phone keeps the last name. The desk's Labels, Counting and the pick list are the same parts and words as the phone's (cards, chips, 'Set numbers aside', 'To count this week'), so the admin side feels like one app with the floor; only 'No count is under way on this phone' reads oddly at a desk.

### Staff crew

*A staff member signed in to the app.*

Signed in, a staff member meets the office's app with the buttons taken off. Home › Me is the one screen that is hers (three calm cards: Your day, Your work, Your leave), and it is good. Everything else she needs is somewhere else: her offers and timesheets are answered on her freelancer link in another look ("Open your page"), her leave is two taps away under Crew, and a job she is booked on opens as the office's job page, with the stage strip and a 92px dock that says "Nothing here for you to press" on the first screen and her own call (Show, Sun 24 Jan, call 08:00) below the fold and her role only on the Crew tab. The bar's Jobs, Stock and Crew tabs open the company's lists (132 jobs coming up, 209 products, 43 calls needing crew) in the office's words (Selling, Planning, No quote yet, 7 lines of kit, 1 short), and the Menu's 20 lines describe the office's and warehouse's work (Labels, Counting, "answers to check", "To approve, and not in yet"). The same person also has two models of time off: Apply for leave with approval in the app, and Add days off with no approval on her link. The walk is not about missing buttons (ADR 0034 is right); it is that the app never turns to face her: her booking, her call time, her leave and what is waiting on her are each shown in a different place with a different name.

#### staff-1 · A staff member's own things are split between Home › Me and her link, under different names, and the link lets her take days off without the approval the app requires

**P1 · severity 3 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles staff crew, freelancer (signed in, inferred: the same split, with less in the app) · phone 390×844 dark, desk 1440×900 dark (Home only) · reproduced

A signed-in staff member's own things are split between the app's Home › Me and her freelancer link, each in a different look, with the app sending her out to the link for the parts that need an answer: "2 offers waiting on your answer · Open your page" and "No timesheets of yours yet. You send them from your own page". The same things carry different names on the two: Home says "Your work (2 confirmed)", the person page says "Coming up", the link says "YOUR BOOKINGS"; Home says "Your leave · 21 days … left", the link says "DAYS YOU CAN'T WORK". Worse, the link offers staff "Add days off" with no approval while the app's Leave asks for approval, so one person has two models of time off and an approver's list that does not see the days she marked on the link. Her offers show in four places (Home hint, person page "Offered" pills, Notifications "Offered RF tech on … to Sarah Farrell · About you", and the link) and can be answered in one. It costs a hop to a second page every time something is waiting on her, and it is the reason the app does not feel like hers.

- **Evidence:** #home (Me) as staff: Cards: Your day ("Nothing on today. Next: Vartry Theatre Company Regatta, Sun 24 Jan."), Your work (2 confirmed), hint at the foot of it "2 offers waiting on your answer · Open your page" (opens /f/<token> in a new tab), Your leave ("21 days of annual leave left this year, 1 day in lieu to take. Open leave"). No glance line of what is waiting on her. (`web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:656 ("Your work"), web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:686-700 (offers hint and "Open your page")`) [Page 1,248 px tall, 3 cards; the offers line is the last line of the second card. Method: Playwright innerText and scrollHeight at 390×844.] · /f/<token> (her link) as the same person: Glance line "2 offers to answer · Next: Vartry Theatre Company Regatta, Sun 24 Jan 2027, call 08:00"; sections OFFERS WAITING ON YOU (Accept / Add a note for the office / Can't do this one?), YOUR BOOKINGS (Call sheet ›, Can't make it any more?), calendar feed, DAYS YOU CAN'T WORK listing "Mon 21 Dec to Fri 25 Dec · Annual leave · Approved leave" and then a form From / To / Note (optional) / Add days off, LET US SEE WHEN YOU'RE BUSY, YOUR DOCUMENTS (Send a document), KIT TO BUY, BROKEN OR MISSING KIT (Tell the warehouse ›), YOUR DETAILS, PAST WORK. (`server/src/crew/page.ts:647-670 (Days you can't work and the Add days off form, no isStaff branch), server/src/crew/page.ts:158-233 (isStaff hides rate only)`) [5,140 px tall; the Add days off form is drawn for staff with no staff check (only the Rate field and counter-offer are hidden for staff).] · #crew/timesheets as staff: Empty: "No timesheets of yours yet. You send them from your own page once a booking's days are done." The Menu line that brought her here says "Timesheets — To approve, and not in yet". (`web/src/crew/Timesheets.tsx:68; web/src/MenuScreen.tsx:219`) · #crew/leave as staff: "MY LEAVE · Sarah Farrell", ANNUAL LEAVE and TIME IN LIEU figure grids (12 figures), then Apply for leave (Type, From, To, Note), Log a day in lieu, My requests. Leave is with approval; nothing here knows about days marked off on the link. (`web/src/crew/Leave.tsx:115-130, 270-275`) [Apply for leave heading at y=889 on an 844 px viewport, below the figures. Method: getBoundingClientRect + scrollY.] · #crew/person/v1-person-00004 (her own page) and #notifications: Own page Coming up lists "Inny Conferences Launch · Show · Audio tech · Thu 26 Nov · Offered" and "Blackwater Brands Fashion Show … Offered" with no way to answer and no link to her page; Notifications lists "Offered Audio tech on Inny Conferences Launch to Sarah Farrell · About you" with no way to the offer, and her own answer made on the link, "Sarah Farrell declined RF tech on Lagan Rugby Club Town Hall", as someone else's change although the page says "Your own changes aren't here". (`shared/src/protocol.ts:327-329 (About you / A job you're on); web/src/crew/PersonScreen.tsx (Coming up list)`)
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4 (consistency and standards: one thing, one name, one place) and 6 (recognition rather than recall); design-system principles rule on one place for a person's own work; behavioural.md Friction (every hop loses answers) and Autonomy (the app shows where they stand and lets them ask); personas.md S ("Before this change … their own bookings were only on their link. Gap rated 2"). The two models of time off break ADR 0024's approval for staff in use: the link's days off are unavailability, never approved, and the approver's queue never sees them. · NN/g ten heuristics (consistency, recognition over recall); NN/g on duplicated functionality across channels; GOV.UK service manual "do the hard work to make it simple" (one place for one task); design-system behavioural.md Friction and Autonomy; sess-sys personas.md S and F; ADR 0024 (leave approval), ADR 0034, ADR 0052.
- **Proposal (S):** First close the time-off hole and the naming on the surfaces that exist; then put the in-app answering to Colly as the departure it is. P1, S: (1) For a staff person the link's 'Days you can't work' lists approved leave only and replaces the Add days off form with one line, 'Ask for leave in the app ›' (#crew/leave), and POST /f/:token/away refuses for kind staff (one branch each in page.ts and links.ts, beside the existing isStaff rate branch). (2) Home › Me's Your day becomes the link's glance line, each part a link: '2 offers to answer · Next: Vartry Theatre Company Regatta, Sun 24 Jan, call 08:00' (offers → the link's #offers anchor until in-app answering exists). (3) One name per thing: 'Your bookings' on Home, the person page and the link; 'Leave' / 'Approved leave' for staff everywhere (the link's 'Days you can't work' stays for freelancers). (4) Person page › Coming up: an Offered row carries 'Answer it on your page ›'. Then, as a decision for Colly (M): in-app answering of own offers and own timesheets for signed-in staff, as the reviewer sketched, recorded as a departure from ADR 0034's one way in.
- **Trade-off:** Staff who used the link to block days (e.g. a course) now ask for leave or the office marks it; the office's one place for staff availability becomes Leave, which ADR 0024 already intends. The glance line adds one row to Home › Me. **Depends on:** Nothing for the S part. The in-app answering follow-on needs the server decision the reviewer names (own-offer check on offer.respond / timesheet.send). **Weighed:** The reviewer's M as the first step: weighed and set second, because its departure from ADR 0034 needs Colly before anything is built, while the days-off hole can be closed today. Moving leave onto the link for staff: rejected with the reviewer (ADR 0052 makes the app the staff member's).
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/compare-home-me-vs-link-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/my-link-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/timesheets-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/leave-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/my-person-page-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-staff/compare-home-me-vs-link-staff-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** A person who is both a signed-in user and a link holder needs one owner for each thing they do; the design system has no rule yet for an app that reaches the same person through two surfaces.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced to the pixel: Home › Me is 1,248 px with '2 offers waiting on your answer · Open your page' (new tab) as the last line of Your work; her link is 5,140 px and draws From / To / Note / Add days off for a staff member (server/src/crew/page.ts:647-670 has no isStaff branch, and POST /f/:token/away at server/src/crew/links.ts:586-600 runs unavailability.add for anyone with the link). The two-model time-off hole is not among the 103 fixed (the audit's staff para only records Home › Me as the gain, personas.md 'Gap rated 2') and no ADR decides it: ADR 0024 says staff leave is approved and 'freelancers mark days off on their link', ADR 0052 only describes the link as where crew 'mark days off'. The pattern has one more instance the reviewer missed: Purchase list is in the Menu for staff and KIT TO BUY is on the link, so asking for kit is also on both surfaces. The M proposal is honest about its dependency (offer.respond and timesheet.send need the crew ability, shared/src/roles.ts:113,117) but it is a recorded departure from personas.md S / ADR 0034 ('staff get offers through the same links'), so it is Colly's call, not a sweep fix. The S alternative the reviewer weighed is the one that removes the incoherence that matters (the unapproved days off) with no ADR cost, so it should lead. (was: "A staff member has two homes that split her work between them and call the same things by different names")

#### staff-2 · A job she is booked on opens as the office's job, with her own call below the fold and never named

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles staff crew, freelancer (signed in, inferred), head of department when on a job as crew (inferred) · phone 390×844 dark · reproduced

Opening her next job from Home, a staff member gets the office's job page: CLIENT, VENUE, WHEN (the whole job, Thu 21 to Wed 27 Jan, not her days), CREW "3 of 6 booked, 1 to confirm", the Where it is strip (Selling · Planning · Going out · On the job · Back), the stage's explanation, the task list, and a 92px dock pinned over the foot that reads "Next is crew booked, for whoever books the crew. Nothing here for you to press." Nothing on the page says she is on it as Monitors for the Show phase, Sun 24 to Tue 26 Jan, call 08:00: that sits on her link's booking card and, in the app, only as "1 × Monitors · Filled · Sarah Farrell" inside the Crew tab. The Jobs tab and the Board are the same list the office sees, 132 coming up with no Mine, in the office's words ("Selling · No quote yet · Still enquiry", "Planning · 5 of 10 booked · 7 lines of kit, 1 short, 2 vehicles"). On a show morning this is the screen she opens for the call time, and it is the one screen in the app that is least about her.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/v1-job-00152 as staff (her confirmed booking), first screen: Head: name, Confirmed pill, CLIENT, VENUE (map), WHEN "Thu 21 Jan to Wed 27 Jan", CREW "3 of 6 booked, 1 to confirm", venue note, then the Where it is card ("In Planning · 2 of 8 tasks done", the five stages, "Planning · the stage the job is in. The crew, the kit and the vans are settled before anything moves.", tasks Crew booked / Kit and vans settled), and the dock. (`web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:27-36 (WHOSE) and :147 (the rest state "Nothing here for you to press"); web/src/jobs/JobScreen.tsx (head and Where it is for every role); web/src/jobs/Steps.tsx:250`) [Where it is at y=480; dock 92 px tall at y=665 (viewport 844); her phase "Show Sun 24 Jan to Tue 26 Jan" at y=842 and its "Call sheet and calendar" fold at y=1001, both below the first screen; her name appears nowhere on the page (only on the Crew tab). Method: getBoundingClientRect + scrollY on text nodes.] · #jobs/v1-job-00152 › Crew tab as staff: "Show · Sun 24 Jan to Tue 26 Jan · 1 × Camera 0 of 1, all 3 days · Nessa Brady (offered) · 1 × Monitors Filled · Sarah Farrell · 1 × LX tech 0 of 1 … · 1 × Sound No.1 Filled · Ben Quinn (accepted)": the booking board, where she finds herself among the office's offered/accepted states. (`web/src/jobs/JobScreen.tsx (Crew tab), web/src/crew/Calls.tsx`) · /f/<token> › YOUR BOOKINGS, the same job: "Vartry Theatre Company Regatta Show · Confirmed · Role Monitors · Dates Sun 24 Jan 2027 to Tue 26 Jan 2027 · Call time 08:00 · Venue Riverside Racecourse, 76 Main Street · Own PPE. Lunch provided. · Call sheet: who's on, where, and who to ring › · Can't make it any more?" — her booking in her words, on one card. (`server/src/crew/page.ts:642 (Your bookings)`) · #jobs and #jobs/board as staff: Filters Coming up (132) · Past (135) · Not going ahead (42); sorts Soonest first · Name · Client · Most crew still needed; rows "Selling · Still quoted", "Selling · No quote yet · Still enquiry", "Planning · 5 of 10 booked · 7 lines of kit, 1 short, 2 vehicles · Kit short". No Mine. The Board has the Selling column with "No quote yet · Still enquiry · 6 days left". (`web/src/jobs/JobsScreen.tsx:84 (filters: coming, past, not going ahead) and :286`) [Jobs list 3,410 px, Board 5,127 px on the phone; her 2 bookings are not in the first fortnight shown. Method: innerText and scrollHeight.]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 2 (match between system and the real world: her words are role, days, call time, venue, who to ring) and 8 (aesthetic and minimalist: every extra unit of information competes with the relevant one); design-system principles "one job of the view" (for staff the view's one job is when, where, what to bring, who with); behavioural.md Fast and Slow Thinking (on the day, under pressure, the first screen must carry the answer). ADR 0104/0109 decided one Next everywhere and a dock that tells the truth; the cost in use for a role that can press nothing is a 92 px rest state on every job page and the stage card above her own call. The 1 November job-flow research's five-stage model and Next rules are written for the office and heads, not for the crew on the job. · NN/g ten heuristics (match, minimalist); NN/g on mobile first-screen content priority; design-system principles (one job of the view) and behavioural.md (Fast and Slow Thinking, Friction); job-flow research 2026-11-01 (the five-stage model and Next button rules, written for the office's flow); ADR 0059, 0104, 0109.
- **Proposal (S):** Put the person's own booking first on a job they are on, and let the Jobs list show Mine; leave the stages and the dock as ADR 0104/0109 decided unless Colly chooses the fold. Job page, when the reader has a booking on the job and lacks jobs/crew/kit: a 'You on this job' card under the head and above Where it is, built from the Crew tab's row the device already has: 'Monitors · Show · Sun 24 to Tue 26 Jan · Call 08:00 · Riverside Racecourse (map)', the call note, 'Call sheet ›' to that phase's sheet, and 'Can't make it any more? ›' opening the link at that booking (in-app withdraw only after staff-1's follow-on). The head's CREW figure drops for this role. Jobs list: a 'Mine (2)' filter chip first for anyone with bookings (web/src/jobs/JobsScreen.tsx:84). Offered to Colly as a separate line: fold Where it is to its one line and omit the rest-state dock for a role with nothing to press, recorded against ADR 0104/0109.
- **Trade-off:** A card only some readers see on the job page, and the stage strip still above the fold on a phone (one scroll). Nothing changes for the office. **Depends on:** staff-1's follow-on for an in-app 'Can't make it'; otherwise none. **Weighed:** The reviewer's full M (fold the strip, no dock): weighed; it is the better page for her but it changes two ADRs for one role, so it goes to Colly as an option rather than as the proposal. Sending staff to the link's booking card from Home instead: rejected with the reviewer (a hop, and Jobs stays useless to them).
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/compare-job-page-vs-link-booking-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/my-job-tab-crew.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/my-link-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/jobs-list-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-staff/job-staff-first-screen-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** A record page read by someone whose relation to it is "I'm on it" needs a "you on this" part before the record's own state; the system's list/record pattern has no such part yet.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced: on #jobs/v1-job-00152 as Sarah her name and 'Monitors' appear nowhere on the main page (only on the Crew tab, #jobs/v1-job-00152/crew); the head carries CREW '3 of 6 booked, 1 to confirm'; Where it is sits at y=533; the dock's rest state is 92 px at y=665 reading 'Next is crew booked, for whoever books the crew. Nothing here for you to press.'; her phase 'Show Sun 24 Jan to Tue 26 Jan' and its 'Call sheet and calendar' are at y=895 and 1,054, under the fold. Jobs list has no Mine (132 coming up). Not among the fixed: audit finding 2 / ADR 0109 took the pressable Next off staff; the rest state is what that fix left. But the rating is high: her call time and date are on Home › Your work ('Call 08:00 · Sun 24 Jan to Tue 26 Jan') and on the link, and the call sheet is one scroll down on the default Phases tab, so on a show morning the first screen is Home, not this page. nn 2 at a desk, 3 only on the day with Home skipped. The proposal's parts (3) and (4) re-open ADR 0104's one Next and ADR 0109's truth-telling for one role and should be Colly's decision, not the sweep's; parts (1), (2) and (5) are sound and self-contained. (was: "A job she is booked on opens as the office's job, with her own call below the fold and never named")

#### staff-3 · The bar and the Menu are the office's: three of five tabs open company work lists and the Menu's lines describe work staff can't do

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles staff crew, freelancer (signed in, inferred) · phone 390×844 dark · reproduced

For a staff member the phone bar is Home · Jobs · Stock · Crew · Menu, and Jobs, Stock and Crew open the company's lists (132 jobs, 209 products, 43 calls still needing crew) that she can only read. Her own things are a tap further: Leave and Timesheets under the Crew row, You and Notifications at the foot of a 20-line Menu whose lines are written for the office and the warehouse: "Calls — Crew needed, and answers to check" (she checks no answers), "Timesheets — To approve, and not in yet" (hers is "No timesheets of yours yet"), "Labels — Print labels for kit" and "Counting — Stocktakes and rolling counts" (1.85 stopped showing staff how to do counts and labels, but the Menu still lists them), "Clients — Who the jobs are for, their contacts and documents". Home still offers her Company · Department · Warehouse · Me, where Company says "Needs attention — Nothing here for you to sort out." over the company's charts. It is not that anything is offered and then refused (ADR 0034 holds); it is that the map of the app is the office's map with her two or three places unmarked on it.

- **Evidence:** #menu as staff: Lines under Jobs: Jobs, Week and month, Clients, Venues; Stock: Stock, Places, Labels, Counting, Testing, Repairs, Racks, Vehicles, Purchase list; Crew: Calls ("Crew needed, and answers to check"), People, Leave ("Time off for staff"), Timesheets ("To approve, and not in yet"); Company: History; You: You ("Signing out, your own calendar feed and this device"), Notifications. (`web/src/MenuScreen.tsx:135 (Labels) and :142 (Counting) have no needs; :198 Calls about; :219 Timesheets about; :279 show() gates by needs and when only`) [20 destination lines (anchors counted), 2,480 px tall; the lines that are hers (Leave, Timesheets, You, Notifications) are lines 16, 17, 19 and 20. Method: anchor innerText list.] · #home as staff, the view toggle: "Company | Department | Warehouse | Me" segmented group offered; Company shows "Needs attention — Nothing here for you to sort out.", Today (1), Jobs by week, Crew, the next fortnight, The week ahead (6) with "Selling · Quoted" and "Kit short", The season; Warehouse shows Going out pick lists and Still out after the job. (`web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:56-61 (the four views), :72 (staff default to me), :146 (withheld all-clear)`) [Company view 2,448 px, Department 1,987 px, Warehouse 1,838 px, against Me 1,248 px. Nothing on the page or the Menu says "broken" or "damage": the way to report is Scan › item › Report damage, while her link says "BROKEN OR MISSING KIT — Tell the warehouse ›". (`web/src/stock/StockScreen.tsx; server/src/crew/page.ts:590-591`) [2,955 px; the words damage/broken/fault do not occur in the first 1,500 characters of the page. Method: regex on innerText.]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 2 (the words of the person) and 8 (minimalist); navigation.md: a bottom bar's five are live addresses, the same for everyone, and the Menu is the index; behavioural.md Limited Choice (two to five options sensibly restricted) and Analysis Paralysis; Colly's rule that a button that can't work for your role isn't offered extends in spirit to a Menu line whose description is someone else's job. · NN/g ten heuristics; NN/g on navigation menus and information scent (a label's description sets the expectation of the page); design-system navigation.md (bar of five, the Menu as index) and behavioural.md (Limited Choice); Colly's standing rule on buttons a role can't use; changelog 1.85 (staff aren't shown how to do counts or labels).
- **Proposal (S):** Keep one bar and one Menu order for everyone; make each line's description true for whoever reaches it, and give staff the one Stock thing they do. MenuScreen: an about per audience where they differ (can('crew') / can('stock') or not): Calls → 'Who's needed where, and who's on today'; People → 'Everyone on the crew, and their numbers'; Timesheets → 'Yours, once a booking's days are done'; Counting → 'How the stocktake is going'; Labels → 'The labels on kit' or needs: ['stock']; Repairs → 'Broken or missing kit, and what's being fixed'. Shell: the #crew route, not only the bar's href, lands a role without crew on People (web/src/Shell.tsx:119 → the router). Home: the Company | Department | Warehouse | Me group only for roles with crew or stock; staff get Me with one quiet link at the foot, 'The company's week ›'. Stock root for roles without stock: a 'Broken or missing kit · Report it ›' card above Kit short, in the link's words, opening Scan.
- **Trade-off:** Two about strings for five Menu places to keep in step. Staff lose the Company view's charts as a tap (kept as a link). **Depends on:** Nothing. **Weighed:** The reviewer's You-first Menu and Leave-first Crew row for staff: dropped, a per-role order is the 'two apps' this sweep is against and support will hear 'where is Leave on yours'. Renaming the Crew tab 'Me' for staff: rejected with the reviewer.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/compare-menu-and-home-company-staff-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/home-company-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/crew-calls-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/stock-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-staff/menu-staff-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** A Menu line's description is a promise about the page; the system's navigation guidance should say that a description is written for the roles that reach it, not for the role that owns the work.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced: the Menu lists 20 places for staff (2,533 px), with Labels and Counting (no needs, web/src/MenuScreen.tsx:135,142), Calls 'Crew needed, and answers to check', Timesheets 'To approve, and not in yet'; the bar's Crew lands on #crew/people (Shell.tsx:119) while the plain address #crew lands on Calls with 'Gráinne: about 30 minutes late' and SHORT OF CREW; Home still offers Company | Department | Warehouse | Me. Not fixed: 1.85 (audit 74) removed the how-to sentences but left the Menu lines; audit 46's fallback (honest all-clear) was taken, so the toggle stands by a fix choice, not an ADR; audit 79 is about place names, not descriptions per audience. The about-strings-per-audience part and the Stock 'Broken or missing kit' card are sound and S. Two parts should go: reordering the Menu You-first and the Crew row Leave-first for one role makes two maps of one app, which navigation.md's 'the Menu is the index' and the sweep's own aim argue against, and the reviewer already names the support cost. Hiding Counting and Labels loses nothing for staff, but the smaller fix is the same about-string change, since the pages are readable and 1.85 already made them honest. One S fix the reviewer saw but did not propose: make the #crew address land where the bar does for a role without crew. (was: "The bar and the Menu are the office's: three of five tabs open company work lists and the Menu's lines describe work staff can't do")

#### staff-4 · "Not yours" is said to staff in eight shapes, some of which still tell her to do the thing

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles staff crew, head of department (money and jobs refusals, inferred), freelancer (signed in, inferred) · phone 390×844 dark · reproduced

Across the screens a staff member reads, that something is someone else's is said in seven shapes (the dock's 'Next is crew booked, for whoever books the crew. Nothing here for you to press.', Home's 'Nothing here for you to sort out.', Quotes' 'Quotes are for the office and directors…', the pick list's 'Whoever keeps the stock scans kit out and back here.', Counting's 'The warehouse and heads of department do the counts.', Timesheets' 'You send them from your own page', a colleague's 'Your role doesn't see … The office has them.'), and the Vehicles empty state still instructs her to book a van with no button. The app already has two tables of who does what (NextDock's WHOSE and roles.ts whoCan) and the sentences use neither, so each screen names the owner its own way.

- **Evidence:** #jobs/v1-job-00152 (dock and task card) as staff: Dock: "Next is crew booked, for whoever books the crew. Nothing here for you to press."; the opened task: "Kit and vans settled … Not given to anyone yet." (`web/src/jobs/NextDock.tsx:27-36, :147; web/src/jobs/Steps.tsx:250`) · #jobs/v1-job-00152 › Kit and vans as staff: "Vehicles — Nothing here yet. Book a van for the job, or for one phase of it, with a driver from the crew." with no button: the empty state instructs an action the role can't take. (`web/src/jobs/Vans.tsx:47`) · #home (Company) · #quotes · #crew/timesheets · #stock/counting · a pick list · a colleague's page: "Nothing here for you to sort out." / "Quotes are for the office and directors. A director can change your role on the Crew tab." / "No timesheets of yours yet. You send them from your own page once a booking's days are done." / "No count is under way on this phone. The warehouse and heads of department do the counts." / "Whoever keeps the stock scans kit out and back here." / "Your role doesn't see Ben Moran's email, notes or company. The office has them." (`web/src/home/HomeScreen.tsx:146; web/src/jobs/QuotesScreen.tsx:59; web/src/crew/Timesheets.tsx:68; web/src/stock/Counts.tsx:284; web/src/jobs/PickScreen.tsx:256; web/src/crew/PersonScreen.tsx:215`) [Eight distinct sentence shapes for the one state on the staff walk, three of them naming who in a different way ("whoever books the crew", "the warehouse and heads of department", "the office and directors"). Method: read on the walk and grepped.]
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4 (consistency: the same situation, the same words) and 9 (plain language naming the problem and the way on); design-system states.md "Not sent to you" (hidden is "Hidden for your role" with ds-lock; an action a role can never take is not drawn; one they could ask for is locked, saying who can) and components.md's empty state rule (what this is, how it fills, the direct way to start as a button: with no button for this role, the body must say who fills it, not instruct the reader). · NN/g ten heuristics (consistency; help users recognise); GOV.UK content design on error and empty-state wording (say who, say what next); design-system states.md (not sent to you, locked) and components.md (empty state); learnings.md 2026-11-01 (refusals and "Hidden for your role" already adopted as the shared rule).
- **Proposal (S):** One sentence shape for someone else's work, naming who from the table the refusals already use, and empty states that do not instruct a role that has no button. A NotYours part beside act.tsx with one shape, '<Who> does this. <What you can do here, if anything>.', in ink.secondary, taking who from whoCan(ability) in shared/src/roles.ts (fold NextDock's WHOSE into it so the dock and the part agree). Dock rest: 'The office books the crew.'; Vehicles for a role without kit: 'No vans booked yet. The heads of department book them.'; Counting: 'No count under way. The warehouse counts.'; Quotes: 'The office and directors do quotes.'; Timesheets until staff-1: 'Yours are sent from your page. Open your page ›'. Hidden data takes states.md's 'Hidden for your role' with the lock in place of 'Your role doesn't see …'. Server refusals keep 'Only the office and directors can …' (an action that was tried).
- **Trade-off:** Eight screens change a sentence; the office reads the new dock shape too. **Depends on:** Nothing; staff-1 changes the Timesheets line again. **Weighed:** Fix only the instructing empty state (Vans.tsx:47): smaller, but the next screen invents an eighth shape.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/my-job-first-screen-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/my-job-tab-kit-and-vans.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/quotes-refused-phone-dark.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-staff/job-kit-staff-phone-dark.png`
- **Learning:** states.md covers data not sent and actions not drawn; it has nothing for a whole view that is someone else's work, read for information, and the sentence that should head it.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced on seven screens: the dock's rest state, Vehicles' empty state 'Nothing here yet. Book a van for the job, or for one phase of it, with a driver from the crew.' with no button (web/src/jobs/Vans.tsx:47 has no mayBook branch, the same class audit 74 fixed for Counts and Labels), Quotes, Timesheets, Counting, the pick list's hint and a colleague's 'Your role doesn't see Ben Moran's email, notes or company. The office has them.' (PersonScreen.tsx:215), which departs from the 'Hidden for your role' wording learnings.md 2026-11-01 records as adopted and states.md sets. One instance is wrong: Steps.tsx:250 'Not given to anyone yet.' is the task's owner line every role reads, not a not-yours sentence, so the count is seven shapes, not eight. Not fixed (1.85 fixed two of the sentences, which is why the pattern is visible) and not decided. The proposal is sound but reinvents a table: the app already has two 'who' tables, NextDock.tsx:27-36 WHOSE and shared/src/roles.ts whoCan() (used at :250 for refusals), so the part should read from whoCan and the dock from the same. (was: ""Not yours" is said to staff in eight shapes, some of which still tell her to do the thing")

**Smaller findings**

- **staff-f3 · Reporting broken kit is in crew words on the link and in warehouse words in the app** (P2 · severity 2 · Kept by the sceptic). Her link says "BROKEN OR MISSING KIT — Tell the warehouse › So it's checked before it goes out again." The app's Stock root and Menu say "Repairs — Faults and missing kit to sort out" and nothing about reporting; the way is Scan › the item › Report damage / Report missing, three taps with no signpost. The one thing staff can do in Stock (ADR 0034) is the one thing Stock does not name. *Direction:* A "Broken or missing kit · Report it ›" card on Stock for roles without stock, in the link's words, opening Scan at the report step; carried by staff-3's sketch. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced: Stock's first screen for staff (3,020 px) has only the word 'Repairs' from the tab row in its first 1,500 characters, no 'broken', 'damage' or 'fault', and the only way to report is Scan › the item › Report damage; the link says 'BROKEN OR MISSING KIT — Tell the warehouse ›'. 1.84 gave the link its way (ADR 0012, audit 267's freelancer gap); the app's side was not touched. personas.md S lists reporting a fault as one of their four things, so a signpost in their words is the frequent-task-one-tap rule (NN/g 7). Carried by staff-3's Stock card; stands alone if staff-3 is not picked. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/stock-phone-dark.png`
- **staff-f1 · Notifications tell staff about offers and their own answers in the office's voice, with nowhere to go** (P3 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic). A staff member's Notifications list says 'Offered Audio tech on Inny Conferences Launch to Sarah Farrell · About you' and opens the office's job page where she can't answer; lists her own link answers ('Sarah Farrell declined RF tech on …') as someone else's change under a foot that says 'Your own changes aren't here'; and words a cancelled job as 'status to cancelled'. *Direction:* An offer row to the reader reads 'You've been offered Audio tech on Inny Conferences Launch · reply by Tue 24 Nov' and opens where it can be answered (the link's #offers until staff-1's follow-on); answers made on a person's own link are their own changes and are left out of their list; 'status to cancelled' becomes 'cancelled' in history-words. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced with one correction: the 'Offered Audio tech on Inny Conferences Launch to Sarah Farrell · About you' rows are links, to the office's job page (#jobs/v1-job-00282), where she cannot answer, so the statement is 'opens the job, not the offer' rather than 'nowhere to go'. Her own link answers ('Sarah Farrell declined RF tech on Lagan Rugby Club Town Hall') do sit in a list whose foot says 'Your own changes aren't here', and 'Changed the job Dodder Credit Union Food Fair: status to cancelled' is the command's words. Audit 61 (1.86) fixed the filled-offer meaning only. P3 holds. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/notifications-phone-dark.png`
- **staff-f2 · Her bookings and offers are listed on four surfaces under three names, and answerable on one** (P3 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic). Home › Your work (2 confirmed), her person page › Coming up (with "Offered" pills), the Jobs tab (two among 132) and the link › YOUR BOOKINGS / OFFERS WAITING ON YOU all list the same bookings; only the link lets her answer. Each list orders and words them differently (Home: "Monitors · Show · Riverside Racecourse, 76 Main Street · Confirmed · Sun 24 Jan to Tue 26 Jan · Call 08:00"; person page: "Show · Monitors · Sun 24 Jan to Tue 26 Jan · Confirmed"; link: Role / Dates / Call time / Venue rows). *Direction:* Folded into staff-1 as its evidence; the shared booking row (name, then role · phase · days · call, status pill) is staff-1's naming step. (S). *Sceptic:* Reproduced: Home 'Monitors · Show · Riverside Racecourse … Confirmed · Sun 24 Jan to Tue 26 Jan · Call 08:00', the person page 'Show · Monitors · Sun 24 Jan to Tue 26 Jan · Confirmed' with Offered rows that cannot be answered, the Jobs list (no Mine), the link's Role / Dates / Call time rows. It is the evidence of staff-1 rather than a finding of its own; keep it only as staff-1's instance list, with its one standalone S (one booking row drawn the same on Home, the person page and the link) folded into staff-1's naming part. Screenshots: `screens/ux-sweep/lens-staff/compare-home-me-vs-link-phone-dark.png`

**What held up:** Home › Me is short and hers: three cards (Your day, Your work, Your leave) in 1,248 px, the leave balance in a sentence, the next booking named with its date; the audit's gain holds. The link page's order and glance line ("2 offers to answer · Next: …, call 08:00") are the model for what staff should get in the app; its booking card (Role, Dates, Call time, Venue, note, Call sheet, Can't make it any more?) is the crew's vocabulary done right. Refusals that are refusals are plain and kind: Quotes and Money say who they are for and how a role changes, with no dead buttons; nothing on the walk was offered and then refused (ADR 0034 holds everywhere seen). Leave's figures and rules are right for the person: allowance, carried over, booked, left; the year-open line names the approvers; days in lieu explained in one sentence. ADR 0062's narrowing is honest and useful: colleagues keep their phone number with a Call / WhatsApp button, and what is hidden says so. The dock, the task card and the stage strip are consistent with each other across every job page seen; the incoherence is only that they face the office on a page staff open for their call.

### The freelancer

*Ninety people on a link, on their own phones.*

The link reads as the same product as the app: the SH mark, the brand red as 'you' (Waiting on you, Accept), the system's colour roles in both themes, muted small-cap section heads, folded destructive actions as the no-script arm. The top of the page is right: the glance line says what is waiting and what is next, offers are three full cards then a line each, bookings the same, and every post comes back with a message in the card that caused it. Where it falls short is the job's journey after the day and what reaches a freelancer when the office changes something. After the show, one job sits in two lists with two words (Past work says 'Confirmed', Timesheets says 'Send your days' / 'Sent' / 'Approved' / 'Paid') and nothing adds up what they are owed, though the office has that figure. A call time the office changes after a yes simply shows the new value; a cancelled job becomes a 'Withdrawn' line 7.6 screens down, the same words as being taken off a job, with nothing at the top. Accept is one tap with no way back except 'I can't make it', which the page then records as 'You pulled out'. And a third of the page (about 1,600 px) is set-once prose about calendars, documents and kit, drawn in full on every visit between the things that are due, even on a page with no work on it.

#### freelancer-2 · What the office changes after a yes reaches the freelancer silently, or as a line at the foot of the page

**P1 · severity 3 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles F, S (staff use the same page to answer), O (who otherwise texts by hand) · phone 390×844 dark · reproduced

The app contacts nobody until a switch is on, so the link page is the only place a freelancer learns that a booking changed. When the office changed the call time of a confirmed booking from 09:00 to 07:30, the card simply showed 'Call time 07:30': no word that it changed, nothing in the glance line, nothing at the top. When the office cancelled a confirmed job, the booking vanished from Your bookings and reappeared under 'Declined and withdrawn' as 'Withdrawn on Fri 6 Nov', 7.6 screens down, in the same words as the office taking them off a job it is still running. A freelancer who checks the page the night before turns up at 09:00 for a 07:30 call, or holds a day for a job that no longer exists. The design system has a part for exactly this (news of others' changes on the toast and notification list) and staff get it; freelancers, whose whole experience is this page, get nothing.

- **Evidence:** /f/xvz5Q58jkuG_Y92jWkvRY2R6 after the office's call.update {callTime:'07:30'} on v1-call-00123 (Maigue Church Roadshow Load out, confirmed): Card reads 'Maigue Church Roadshow Load out · Confirmed · Role Stagehand · Dates Mon 21 Dec · Call time 07:30 · …'. Nothing says it was 09:00 when she said yes. Glance line unchanged: '1 offer to answer · Next: Clare Hotels Fundraiser, Sun 29 Nov, call 07:00'. (`server/src/crew/page.ts:160-172 (facts draws the call's current values only); page.ts:352-365 (glance: offers to answer, timesheets to send, next); no 'changed since' anywhere in page.ts or sheet.ts (grep 'changed|since you')`) [0 words of change on the page; 0 items in the glance; the card is the 2nd full booking card, 1,900 px down.] · /f/xvz5Q58jkuG_Y92jWkvRY2R6 after the office's project.update {status:'cancelled'} on v1-job-00032 (Nore Pharma Product Reveal, confirmed booking for Sun 3 Jan 2027): Your bookings no longer lists it; 'Declined and withdrawn' (last section) gains 'Nore Pharma Product Reveal (Build) · Rigger · Sun 3 Jan 2027 · Withdrawn on Fri 6 Nov'. The same section's 'Dargle Arts Collective Hackathon … Withdrawn on Fri 6 Nov' is an offer the office withdrew from a job still going ahead: one word for both. (`server/src/crew/page.ts:395-412 (ended(): 'cancelled' → 'Withdrawn' whether the job was cancelled or the offer pulled); server/src/projects/handlers.ts:150-151 (cancelCall for every open call on a cancelled job); server/src/crew/handlers.ts:48 (OVER statuses); web/src/crew/Calls.tsx:62 (office: 'Withdrawn')`) [Section 'Declined and withdrawn' at scrollY 6,434 px of 7,163 px: 7.6 screens down on a 390×844 phone. No message at the top; the glance line says nothing.] · /f/<token>/sheet/<call> footer: 'This sheet shows what the office has as you open it: open it again on the day for any changes.' The page knows changes happen and puts the checking on the freelancer. (`server/src/crew/sheet.ts:174-176`)
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 1 (visibility of system status: keep people informed of what changed) and 9 (recognise, diagnose: a cancelled job and a withdrawn offer are not the same thing); design-system toast 'News of others' changes' (someone moved your call time) and notification list; Colly's rule that the app contacts nobody until the switch is on, which makes the page itself the only channel; map-flow handoff 'O marks the job cancelled → F, S: their link page tag becomes Withdrawn'. · NN/g heuristics 1 and 9; design-system components.md Toast ('News of others' changes'), Notice, Notification list; behavioural.md Friction (no extra step asked of them); ADR 0002 (the link is the whole channel), ADR 0028 (the day card), map-flow.json handoffs
- **Proposal (M):** Draw what changed on their held bookings since they said yes, on the card and at the top of the page, in the notice's shape, without sending anything. Slice 1 (S): in facts(), when the mutations log holds a call.update to start, end, callTime or venue since the offer's respondedAt (or confirmedAt), draw the old value quietly beside the fact for seven days: 'Call time 07:30 (was 09:00)'. In ended(), say 'The job was cancelled' when the project is cancelled or lost and 'Withdrawn by the office' when only the offer or call was; the office's Calls.tsx keeps 'Withdrawn' or takes the split. Slice 2 (M): under the glance line, a 'Since you said yes' block, one ds-notice-shaped '--warn' line per net change per field, in history-words: 'Maigue Church Roadshow, Mon 21 Dec: call time is now 07:30, was 09:00', 'Nore Pharma Product Reveal, Sun 3 Jan: the job is cancelled, so you're not needed', each a link to the card or the Declined line; the glance gains '2 changes to your bookings' as its first part. Collapse to a Disclosure count past three. A per-person last-opened timestamp can replace the said-yes baseline later if the block grows noisy.
- **Trade-off:** The said-yes baseline shows a change until the card leaves the page, not until they have seen it; a last-opened column fixes that at the cost of one write per page open. A change list surfaces office mistakes (a call time set wrong and fixed) to the crew, which is honest. The existing tell-the-crew prompts (Calls.tsx:308 'call-changed', JobScreen.tsx:409 'job-stopped') stay as the complement once messages are on. **Depends on:** The mutations log already kept per command (server/src/commands.ts, history-words.ts); the person's held offers; no change to the contact-nobody rule (ADR 0048) and consistent with ADR 0058's reason for giving freelancers no notifications. **Weighed:** (a) Rely on the office's tell-the-crew prompt after a change: already built, but off until the switch and dependent on the office; keep as a complement. (b) Re-confirm after every change: adds a tap and an unanswered state for every small edit; rejected by Friction.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-freelancer/booking-after-office-changed-call-time-dark-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-freelancer/past-and-declined-dark-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-freelancer/maigue-card-call-time-changed-dark-phone.png`
- **Learning:** A server-drawn page with no push channel needs its own 'since you last looked' part: the notification list's content drawn inline from the log, keyed by last-opened.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced by pushing the office's two changes through /api/sync/push on my own server (call.update callTime 07:30 on v1-call-00123; project.update status cancelled on v1-job-00032) and reopening Nessa's page: the Maigue card reads 'Confirmed · Call time 07:30' with no word that it was 09:00, no flash, glance unchanged ('2 offers to answer · 1 timesheet to send · Next: Clare Hotels Fundraiser…'); the Nore Pharma booking has left Your bookings and sits under Declined and withdrawn as 'Withdrawn on Fri 6 Nov' at scrollY 6,727 of 7,455 (8 screens down), the same words as the Dargle offer the office withdrew from a job still going ahead. No 'changed' or 'was' anywhere on the page (grep of body text). Code as cited: facts() draws current values (page.ts:160-172); ended() maps 'cancelled' to 'Withdrawn' (page.ts:398-400); cancelCall runs for every open call on a stopped job (projects/handlers.ts:150-151). Not in the audit's 103. ADR 0058 decides freelancers get no notifications 'because their link page already shows what's theirs': the premise the proposal fulfils, not a decision against it, and the proposal sends nothing, so ADR 0048 holds. Three adjustments: (1) the reviewer's alternative (a) says to 'add' a tell-the-crew prompt after a call-time change, but it exists: Calls.tsx:308 promptFor('call-changed') and JobScreen.tsx:409 'job-stopped'. They are off until the messages switch (ADR 0048) and depend on the office pressing Send, which is why the page itself still needs to say it. (2) Start from the baseline that needs no write: 'since you said yes' (respondedAt, or the confirm's time from the mutations log) before adding a last-opened column, which turns a pure read into a write per open. (3) Split the size: the 'was 09:00' on the card and 'The job was cancelled' in ended() are an S slice worth doing on their own; the Since-you-last-looked block is the M. Staff use the same page, so they get it too, which is right. NN 3: a 07:30 call missed is a job lost on the day; P1 stands. (was: "What the office changes after a yes reaches the freelancer silently, or as a line at the foot of the page")

#### freelancer-1 · After the day, one job is in two lists with two different states, and nothing says what they are owed

**P2 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles F · phone 390×844 dark, phone 390×844 light · reproduced

Once a booking's day has passed it appears under Past work as 'Confirmed' and again under Timesheets as 'Send your days and extras' / 'Sent: €200. The office will check it' / 'Approved: €263' / 'Paid Sat 31 Oct: €200'. Seven of the eight Past work rows on the busiest freelancer's page are also Timesheets rows, with a different state word in each place, 4,477 px (5.3 phone screens) apart, and 'Confirmed' means nothing after the show. The freelancer's own journey is offered → booked → worked → paid; the last two steps are split across two lists. Timesheets are off live today (ADR 0090), so this bites the day they are turned on, for every freelancer at once.

- **Evidence:** /f/xvz5Q58jkuG_Y92jWkvRY2R6 (Nessa Walsh, busiest), phone 390×844 dark: Past work: 'Slaney Theatre Company Showcase (Build) · Rigger · Thu 29 Oct · Confirmed', 'Fergus Festivals Regatta (Load out) · Stagehand · Tue 27 Oct · Confirmed' … eight rows, every one 'Confirmed'. Timesheets, 1,359 px up the page: 'Fergus Festivals Regatta Load out · Stagehand · Tue 27 Oct · Send your days and extras ›' and, folded under 'Sent and approved (15)', the same Slaney, Shannonside, Moy Film, Deel Council and Barrow jobs as 'Sent: €290. The office will check it ›', 'Approved: €241 ›', 'Paid Mon 26 Oct: €290 ›'. (`server/src/crew/page.ts:682 (Past work drawn with 'Confirmed' / 'Accepted, never confirmed'), page.ts:315-341 (timesheets rows and states), page.ts:352-365 (glance counts only 'to send'); web/src/jobs/Money.tsx:115,525 (office's 'Owed to freelancers')`) [Section heads by scrollY (getBoundingClientRect + scrollY): Timesheets at 1,359 px, Past work at 5,835 px: 4,476 px (5.3 phone screens) apart. 7 of 8 Past work rows also in Timesheets (matched by job and day in the page text). Seven timesheets 'Sent … The office will check it' go back to Fri 27 Feb 2026; three 'Approved' with no paid day back to Thu 12 Feb 2026; no total anywhere.] · /f/xvz5Q58jkuG_Y92jWkvRY2R6/timesheet/<approved offer>: 'APPROVED: €263 · 1 day at €200, Thu 12 Feb 2026 €200 · Parking €24 · Mileage €39 · Approved on Fri 6 Nov. If something's wrong, ask the office to reopen it.' Clear for one timesheet; nothing on the page or the list says how many are approved and unpaid together.
- **Basis:** NN/g heuristic 4 (consistency: one thing, one name on one page) and 1 (visibility of status: where the money is); design-system list rule 'every count opens the list that explains it' and Colly's rule that the job's journey is followable; the 1 Nov research's five-stage model ends at 'paid', and the freelancer's page ends at 'Confirmed'. · NN/g ten heuristics (1 visibility of system status, 4 consistency); 1 Nov job-flow research (five stages end at paid); design-system components.md List and Disclosure ('say what is inside', a count on a closed fold); ADR 0090, 0110
- **Proposal (M):** Fold Past work and Timesheets into one 'Work done' section, ordered by what is due, each past booking a line that carries its money state instead of 'Confirmed'. On /f/<token>: replace the Timesheets section and the Past work section with 'Work done' where Timesheets is now (after Offers, before Your bookings). Each row is the page's existing ts-list line: job, phase, role, days, and the state as the third line: 'Send your days and extras ›' (accent edge, first), 'Sent €200, with the office ›' (warn edge), 'Approved €263, not paid yet ›' (ink edge), 'Paid Sat 31 Oct, €200 ›' (quiet), 'Accepted, never confirmed' (quiet). The first three in full, the rest folded under one summary with a count the way bookings fold. With timesheets off (ADR 0090) the same rows carry only the days and 'Accepted, never confirmed' where it applies: today's Past work in the new place. The glance line keeps '1 timesheet to send' and gains nothing else. Drop the word 'Confirmed' after the day everywhere on the link.
- **Trade-off:** The office's Timesheets page keeps its own grouping (To approve / Not in yet / Approved lately), so the two sides still group differently. Work done sits above Your bookings, so a freelancer with many past rows scrolls past the fold line to reach bookings; the fold keeps that to one line. **Depends on:** The paid-on day already on the link (1.84); ADR 0090's switch respected; nothing in the app. **Weighed:** Keep two lists but make each Past work row say its timesheet state (S): removes the wrong word but keeps the same job in two lists 5 screens apart. The merge is preferred because the freelancer's question after the day is one question. The reviewer's glance sum of approved-unpaid was weighed and left out: healthy is quiet, and it adds a money total to a page shown to others.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-freelancer/side-by-side-timesheets-vs-past-work-dark-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-freelancer/timesheet-approved-dark-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-freelancer/nessa-dark-phone-full.png`
- **Learning:** A person's own page should carry one list for 'done' with the state that matters after the day, not a status word that stopped meaning anything when the day passed.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced on a fresh template (Nessa, 390x844 dark): Past work has eight rows, every one 'Confirmed'; Timesheets at scrollY 2,175 and Past work at 6,652, 4,477 px apart; 7 of the 8 Past work jobs are also Timesheets rows with a different state word (Fergus 'Send your days and extras', Slaney/Shannonside/Deel 'Sent: the office will check it', Moy Film Summit 23 Jun 'Approved', Moy Film Trade Show and Barrow 'Paid'). Code as cited: page.ts:682 draws 'Confirmed' for past rows; the timesheet rows carry the money state. Not in the audit's 103 (finding 65 was 'never says it was paid', fixed 1.84; finding 32 folded the sent rows) and no ADR decides that a past booking is listed twice. The merge is the smallest change that gives the job one line after the day, and it respects ADR 0090 (with the switch off the rows carry only the days, which is today's Past work). Two adjustments: (1) drop the glance's '€1,043 approved, not paid yet' sum. Healthy is quiet, the office's 'owed' figure (Money.tsx costsOwed) is the office's view, and it would put one more money total on a page a freelancer shows other people, which audit finding 64 already argued against; each row says its own state, which is enough. (2) Say that timesheets are off live today (ADR 0090), so the incoherence bites the day Colly turns them on, not now; that makes it worth doing before, not a P1. 'Accepted, never confirmed' should stay as a row word (it is the one past state that matters for pay). Size M is honest. (was: "After the day, one job is in two lists with two different states, and nothing says what they are owed")

#### freelancer-4 · Set-once prose is drawn in full on every visit between the things that are due

**P2 · severity 2 · Kept by the sceptic** · roles F · phone 390×844 dark, phone 390×844 light · reproduced

The page is ordered by what's due and folds its lists after three, but the things a freelancer sets once (the calendar feed, sharing their own calendar, sending a document, the purchase list, broken kit) are drawn as full prose every time, in the middle of the page. The calendar feed's two paragraphs sit inside Your bookings on every visit, even for a freelancer with no bookings; 'Let us see when you're busy' is 79 words and 420 px; the documents' send form is open at 576 px. Together they are about 1,600 px (two phone screens) of a nine-screen page, and on a new freelancer's page they are most of what there is. The page already has the right part for this (Your details folds to one 54 px line) and uses it for only that one section. Kit to buy and Broken or missing kit, which are the company's asks rather than the freelancer's, sit above their own details and past work.

- **Evidence:** /f/xvz5Q58jkuG_Y92jWkvRY2R6 (Nessa), phone dark: Inside Your bookings, after the folded bookings: 'Put your bookings in your calendar, kept up to date. It shows your bookings and what each pays, so share it only with people you'd tell that to. If our Google Calendar invites already reach you, you don't need it as well.' + field + Subscribe / Copy address + 'On a phone, Subscribe adds it to your calendar app. Google Calendar can't take it on a phone: copy the address and add it on a laptop, under Other calendars, From URL.' Then LET US SEE WHEN YOU'RE BUSY with another 79 words and a field; then YOUR DOCUMENTS with three cards and an open send form; then KIT TO BUY and BROKEN OR MISSING KIT; then YOUR DETAILS folded to one line. (`server/src/crew/page.ts:445-454 (feedBlock drawn inside #bookings at 643), page.ts:473-514 (ownCalendarBlock always open), page.ts:376-391 (details: the one folded section, class 'me'), page.ts:579-594 (purchases and broken blocks), page.ts:655-661 (order)`) [Block heights by getBoundingClientRect at 390 px: .feed 368 px / 79 words; #calendar 420 px / 79 words; #documents 576 px; #buy 139 px; #broken 116 px; section.me (Your details, folded) 54 px. Sum of set-once blocks 1,619 px = 1.9 screens of a 7,163 px (8.5-screen) page; 979 words on the page in all.] · /f/znwuoZSVEcHOWfWKM4yzvLxM (Úna Kenny, no offers, no bookings), phone dark: After 'Nothing waiting right now.' and 'No upcoming bookings.' the first thing on the page is the feed's two paragraphs about Google Calendar on a laptop; then busy-calendar prose, days off, documents ('None yet…'), Kit to buy, Broken kit, Your details. [Page 2,411 px (2.9 screens) with nothing to do on it; no glance line (it draws nothing when there is nothing to say), so the first word of substance is the calendar prose.]
- **Basis:** Design-system Disclosure ('shut by default, opens only when what is inside needs the person; never opens to advertise'; a count that says what's inside); ADR 0104's fold after three applied to lists but not prose; NN/g heuristic 8 (aesthetic and minimalist design: every extra unit of information competes with the relevant units); personas.md F 'ordered by what's due'. · Design-system components.md Disclosure and Empty state; NN/g heuristic 8; ADR 0104 (folds after three); audit 5 Nov findings 32 and 53 (lists folded, prose not)
- **Proposal (S):** Fold every set-once block the way Your details already folds, with a one-line summary that says its state, and open it only when it needs them. On /f/<token>: (1) Take the feed out of Your bookings into its own folded section 'Your calendar' with the summary 'Your calendar · put your bookings in it' (details.me style, 54 px); inside, the field and the two buttons with the laptop note. (2) 'Let us see when you're busy' folds to 'When you're busy · not shared' or '· shared, read today' / '· couldn't be read' (opens on its own when there is a problem or a flash, as Disclosure says). (3) 'Your documents' folds to 'Your documents · 3, one runs out in 40 days' and opens on its own when one has run out or is running out, or after a send. (4) Kit to buy and Broken or missing kit become one folded 'Kit' line ('Kit · ask for kit, or report it broken') after Your details. (5) Order: glance, Today, Offers, Work done (freelancer-1), Your bookings, Days you can't work, Your calendar, When you're busy, Your documents, Your details, Kit, Declined and withdrawn. Nessa's page drops by about 1,400 px; Úna's to about one screen.
- **Trade-off:** A first-time freelancer may never open the calendar fold; the summary line names what it does in six words, and the office's first message can say it. Folds with no script are native details, so nothing is lost on an old phone. The documents' 'runs out' warning must still be seen: the fold's count carries it and opens on its own when one is running out. **Depends on:** Nothing; page.ts only. Pairs with freelancer-1's section order. **Weighed:** A second page /f/<token>/settings for the set-once things: cleaner but a second address to send and one tap further for the calendar, and the link is meant to be one page (ADR 0002). Rejected. Shorten the prose only: helps less; the feed note about Google on a phone is needed when they open it.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-freelancer/feed-and-busy-blocks-dark-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-freelancer/una-noffers-dark-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-freelancer/una-dark-phone-full.png`
- **Learning:** The fold-after-three rule should say it covers prose blocks too: a set-once block on a page ordered by what's due folds to its state line.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced to the pixel on a fresh template (390x844 dark, getBoundingClientRect): .feed 368 px / 78 words inside Your bookings; #calendar 420 px / 155 words (the reviewer's 79 was the paragraph alone); #documents 576 px; #buy 139; #broken 116; section.me folded to 54 px. The five set-once blocks are 1,619 px of Nessa's 7,980 px page, and on Úna Kenny's page (no offers, no bookings, 2,411 px, no glance line) the feed's two paragraphs are the first thing after 'No upcoming bookings.' The page already folds one section (Your details, page.ts:376-391) and nothing else. Not in the audit's 103: finding 32 and 53 folded lists; ADR 0104's fold after three is about rows; ADR 0108 asks only that the purchase section carry its count, which a fold's summary does; ADR 0044 and 0029 decide the blocks exist, not that they stay open. The design system's Disclosure ('shut by default; opens when it holds something; never opens to advertise') is the part, and the proposal names when each fold opens on its own (a document running out, a calendar that couldn't be read, after a send), which keeps the warnings seen. One thing to watch: the feed's 'Subscribe' is the one script-free way onto a phone's calendar and a fold hides it from a first-time freelancer; the summary wording 'Your calendar · put your bookings in it' carries it, and the office's first text can say it. Size S, page.ts only: honest. nn 2 (every visit, no harm, costs scrolling), P2.

#### freelancer-3 · The way out of an accepted offer has three names, and a mis-tapped Accept can only be undone by pulling out

**P3 · severity 2 · Adjusted by the sceptic** · roles F, S, O · phone 390×844 dark · reproduced

Accept is one tap and books them at once. The only way back, before or after the office confirms, is 'Can't make it any more?' → 'I can't make it', which the page then records as 'You've pulled out' on the tag and 'You pulled out' in the list, and the office sees as 'Can't make it': three names for one act on two sides of one phone call. Accept is only reversible while the call stays short: once it fills the call, every other offer on it ends as 'Filled by someone else' at once (ADR 0002), so a take-back there would reopen what others were told was gone.

- **Evidence:** /f/xvz5Q58jkuG_Y92jWkvRY2R6#o-v1-offer-00515 after one tap on Accept: Card: 'Thanks, you're down for it. The office will confirm.' · tag 'Accepted, the office will confirm' · the only controls left are 'Can't make it any more?' and, inside it, 'I can't make it' with 'Tell us as soon as you can, so we can find cover.' (`server/src/crew/page.ts:189-195 (holding → pullOut, no other way back), page.ts:134-143 (pullOut fold), links.ts:389 (answer 'pullOut' → offer.respond pullOut), server/src/crew/handlers.ts:567-580 (status 'pulled-out'; refusal 'You've pulled out of this one')`) [1 tap to accept; 0 undo; 2 taps to pull out (open fold, press). Buttons in the card after accept, by DOM: ['Can't make it any more?', 'I can't make it'].] · /f/xvz5Q58jkuG_Y92jWkvRY2R6 'Declined and withdrawn' and the office's Crew › Calls: Link: 'Clare Hotels Showcase (Build) · Stagehand · Tue 26 Jan 2027 · You pulled out on Fri 6 Nov'; tag text for the same status 'You've pulled out'; the button that did it 'I can't make it'. Office: STATUS['pulled-out'] = 'Can't make it'. (`server/src/crew/page.ts:118-127 (STATUS_TEXT 'pulled-out': "You've pulled out"), page.ts:398-400 (ended(): 'You pulled out'), page.ts:141 (button 'I can't make it'); web/src/crew/Calls.tsx:64 ("Can't make it")`) · /f/xvz5Q58jkuG_Y92jWkvRY2R6#o-v1-offer-00562, Decline: Decline is correctly folded: 'Can't do this one?' → 'Once declined, it's off your page: only the office can offer it to you again.' → 'Decline Dee Brands Fundraiser'. 2 taps, says what it does. The irreversible act is armed; the reversible one is not.
- **Basis:** Design-system principles.md rule 1 'Undo beats asking; what cannot be undone arms first' and behavioural.md's friction table (reversible: none, with Undo); NN/g heuristic 3 (user control and freedom: a clearly marked way out, undo) and 4 (consistency: one name for one thing); learnings.md 2026-10-10 (the fold is the arm without script). · Design-system principles.md rule 1 and behavioural.md 'Acting under pressure' (Fast and Slow Thinking, Friction); NN/g heuristics 3 and 4; ADR 0002 (answers run through the same commands, so an unaccept is one more answer)
- **Proposal (S):** Give the pull-out one name on both sides, and offer Take back only while the accept has not filled the call. Words first (S): tag "You've pulled out" → "Can't make it"; list 'You pulled out on Fri 6 Nov' → 'You said you can't make it on Fri 6 Nov'; refusal 'You've pulled out of this one' → 'You said you can't make this one'; the office's Calls.tsx keeps "Can't make it". Then, in the accepted card while the call is still short (closeIfFull changed nothing), the thank-you carries one action in the notice's place: 'Thanks, you're down for it. The office will confirm. · Not meant? Take it back', a form button posting answer=unaccept, a new branch of offer.respond that returns the offer to 'offered' with days and note kept and no pulled-out record; the office's Answers to check row simply goes. Once the call is full or the office confirms, the card is as now.
- **Trade-off:** Two behaviours for one button depending on a state the freelancer can't see; the words 'Not meant?' carry it. An accepted-then-taken-back offer still flickers on the office's Answers to check: bundle it so a take-back within the hour removes the notice rather than adding one. **Depends on:** A new 'unaccept' answer on offer.respond (shared/src/crew.ts:466-477, server/src/crew/handlers.ts) guarded by the call being short; history-words for it. **Weighed:** Arm Accept: protects the mis-tap but adds a tap to every offer's fast path; rejected by Friction. Undo in every case: would reopen 'filled' offers already ended for other people (ADR 0002); rejected.
- **Screenshots:** `screens/ux-sweep/lens-freelancer/after-accept-dark-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-freelancer/past-and-declined-dark-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/lens-freelancer/decline-fold-open-dark-phone.png`, `screens/ux-sweep/sceptic-freelancer/after-accept-dark-phone.png`
- **Learning:** A no-script page can still offer Undo: the notice's one action as a form button, while the thing is still reversible on the server.
- **Sceptic:** Reproduced: one tap on Accept on o-v1-offer-00515 gives 'Thanks, you're down for it. The office will confirm.', tag 'Accepted, the office will confirm', and the card's only controls are 'Can't make it any more?' and 'I can't make it'. The three names for one act are as cited (STATUS_TEXT "You've pulled out", ended() 'You pulled out', button 'I can't make it', office 'Can't make it' in Calls.tsx:64). Not in the audit's 103 (finding 10 added the pull-out fold). But the claim that Accept is 'reversible until the office confirms' is wrong in the case that matters: offer.respond accept calls closeIfFull (server/src/crew/handlers.ts:600-601, 117-122), which ends every other offer on the call as 'filled' the moment the accept makes the call full, and ADR 0002 ('first to accept gets the place') makes that final: those people see 'Filled by someone else', and with messages on the office is prompted to tell them. A 'Take it back' would then have to reopen offers already ended and risk a second message, which no part of the system does. So Undo is clean only when the accept did not fill the call, which the server knows at the time. The design-system rule (reversible happens at once with Undo; irreversible arms) therefore cuts both ways: where the accept fills the call it is the irreversible kind, and the mis-tap protection would be an arm, which Friction rejects on the fast path. Adjusted: keep the one-name fix (S) as the real pattern; offer Take back only while the call is still short after the accept; otherwise leave Accept as it is. Re-rated nn 2, P3: a mis-tap costs a pull-out record the office sees in Answers to check, and no evidence shows it happening. (was: "Accept is one tap with no way back, and the way out it offers is recorded under three different names")

#### freelancer-8 · Past work and Declined and withdrawn stop silently at eight rows while every other list on the page folds with a count

**P3 · severity 1 · Added by the sceptic** · roles F · phone 390x844 dark · reproduced

Offers fold after three with 'N more' lines, bookings fold to a line each, and sent timesheets fold under 'Sent and approved (14)'. Past work and Declined and withdrawn are instead cut to the last eight (slice(-8)) with no fold, no count and no word that more exists; the only way to the rest is the footer's 'Download everything we hold on you', a JSON file.
